# Browser
## Stop: choose the right surface before any browser action
Explicit browser intent wins: if the user names the in-app browser or Chrome, or asks to open, show, or navigate to a page; inspect its visual or interactive state; or interact with its UI, continue with Browser and do not substitute a connector.

Otherwise, treat a URL or open browser tab as context, not browser intent. Earlier Browser use does not make later semantic work browser-first. Before each semantic operation on a linked resource, you MUST query available and deferred tools for an applicable connector, API, or CLI. Reading this skill or scanning visible tools does not count. Do not initialize Browser for that operation until the query is complete. Use the non-browser tool when available. If it handles the current operation, continue the larger workflow without Browser for that operation. Use Browser when no such tool exists, the tool cannot access the resource or lacks a required capability, or UI work remains; use available browser context before asking the user to repeat it.

Use this skill for browser automation tasks such as inspecting pages, navigating, testing local apps, clicking, typing, taking screenshots, and reading visible page state.

If this plugin is listed as available in the session, treat that as mandatory reading before browser work. Open and follow this skill before saying that Browser is unavailable and before falling back to standalone Playwright or Computer Use.

Do not skip this skill just because Computer Use MCP tool calls are directly visible or appear easier to invoke. The presence of Computer Use tools is not evidence that Computer Use is the preferred browser surface.

## Setup Documentation
Use `await agent.documentation.get("<name>")` when one of these setup topics applies:
- `bootstrap-troubleshooting`: read when browser setup succeeds but discovery or selection fails
- `chrome-troubleshooting`: read when Chromium browser extension setup, installation, or communication fails

## Bootstrap
These setup details are internal. User-facing progress updates should be less technical in nature. Never mention `Node REPL`, `node_repl`, `REPL`, JavaScript sessions, module exports, reading documentation, or loading instructions unless a user is asking for that exact information. If setup or recovery is needed, describe it naturally as connecting to the browser or retrying the browser connection.

The `browser-client` module is the core entry point for browser use, and is available under `scripts/browser-client.mjs` in this plugin's root directory. ALWAYS import it using an absolute path. IMPORTANT: If this path cannot be found, stop and report that this plugin is missing `scripts/browser-client.mjs`. NEVER use the built in `browser-client` library.

Run browser setup code through the Node REPL `js` tool. In this environment the callable tool id typically appears as `mcp__node_repl__js`. If it is not already available, use tool discovery for `node_repl js` without setting a result limit. You need the `js` execution tool: `js_reset` only clears state, and `js_add_node_module_dir` only changes package resolution. Do not call either helper while trying to expose `js`. If `js` is still not available, search again for `node_repl js` with `limit: 10`.

CODE MODE REQUIREMENT: When you call the Node REPL `js` tool from the code-mode `exec` tool, the outer `exec` script containing the initial `documentation()` call MUST begin with `// @exec: {"max_output_tokens": 20000}`. This is a first-line pragma for the outer `exec` call, not an argument to the nested `js` tool. Keep the exact `nodeRepl.write(await <browser>.documentation());` call shown in the applicable selection scenario below and forward its complete result.

Initialize the runtime once per fresh Node session. If `agent.browsers` already exists, reuse it; do not import or initialize another browser runtime.

```js
if (globalThis.agent?.browsers == null) {
  const { setupBrowserRuntime } = await import("<plugin root>/scripts/browser-client.mjs");
  await setupBrowserRuntime({ globals: globalThis });
}
```

Once a browser connection is established, reuse its existing browser binding across later turns and do not reread this skill. Once you have read a browser's complete documentation, do not read it again unless you select a different browser.

A tab binding is separate from its browser binding. If a later turn reports that a tab is missing, stale, closed, or not part of the current browser session, discard that tab binding and obtain or create a fresh tab from the existing browser binding. An empty `browser.tabs.list()` or `browser.user.openTabs()` result is normal after tab cleanup and does not invalidate the browser binding. Never call `agent.browsers.get*` to recover a tab; only an explicit browser-disconnected error invalidates the binding.

## Browser selection
The scenarios below are for the initial browser selection only. Before calling any `agent.browsers.get*` method, reuse an existing `globalThis.browser`, `globalThis.iab`, `globalThis.chrome`, or `globalThis.edge` binding that already serves the task. A new user turn does not invalidate a browser binding or require another selection or documentation call.

Select the initial browser with exactly one of these scenarios, in the order
shown. An explicit request for the in-app browser, Chrome, or Edge always wins
over URL selection. Never call `getForUrl()` when the user names a browser.
An explicit browser request is a hard constraint: use only that browser and
never fall back to another browser surface. If its exact selector is
unavailable, report that browser as unavailable instead of calling
`getDefault()`, `getForUrl()`, or `get("extension")`.

App-provided in-app-browser context is ambient UI state, not a user instruction to select or switch browsers. Only the text of the user's request can explicitly choose a browser.

Do not inspect browser cookies, local storage, profiles, passwords, or session stores. Browser discovery must remain read-only.

When authentication blocks requested browser navigation, do not replace it with web search, a search engine, another site, or another source merely to bypass sign-in.

### The user explicitly requests a browser
A plugin mention in the user's request explicitly names its browser.
`[@Browser](plugin://browser@openai-bundled)` names the in-app browser.
Browser plugin mentions whose URL contains `browserFamily=chrome` or
`browserFamily=edge` name Chrome or Edge respectively.
`[@Chrome](plugin://chrome@openai-bundled)`,
`[@chrome-internal](plugin://chrome-internal@openai-bundled)`, and
`[@chrome-dev](plugin://chrome-dev@openai-bundled)` name Chrome. Follow the
corresponding explicit-browser scenario below.

The in-app browser is available only when the Browser skill is listed for the session. If the user explicitly requests the in-app browser and that skill is available, use a distinct persistent binding and immediately read its complete documentation:

```js
if (globalThis.iab == null) {
  globalThis.iab = await agent.browsers.get("iab");
  nodeRepl.write(await iab.documentation());
}
```

If the user explicitly requests the in-app browser but the Browser skill is not available, report that the in-app browser is unavailable instead of substituting another browser.

Chrome or Edge is available only when a Browser or Chrome skill is listed for
the session and `agent.browsers.get("chrome")` or
`agent.browsers.get("edge")` succeeds. The browser family is a stable selector;
do not list browsers first or pass an opaque browser ID for an explicit family.

For Chrome, use a separate persistent binding and immediately read its complete
documentation:

```js
if (globalThis.chrome == null) {
  globalThis.chrome = await agent.browsers.get("chrome");
  nodeRepl.write(await chrome.documentation());
}
```

For Edge, use its own persistent binding and immediately read its complete
documentation:

```js
if (globalThis.edge == null) {
  globalThis.edge = await agent.browsers.get("edge");
  nodeRepl.write(await edge.documentation());
}
```

If the user explicitly requests Chrome or Edge but that family is unavailable,
tell them that browser needs the ChatGPT browser extension and direct them to
**Settings → Computer use** to install it. Do not substitute another browser.

An explicit browser choice remains in force for the task. If authentication blocks the task in an explicitly selected browser, your next response must explicitly ask the user to sign in in that browser and tell you when it is ready, unless that browser's documentation provides a supported authentication flow to try first. Merely reporting that sign-in is required is not sufficient. Do not switch to another browser unless the user asks or approves the switch.

### The user explicitly requests an external browser without naming a family
When the user says to use their external browser, browser extension, or a
similar external-browser surface without naming Chrome or Edge, select the
first connected extension instance directly. Do not call
`agent.browsers.list()` first:

```js
if (globalThis.browser == null) {
  globalThis.browser = await agent.browsers.get("extension");
  nodeRepl.write(await browser.documentation());
}
```

If no extension instance is available, tell the user that their external
browser needs the ChatGPT browser extension and direct them to
**Settings → Computer use** to install it. Do not substitute the in-app
browser.

### The task requires browser interaction, the user does not specify a browser, and the task has a target URL
When the user supplies a URL or the intended URL can be reasonably inferred from the request, replace the example below with that URL and let browser-client choose the browser best suited to it. Do not call `agent.browsers.list()` first:

```js
if (globalThis.browser == null) {
  globalThis.browser = await agent.browsers.getForUrl("https://example.com/");
  nodeRepl.write(await browser.documentation());
}
```

### The user specifies neither a browser nor a target URL
Use the runtime default, which prefers the in-app browser when it is available and otherwise uses Chrome. Do not list browsers first:

```js
if (globalThis.browser == null) {
  globalThis.browser = await agent.browsers.getDefault();
  nodeRepl.write(await browser.documentation());
}
```

## After setup
If setup succeeds but browser discovery or selection fails, read `await agent.documentation.get("bootstrap-troubleshooting")` before resetting the JavaScript session or trying another browser-control mechanism.

If the failure is specific to Chrome extension setup, installation, or communication, read `await agent.documentation.get("chrome-troubleshooting")` before retrying or taking another recovery action.

When the user did not explicitly choose a browser, a browser selected by the runtime is not a user constraint. Do not switch browsers based only on an assumption about authentication. If navigation shows that the selected browser lacks the required authentication, select another available browser before asking the user to sign in. You may select it without resetting the Node session. Preserve existing `iab`, `chrome`, `edge`, and `browser` bindings when they are still useful. Existing tabs remain bound to the browser that created them. After selecting a different browser, obtain a tab from that browser before continuing and read its complete documentation.

The ability to interact directly with browsers is exposed through the `browser-client` runtime via the `agent.browsers.*` API. Before trying to interact with a selected browser for the first time, you MUST emit and read the complete documentation returned by its `documentation()` call in one go. For the initial documentation read, run the exact direct `nodeRepl.write(await <browser>.documentation());` call shown in the applicable scenario above. Do not assign the documentation to a variable, inspect its length, slice it, truncate it, summarize it, or emit only an excerpt. Do not proactively split the documentation into pages or chunks. Only if the tool output itself explicitly reports that it was truncated may you emit and read smaller chunks until you have read the documentation in its entirety.

Only the Node REPL `js` tool (`mcp__node_repl__js`) can be used to control the selected browser. Do not use external MCP browser-control tools, separate browser automation servers, or other browser skills for this surface. References to Playwright mean the in-skill `tab.playwright` API after browser-client setup.

   148	  function nearestInteraction() {
   149	    const h = game.hero;
   150	    if (game.world === 'town') {
   151	      const choices = [{ type: 'inn', x: 78, y: 129 }, { type: 'shop', x: 307, y: 129 }, { type: 'crystal', x: 195, y: 340 }, { type: 'gate', x: 367, y: 244 }, ...npcData.map(n => ({ type: 'npc', id: n.id, x: n.x, y: n.y }))];

   158	  }
   159	  function interactNearest() { const n = nearestInteraction(); setTarget(n.x, n.y, n); }
   160	  function handleWorldTap(x, y) {
   161	    const h = game.hero;
   162	    if (game.world === 'town') {
   163	      if (x > 340 && y > 150 && y < 330) return setTarget(360, 244, { type: 'gate', x: 367, y: 244 });
   164	      if (x < 136 && y < 190) return setTarget(78, 129, { type: 'inn', x: 78, y: 129 });
   165	      if (x > 245 && y < 190) return setTarget(307, 129, { type: 'shop', x: 307, y: 129 });
   166	      for (const n of npcData) if (dist(x, y, n.x, n.y) < 28) return setTarget(n.x, n.y, { type: 'npc', id: n.id, x: n.x, y: n.y });
   167	      if (dist(x, y, 195, 340) < 30) return setTarget(195, 340, { type: 'crystal', x: 195, y: 340 });
   168	    } else if (game.world === 'field') {
   169	      if (x > 334 && y > 135 && y < 330) return setTarget(366, 224, { type: 'gate', x: 366, y: 224 });
   170	      if (dist(x, y, 234, 284) < 32) return setTarget(234, 284, { type: 'chest', x: 234, y: 284 });
   171	      if (dist(x, y, 62, 122) < 28) return setTarget(62, 122, { type: 'crystal', x: 62, y: 122 });
   172	    } else {
   173	      if (dist(x, y, 54, 105) < 28) return setTarget(54, 105, { type: 'crystal', x: 54, y: 105 });
   174	      if (dist(x, y, 250, 300) < 30) return setTarget(250, 300, { type: 'chest', x: 250, y: 300 });
   175	      if (game.floor < 3 && x > 302 && x < 378 && y > 140 && y < 240) return setTarget(340, 190, { type: 'stair', x: 340, y: 190 });
   176	      if (game.floor === 3 && dist(x, y, 287, 154) < 42) return setTarget(287, 154, { type: 'boss', x: 287, y: 154 });
   177	    }
   178	    setTarget(x, y);
   179	  }
   180	  function interact(type) {
   181	    if (!type) return;
   182	    if (game.world === 'town') {
   183	      if (type.type === 'inn') { if (game.coins >= 8) { game.coins -= 8; game.party.forEach(p => p.hp = p.maxHp); announce('The Ember Inn restores every circuit. 8 crowns paid.'); addParticles(78, 129, '#ff9a6d', 16, 32); } else announce('The inn needs 8 crowns for a full mend.'); }
   184	      else if (type.type === 'shop') game.modal = 'shop';
   185	      else if (type.type === 'npc') { const n = npcData.find(v => v.id === type.id); if (n) game.dialog = { name: n.name, color: n.color, lines: n.lines, index: 0 }; }
   186	      else if (type.type === 'crystal') restAtCrystal();
   187	      else if (type.type === 'gate') { game.world = 'field'; game.hero.x = 35; game.hero.y = 235; game.hero.tx = 80; game.hero.ty = 235; announce('A service path opens into the glassbloom verge.'); }
   188	    } else if (game.world === 'field') {
   189	      if (type.type === 'crystal') restAtCrystal();
   190	      else if (type.type === 'chest') openChest('field');
   191	      else if (type.type === 'gate') { game.world = 'dungeon'; game.floor = 1; game.hero.x = 42; game.hero.y = 350; game.hero.tx = 42; game.hero.ty = 350; game.risk = 0; saveGame(); announce('The reactor shell descends three floors below.'); }
   192	    } else {
   193	      if (type.type === 'crystal') restAtCrystal();
   194	      else if (type.type === 'chest') openChest(`floor${game.floor}`);
   195	      else if (type.type === 'stair') { game.floor++; game.hero.x = 42; game.hero.y = 350; game.hero.tx = 42; game.hero.ty = 350; game.risk = 0; saveGame(); announce(`Floor ${game.floor}: coolant sings through the walls.`); }
   196	      else if (type.type === 'boss') startCombat('boss');
   197	    }
   198	  }
   199	  function restAtCrystal() { game.party.forEach(p => p.hp = p.maxHp); game.party.forEach(p => p.limit = Math.min(100, p.limit)); saveGame(); addParticles(game.hero.x, game.hero.y, '#72e7ff', 18, 40); announce('Save crystal lit. The party is restored.'); }
   200	  function openChest(key) {
   201	    if (game.openChests[key]) { announce('The chest is empty, but its hinge still glows.'); return; }
   202	    const found = key === 'field' ? 'frost' : key === 'floor1' ? 'mend' : key === 'floor2' ? 'jolt' : 'ember';
   203	    game.openChests[key] = true; if (!game.orbs.includes(found)) game.orbs.push(found); announce(`Glyph orb found: ${glyphs[found].name}. Tap ORBS to socket it.`); addParticles(game.hero.x, game.hero.y, glyphs[found].color, 24, 75); saveGame();
   204	  }
   205	
   206	  function advanceDialog() { if (!game.dialog) return; if (game.dialog.index < game.dialog.lines.length - 1) game.dialog.index++; else game.dialog = null; }
   207	
   208	  // ----- World and combat transitions -----
   209	  function onStep() {
   210	    game.stepCount++; game.stepsSinceBattle++;
   211	    if (game.world === 'town') return;
   212	    game.risk = clamp(game.risk + (game.world === 'field' ? 10 : 14), 0, 100);
   213	    const first = !game.firstEncounter && game.stepsSinceBattle >= 2;
   214	    if (first || (game.stepsSinceBattle >= 2 && (game.risk >= 100 || Math.random() < (game.world === 'field' ? .13 : .18)))) {
   215	      game.firstEncounter = true; startCombat(game.world === 'field' ? 'field' : 'dungeon');
   216	    }
   217	  }
   218	  function startCombat(kind) {
   219	    const source = kind === 'field' ? enemySets.field : enemySets.dungeon;
   220	    const raw = kind === 'boss' ? [{ name: 'The Cinder Warden', hp: 560, maxHp: 560, speed: 30, color: '#ff5d73', power: 23, boss: true }] : source[Math.floor(Math.random() * source.length)];
   221	    game.combat = { kind, enemies: raw.map(e => ({ ...e, hp: e.maxHp, atb: Math.random() * 25, guard: false })), activeHero: null, menu: 'root', cursor: 0, sub: null, log: kind === 'boss' ? 'A furnace-heart wakes beneath the city.' : 'Hostiles emerge from the blue dust.', phase: 'fight', timer: 0, totalDamage: 0, actions: 0 };
   222	    game.screen = 'combat'; game.interaction = null; game.stepPending = false; game.party.forEach(p => { p.atb = Math.min(p.atb, 50); p.guard = false; }); game.risk = Math.max(0, game.risk - 30); addParticles(195, 250, '#ff5d73', 28, 100); hurtShake(3);
   223	  }
   224	  function combatLog(text) { if (game.combat) game.combat.log = text; }
   225	  function livingParty() { return game.party.filter(p => p.hp > 0); }
   226	  function livingEnemies() { return game.combat.enemies.filter(e => e.hp > 0); }
   227	  function chooseReadyHero() {
   228	    if (game.combat.activeHero !== null) return;
   229	    const ready = game.party.findIndex(p => p.hp > 0 && p.atb >= 100);
   230	    if (ready >= 0) { game.combat.activeHero = ready; game.combat.menu = 'root'; game.combat.cursor = 0; }
   231	  }
   232	  function enemyAction(enemy) {
   233	    const targets = livingParty(); if (!targets.length) return;
   234	    const target = targets[Math.floor(Math.random() * targets.length)];
   235	    const dmg = Math.max(3, Math.round(enemy.power + Math.random() * 7 - target.level * 1.5));
   236	    const actual = target.guard ? Math.ceil(dmg * .45) : dmg;
   237	    target.hp = Math.max(0, target.hp - actual); target.limit = clamp(target.limit + actual * 1.45, 0, 100); game.combat.totalDamage += actual;
   238	    addParticles(78 + game.party.indexOf(target) * 38, 330, '#ff6376', 8, 42); hurtShake(4); combatLog(`${enemy.name} strikes ${target.name} for ${actual}.`);
   239	    if (target.hp <= 0) combatLog(`${target.name} falls, but the crystal remembers.`);
   240	  }
   241	  function finishHeroAction(hero) { hero.atb = 0; hero.guard = false; game.combat.activeHero = null; game.combat.menu = 'root'; game.combat.sub = null; game.combat.actions++; }
   242	  function targetEnemy(index) { const e = game.combat.enemies[index]; return e && e.hp > 0 ? e : livingEnemies()[0]; }
   243	  function damageEnemy(enemy, amount, color, label) {
   244	    if (!enemy) return;
   245	    const actual = Math.max(1, Math.round(amount + Math.random() * 5)); enemy.hp = Math.max(0, enemy.hp - actual); addParticles(enemy.x || 290, enemy.y || 200, color, 15, 80); hurtShake(3); game.flash = .08; combatLog(`${label} hits ${enemy.name} for ${actual}.`);
   246	    if (enemy.hp <= 0) { addParticles(enemy.x || 290, enemy.y || 200, '#fff3a6', 22, 110); combatLog(`${enemy.name} disperses into motes.`); }
   247	  }
   248	  function heroAttack(hero, targetIndex) { const e = targetEnemy(targetIndex); damageEnemy(e, 18 + hero.level * 4 + (hero.role === 'blade' ? 7 : 0), hero.color, `${hero.name}'s strike`); finishHeroAction(hero); }
   249	  function heroArt(hero, targetIndex) {
   250	    if (hero.role === 'blade') { damageEnemy(targetEnemy(targetIndex), 34 + hero.level * 5, '#ff9a6d', `${hero.name} uses Rift Cut`); }
   251	    else if (hero.role === 'scout') { damageEnemy(targetEnemy(targetIndex), 27 + hero.level * 4, '#70d9ff', `${hero.name} uses Pinpoint`); }
   252	    else { const target = livingParty().sort((a, b) => a.hp / a.maxHp - b.hp / b.maxHp)[0]; const heal = 30 + hero.level * 6; target.hp = Math.min(target.maxHp, target.hp + heal); addParticles(78 + game.party.indexOf(target) * 38, 330, '#b4ff9e', 16, 45); combatLog(`${hero.name}'s Soft Chord restores ${target.name} by ${heal}.`); }
   253	    finishHeroAction(hero);
   254	  }
   255	  function heroOrb(hero, orbId, targetIndex) {
   256	    const orb = glyphs[orbId]; if (!orb) return;
   257	    const orbLevel = Math.floor((game.orbUses[orbId] || 0) / 3) + 1;
   258	    if (orb.kind === 'heal') { const target = livingParty().sort((a, b) => a.hp / a.maxHp - b.hp / b.maxHp)[0]; const heal = orb.power + hero.level * 4 + (orbLevel - 1) * 5; target.hp = Math.min(target.maxHp, target.hp + heal); addParticles(78 + game.party.indexOf(target) * 38, 330, orb.color, 18, 48); combatLog(`${orb.spell} restores ${target.name} by ${heal}.`); }
   259	    else damageEnemy(targetEnemy(targetIndex), orb.power + hero.level * 4 + (orbLevel - 1) * 4, orb.color, `${hero.name} casts ${orb.spell}`);
   260	    game.orbUses[orbId] = (game.orbUses[orbId] || 0) + 1;
   261	    finishHeroAction(hero);
   262	  }
   263	  function useTonic(hero) {
   264	    if (game.tonics <= 0) { combatLog('The tonic satchel is empty.'); return; }
   265	    const target = livingParty().sort((a, b) => a.hp / a.maxHp - b.hp / b.maxHp)[0]; const heal = 32; game.tonics--; target.hp = Math.min(target.maxHp, target.hp + heal); addParticles(78 + game.party.indexOf(target) * 38, 330, '#ffe66d', 14, 40); combatLog(`${hero.name} gives ${target.name} a tonic (+${heal}).`); finishHeroAction(hero);
   266	  }
   267	  function useSurge(hero) {
   268	    if (hero.limit < 100) return;
   269	    hero.limit = 0; livingEnemies().forEach(e => damageEnemy(e, 58 + hero.level * 9, '#fff1a0', `${hero.name}'s SKYBREAK`)); addParticles(294, 202, '#fff1a0', 40, 140); game.flash = .2; combatLog(`${hero.name} unleashes SKYBREAK!`); finishHeroAction(hero);
   270	  }
   271	  function commandRoot(index) {
   272	    const c = game.combat, hero = game.party[c.activeHero]; if (!hero) return;
   273	    if (index === 0) c.menu = 'target', c.sub = 'attack';
   274	    if (index === 1) c.menu = 'arts';
   275	    if (index === 2) c.menu = 'orbs';
   276	    if (index === 3) c.menu = 'item';
   277	    if (index === 4) { hero.guard = true; combatLog(`${hero.name} braces behind a prism guard.`); finishHeroAction(hero); }
   278	  }
   279	  const rootLabels = ['ATTACK', 'ARTS', 'ORBS', 'ITEM', 'GUARD'];
   280	  function handleCombatTap(x, y) {
   281	    const c = game.combat; if (!c) return;
   282	    if (c.phase === 'victory') { if (c.kind === 'boss') showWin(); else leaveCombat(); return; }
   283	    if (c.phase === 'fail') { resetFromSave(); return; }
   284	    if (c.activeHero === null) return;
   285	    const hero = game.party[c.activeHero];
   286	    if (hero.limit >= 100 && x > 278 && y > 430 && y < 525) return useSurge(hero);
   287	    if (c.menu === 'root') {
   288	      if (y >= 536 && y < 621) { const col = Math.floor(x / 77.5); if (col >= 0 && col < 5) commandRoot(col); }
   289	    } else if (c.menu === 'arts') {
   290	      if (y > 532 && y < 592 && x < 190) heroArt(hero, 0); else if (y > 532 && y < 592) c.menu = 'root'; else if (y > 604 && y < 664) c.menu = 'root';
   291	    } else if (c.menu === 'orbs') {
   292	      const orb = game.equipped[hero.id];
   293	      if (!orb) { combatLog('No glyph orb is socketed to this weapon.'); c.menu = 'root'; }
   294	      else if (y > 532 && y < 596 && x < 190) { c.menu = glyphs[orb].kind === 'heal' ? 'item' : 'target'; c.sub = 'orb'; }
   295	      else if (y > 532 && y < 596) { game.modal = 'orbs'; }
   296	      else if (y > 604 && y < 664) { c.menu = 'root'; }
   297	    } else if (c.menu === 'item') {
   298	      if (y > 532 && y < 592 && x < 190) useTonic(hero); else if (y > 532 && y < 592) c.menu = 'root'; else if (y > 604 && y < 664) c.menu = 'root';
   299	    } else if (c.menu === 'target') {
   300	      const idx = enemyIndexAt(x, y); if (idx >= 0) { if (c.sub === 'attack') heroAttack(hero, idx); else heroOrb(hero, game.equipped[hero.id], idx); }
   301	      else if (y > 604 && y < 664) c.menu = 'root';
   302	    }
   303	  }
   304	  function enemyIndexAt(x, y) { const n = game.combat.enemies.length; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const ex = 272 + i * 52, ey = n === 1 ? 212 : 170 + i * 72; if (dist(x, y, ex, ey) < 34 && game.combat.enemies[i].hp > 0) return i; } return -1; }
   305	  function combatKey(key) {
   306	    const c = game.combat; if (!c || c.activeHero === null) return;
   307	    if (key === 'x' || key === 'Escape') { c.menu = 'root'; return; }
   308	    const max = c.menu === 'root' ? 4 : 1;
   309	    if (key === 'ArrowLeft' || key === 'a' || key === 'A') c.cursor = clamp(c.cursor - 1, 0, max);
   310	    if (key === 'ArrowRight' || key === 'd' || key === 'D') c.cursor = clamp(c.cursor + 1, 0, max);
   311	    if (key === 'ArrowUp' || key === 'w' || key === 'W') c.cursor = clamp(c.cursor - 1, 0, max);
   312	    if (key === 'ArrowDown' || key === 's' || key === 'S') c.cursor = clamp(c.cursor + 1, 0, max);
   313	    if (key === 'Enter' || key === ' ') {
   314	      if (c.menu === 'root') commandRoot(c.cursor); else if (c.menu === 'arts') heroArt(game.party[c.activeHero], 0); else if (c.menu === 'orbs') heroOrb(game.party[c.activeHero], game.equipped[game.party[c.activeHero].id], 0); else if (c.menu === 'item') useTonic(game.party[c.activeHero]); else if (c.menu === 'target') game.party[c.activeHero].role === 'blade' ? heroAttack(game.party[c.activeHero], 0) : heroOrb(game.party[c.activeHero], game.equipped[game.party[c.activeHero].id], 0);
   315	    }
   316	  }
   317	  function leaveCombat() {
   318	    game.screen = 'world'; game.combat = null; game.stepsSinceBattle = 0; game.risk = Math.max(0, game.risk - 25); game.stepPending = false; game.hero.tx = game.hero.x; game.hero.ty = game.hero.y; announce('The path clears. Keep moving toward the reactor.');
   319	  }
   320	  function resetFromSave() {
   321	    const s = game.savePoint || saveData();
   322	    game.world = s.world || 'town'; game.floor = s.floor || 0; game.hero.x = s.hero?.x ?? 195; game.hero.y = s.hero?.y ?? 268; game.hero.tx = game.hero.x; game.hero.ty = game.hero.y;
   323	    game.party = partyBlueprint.map((base, i) => ({ ...base, ...(s.party?.[i] || {}), atb: 0, guard: false })); game.coins = s.coins ?? 26; game.tonics = s.tonics ?? 3; game.orbs = s.orbs || ['ember']; game.orbUses = s.orbUses || { ember: 0, frost: 0, jolt: 0, mend: 0 }; game.equipped = s.equipped || { tavi: 'ember', nema: null, iri: null }; game.openChests = s.openChests || {};
   324	    game.screen = 'world'; game.combat = null; game.stepsSinceBattle = 0; game.risk = 0; game.stepPending = false; announce('The crystal catches the party. Try a different rhythm.');
   325	  }
   326	  function showWin() {
   327	    const score = Math.max(100, Math.round(6200 - game.elapsed * 3 + game.party.reduce((n, p) => n + p.level * 120 + p.xp, 0) - game.combat.totalDamage));
   328	    game.best = Math.max(game.best, score); try { localStorage.setItem(BEST_KEY, String(game.best)); } catch (_) {}
   329	    game.victoryStats = { score, time: game.elapsed, actions: game.combat.actions }; game.screen = 'win'; game.combat = null;
   330	  }
   331	
   332	  // ----- Orb and shop menus -----
   333	  function handleModalTap(x, y) {
   334	    if (game.modal === 'shop') {
   335	      if (y > 240 && y < 320) { if (game.coins >= 6) { game.coins -= 6; game.tonics++; announce('Tonic added to the satchel.'); } else announce('The vendor needs 6 crowns.'); }
   336	      else if (y > 590) game.modal = null;
   337	      return;
   338	    }
   339	    if (game.modal === 'orbs') {
   340	      if (x > 340 && y < 100) { game.modal = null; return; }
   341	      if (y > 164 && y < 320) { game.selectedHero = clamp(Math.floor((y - 164) / 52), 0, 2); return; }
   342	      if (y > 355 && y < 620) { const i = clamp(Math.floor((y - 355) / 58), 0, game.orbs.length - 1); const orb = game.orbs[i]; if (orb) { const hero = game.party[game.selectedHero]; const other = game.party.find(p => game.equipped[p.id] === orb); const old = game.equipped[hero.id]; if (other && other.id !== hero.id) game.equipped[other.id] = old || null; game.equipped[hero.id] = orb; announce(`${glyphs[orb].name} socketed to ${hero.name}.`); saveGame(); } }
   343	    }
   344	  }
   345	  function restOrShopLabel() { return game.world === 'town' ? 'ORBS' : 'ORBS'; }
   346	
   347	  // ----- Drawing -----
   348	  function resizeTransform() { ctx.setTransform(canvas.width / W, 0, 0, canvas.height / H, 0, 0); }
   349	  function roundedRect(x, y, w, h, r, fill, stroke, line = 1) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect?.(x, y, w, h, r); if (!ctx.roundRect) { ctx.rect(x, y, w, h); } if (fill) { ctx.fillStyle = fill; ctx.fill(); } if (stroke) { ctx.strokeStyle = stroke; ctx.lineWidth = line; ctx.stroke(); } }
   350	  function text(str, x, y, size = 12, color = '#d9fbff', align = 'left', weight = '400') { ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.font = `${weight} ${size}px ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Consolas, monospace`; ctx.textAlign = align; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle'; ctx.fillText(str, x, y); }
   351	  function line(x1, y1, x2, y2, color, width = 1) { ctx.strokeStyle = color; ctx.lineWidth = width; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x1, y1); ctx.lineTo(x2, y2); ctx.stroke(); }
   352	  function panel(x, y, w, h, fill = 'rgba(6,15,24,.92)', stroke = '#245366') { roundedRect(x, y, w, h, 8, fill, stroke, 1); }
   353	  function drawBackground() {
   354	    const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H); g.addColorStop(0, '#071824'); g.addColorStop(1, '#05090f'); ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   355	    ctx.globalAlpha = .55; for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) { const x = (i * 79 + 17) % W, y = (i * 43 + 23) % 470; ctx.fillStyle = i % 3 ? '#58c9e5' : '#ff7995'; ctx.fillRect(x, y, 1.5, 1.5); } ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   356	    line(0, 486, W, 486, '#1b4051', 2);
   357	  }
   358	  function drawSkyline() {
   359	    ctx.fillStyle = '#09131e'; ctx.fillRect(0, 82, W, 340);
   360	    const buildings = [[0,140,58,280],[65,94,47,326],[120,160,70,260],[196,112,42,308],[246,72,58,348],[311,132,79,288]];
   361	    buildings.forEach((b, bi) => { ctx.fillStyle = bi % 2 ? '#0d2430' : '#0b1c29'; ctx.fillRect(...b); for (let wy = b[1] + 16; wy < 400; wy += 24) for (let wx = b[0] + 10; wx < b[0] + b[2] - 4; wx += 18) { ctx.fillStyle = ((wx + wy + bi) % 3) ? '#195161' : '#e1c86e'; ctx.globalAlpha = ((wx + wy) % 5 === 0) ? .9 : .35; ctx.fillRect(wx, wy, 4, 6); } }); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   362	    ctx.fillStyle = '#0a121d'; ctx.fillRect(0, 395, W, 72); line(0, 410, W, 410, '#234b5e', 2); line(0, 463, W, 463, '#1b4051', 2);
   363	    // skyrail
   364	    line(0, 72, W, 72, '#4ca5b7', 2); line(0, 78, W, 78, '#173e4c', 2); for (let x = 20; x < W; x += 58) { line(x, 72, x - 8, 130, '#1c5260', 2); ctx.fillStyle = '#ff7191'; ctx.fillRect(x + 8, 66, 9, 3); }
   365	  }
   366	  function drawTown() {
   367	    drawSkyline();
   368	    ctx.fillStyle = '#111c2a'; ctx.fillRect(26, 102, 109, 112); ctx.fillStyle = '#173348'; ctx.fillRect(36, 116, 90, 82); ctx.fillStyle = '#ff7f76'; ctx.fillRect(46, 91, 70, 24); text('EMBER INN', 81, 103, 10, '#fff1a0', 'center', '700'); ctx.fillStyle = '#08131d'; ctx.fillRect(75, 164, 24, 34); ctx.fillStyle = '#c7f3ff'; ctx.fillRect(47, 137, 17, 18); ctx.fillRect(108, 137, 17, 18);
   369	    ctx.fillStyle = '#111c2a'; ctx.fillRect(253, 102, 111, 112); ctx.fillStyle = '#173348'; ctx.fillRect(263, 116, 91, 82); ctx.fillStyle = '#73e6ef'; ctx.fillRect(269, 91, 78, 24); text('LUMEN MART', 308, 103, 10, '#071018', 'center', '700'); ctx.fillStyle = '#08131d'; ctx.fillRect(300, 163, 25, 35); ctx.fillStyle = '#c7f3ff'; ctx.fillRect(273, 137, 18, 18); ctx.fillRect(330, 137, 18, 18);
   370	    ctx.fillStyle = '#0e2630'; ctx.fillRect(24, 214, 340, 200); ctx.fillStyle = '#142d39'; ctx.fillRect(45, 231, 300, 145); ctx.fillStyle = '#173f4a'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(195, 310, 72, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = '#1d5360'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(195, 310, 56, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   371	    // gate

   395	  function drawCrystal(x, y) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.shadowBlur = 16; ctx.shadowColor = '#72e7ff'; ctx.fillStyle = '#72e7ff'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, -18); ctx.lineTo(11, -5); ctx.lineTo(7, 14); ctx.lineTo(-7, 14); ctx.lineTo(-11, -5); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = '#e5feff'; ctx.globalAlpha = .75; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-2, -11); ctx.lineTo(3, -5); ctx.lineTo(0, 8); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); }
   396	  function drawChest(x, y, opened) { ctx.fillStyle = opened ? '#293642' : '#b9704f'; ctx.fillRect(x - 16, y - 11, 32, 22); ctx.fillStyle = opened ?    426	  function drawEnemy(x, y, color, boss, scale) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.scale(scale, scale); ctx.shadowBlur = boss ? 24 : 12; ctx.shadowColor = color; ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, -33); ctx.lineTo(23, -5); ctx.lineTo(17, 30); ctx.lineTo(-18, 30); ctx.lineTo(-24, -5); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; ctx.fillStyle = '#171328'; ctx.fillRect(-13, -7, 8, 5); ctx.fillRect(7, -7, 8, 5); ctx.fillStyle = '#fff1a0'; ctx.fillRect(-11, -6, 4, 3); ctx.fillRect(7, -6, 4, 3); if (boss) { ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffdd84'; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, -12, 34, Math.PI * 1.1, Math.PI * 1.9); ctx.stroke(); } ctx.restore(); }

'CHOOSE A TARGET' : 'CHOOSE A TARGET', 195, 568, 12, '#d9fbff', 'center', '700'); button(15, 604, 360, 45, 'BACK', '#8aaab2'); }
   429	  }

   434	  function drawOrbModal() { panel(16, 70, 358, 570, 'rgba(7,18,28,.98)', '#70d9ff'); text('GLYPH LOADOUT', 34, 103, 17, '#70d9ff', 'left', '700'); text('X', 354, 103, 15, '#ff9a6d', 'center', '700'); text('Select a hero, then tap an orb to socket it.', 34, 130, 10, '#9bb9bf'); text('HEROES', 34, 153, 9, '#78a8b2', 'left', '700'); game.party.forEach((p, i) => { const y = 169 + i * 52; const selected = i === game.selectedHero; panel(30, y, 330, 43, selected ? 'tap to continue' : 'tap to close', 354, 443, 9, '#8aaab2', 'right'); }
   436	  function drawFail() { ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(3,7,12,.86)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); text('SIGNAL LOST', 195, 238, 29, '#ff7185', 'center', '700'); text('The last crystal catches your names.', 195, 282, 11, '#d9fbff', 'center'); button(74, 342, 242, 58, 'RETURN TO CRYSTAL', '#70d9ff', true); text('tap / Enter to restart', 195, 426, 10, '#8aaab2', 'center'); }

   438	
   439	  function update(dt) {
   440	    game.elapsed += game.screen === 'win' ? 0 : dt;
   441	    if (game.messageTime > 0) game.messageTime -= dt;
   442	    game.shake = Math.max(0, game.shake - dt * 12); game.flash = Math.max(0, game.flash - dt); game.particles.forEach(p => { p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt; p.vy += 32 * dt; p.life -= dt; }); game.particles = game.particles.filter(p => p.life > 0);
   443	    if (game.screen === 'world' && !game.dialog && !game.modal) { const dx = game.hero.tx - game.hero.x, dy = game.hero.ty - game.hero.y, d = Math.hypot(dx, dy); if (d > 2) { const step = Math.min(d, 92 * dt); game.hero.x += dx / d * step; game.hero.y += dy / d * step; } else if (game.stepPending) { game.stepPending = false; onStep(); if (game.screen === 'world' && game.interaction) { const interaction = game.interaction; game.interaction = null; interact(interaction); } } }
   444	    if (game.screen === 'combat') updateCombat(dt);
   445	  }
   446	  function updateCombat(dt) {
   447	    const c = game.combat; if (!c) return;
   448	    if (c.phase === 'fight') {
   449	      game.party.forEach(p => { if (p.hp > 0 && p.atb < 100) p.atb = Math.min(100, p.atb + p.speed * dt * .7); });
   450	      c.enemies.forEach(e => { if (e.hp > 0) { e.atb += e.speed * dt * .58; if (e.atb >= 100) { e.atb = 0; enemyAction(e); } } });
   451	      chooseReadyHero();
   452	      if (!livingEnemies().length) { c.phase = 'victory'; c.timer = 0; const xp = c.kind === 'boss' ? 160 : 36; game.party.forEach(p => { if (p.hp > 0) addXp(p, xp); }); game.coins += c.kind === 'boss' ? 120 : 9; saveGame(); }
   453	      else if (!livingParty().length) { c.phase = 'fail'; c.timer = 0; }
   454	    } else if (c.phase === 'victory') { c.timer += dt; if (c.kind !== 'boss' && c.timer > 2.2) leaveCombat(); }
   455	  }
   456	  function render() {

    67	    return () => {
    68	      let t = seed += 0x6D2B79F5;
    69	      t = Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 15, t | 1);
    70	      t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, t | 61);
    71	      return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    72	    };
    73	  }
    74	
    75	  function rand(a = 0, b = 1) { return a + (b - a) * waveRng(); }
    76	  function clamp(v, a, b) { return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, v)); }
    77	  function lerp(a, b, t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }
    78	  function dist2(a, b) { const x = a.x - b.x, y = a.y - b.y; return x * x + y * y; }
    79	  function angleDiff(a, b) { return Math.atan2(Math.sin(b - a), Math.cos(b - a)); }
    80	  function wrap(o, margin = 0) {
    81	    if (o.x < -margin) o.x = W + margin;
    82	    else if (o.x > W + margin) o.x = -margin;
    83	    if (o.y < -margin) o.y = H + margin;
    84	    else if (o.y > H + margin) o.y = -margin;
    85	  }
    86	
    87	  function resize() {
    88	    W = Math.max(280, window.innerWidth || 390);
    89	    H = Math.max(480, window.innerHeight || 700);
    90	    const longAxis = Math.max(W, H);
    91	    dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, DPR_CAP, 960 / longAxis);
    92	    canvas.width = Math.floor(W * dpr);
    93	    canvas.height = Math.floor(H * dpr);
    94	    canvas.style.width = W + 'px';
    95	    canvas.style.height = H + 'px';
    96	    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    97	    if (stars.length) makeStars();
    98	  }
    99	
   100	  function makeStars() {
   101	    const starRng = mulberry32(runSeed ^ 0x9e3779b9);
   102	    stars = [];
   103	    const count = Math.round(clamp(W * H / 6200, 46, 105));
   104	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   105	      stars.push({ x: starRng() * W, y: starRng() * H, r: starRng() * 1.5 + 0.25, a: starRng() * 0.46 + 0.16, p: starRng() * TAU });
   106	    }
   107	  }
   108	
   109	  function resetRun() {
   110	    runSeed = ((Date.now() ^ Math.floor(Math.random() * 0x7fffffff)) >>> 0) || 1;
   111	    rng = mulberry32(runSeed);
   112	    makeStars();
   113	    wave = 1; score = 0; ore = 0; state = 'play';
   114	    flash = 0; shake = 0; message = ''; messageTimer = 0; waveClearTimer = 0;
   115	    dashCooldown = 0; firePointer = -1; stickPointer = -1; stick.active = false; stick.x = 0; stick.y = 0;
   116	    ship = { x: W / 2, y: H * 0.53, vx: 0, vy: 0, r: 13, angle: -Math.PI / 2,
   117	      shields: 3, invuln: 0, fireTimer: 0, fireRate: 4.5, thrust: 245, damage: 1, dash: 0 };
   118	    particles = []; bullets = []; asteroids = []; mines = []; drones = []; crystals = [];
   119	    beginWave();
   120	  }
   121	
   122	  function beginWave() {
   123	    waveRng = mulberry32((runSeed + Math.imul(wave, 0x45d9f3b)) >>> 0);
   124	    asteroids = []; bullets = []; mines = []; drones = []; crystals = [];
   125	    waveBanner = 2.2;
   126	    const count = Math.min(3 + wave, 9);
   127	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) spawnAsteroid(3, true);
   128	    const crystalCount = Math.min(1 + Math.floor((wave + 1) / 2), 4);
   129	    for (let i = 0; i < crystalCount; i++) spawnCrystal();
   130	    if (wave >= 2) {
   131	      const mineCount = Math.min(1 + Math.floor(wave / 2), 4);
   132	      for (let i = 0; i < mineCount; i++) spawnMine();
   133	    }
   134	    if (wave >= 3) spawnDrone();
   135	    message = wave === 1 ? 'HULLBREAKER' : 'WAVE ' + wave;
   136	    messageTimer = 1.7;
   137	  }
   138	
   139	  function safeSpawnPoint(min = 145) {
   140	    let x, y, tries = 0;
   141	    do {
   142	      x = rand(25, W - 25); y = rand(80, H * 0.56); tries++;
   143	    } while (tries < 30 && ((x - ship.x) * (x - ship.x) + (y - ship.y) * (y - ship.y) < min * min));
   144	    return { x, y };
   145	  }
   146	
   147	  function spawnAsteroid(size, away) {
   148	    const p = safeSpawnPoint(away ? 175 : 40);
   149	    const radii = [16, 27, 43];
   150	    const r = radii[size - 1];
   151	    const angle = rand(0, TAU);
   152	    const speed = rand(18, 50) + wave * 2;
   153	    const chunks = [];
   154	    for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) chunks.push(rand(0.76, 1.22));
   155	    asteroids.push({ x: p.x, y: p.y, vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed, vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
   156	      r, size, rot: rand(0, TAU), spin: rand(-0.9, 0.9), chunks, hit: 0 });
   157	  }
   158	
   159	  function splitAsteroid(a) {
   160	    if (a.size > 1) {
   161	      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
   162	        const ang = Math.atan2(a.vy, a.vx) + (i ? 1 : -1) * rand(0.5, 1.3);
   163	        const speed = Math.max(34, Math.hypot(a.vx, a.vy) + rand(24, 56));
   164	        const r = a.size === 3 ? 27 : 16;
   165	        const chunks = [];
   166	        for (let j = 0; j < 9; j++) chunks.push(rand(0.76, 1.22));
   167	        asteroids.push({ x: a.x + Math.cos(ang) * 7, y: a.y + Math.sin(ang) * 7,
   168	          vx: Math.cos(ang) * speed, vy: Math.sin(ang) * speed, r, size: a.size - 1,
   169	          rot: rand(0, TAU), spin: rand(-1.5, 1.5), chunks, hit: 0 });
   170	      }
   171	    }
   172	  }
   173	
   174	  function spawnMine() {
   175	    const p = safeSpawnPoint(170);
   176	    const ang = rand(0, TAU), speed = rand(12, 31);
   177	    mines.push({ x: p.x, y: p.y, vx: Math.cos(ang) * speed, vy: Math.sin(ang) * speed,
   178	      r: 13, phase: rand(0, TAU), armed: rand(0, 1) > 0.2 });
   179	  }
   180	
   181	  function spawnDrone() {
   182	    const p = safeSpawnPoint(210);
   183	    drones.push({ x: p.x, y: p.y, vx: 0, vy: 0, r: 17, hp: 3, phase: rand(0, TAU), hit: 0 });
   184	  }
   185	
   186	  function spawnCrystal() {
   187	    const p = safeSpawnPoint(115);
   188	    const a = rand(0, TAU), speed = rand(8, 22);
   189	    crystals.push({ x: p.x, y: p.y, vx: Math.cos(a) * speed, vy: Math.sin(a) * speed,
   190	      r: 10, spin: rand(0, TAU), phase: rand(0, TAU) });
   191	  }
   192	
   193	  function bindInput() {
   194	    window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
   195	    window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
   196	      const k = e.key.toLowerCase();
   197	      if (['arrowup', 'arrowdown', 'arrowleft', 'arrowright', ' ', 'enter', 'w', 'a', 's', 'd', 'shift'].includes(k)) e.preventDefault();
   198	      if ((state === 'gameover' || state === 'win') && (k === 'enter' || k === ' ')) { resetRun(); return; }
   199	      if (state === 'upgrade' && (k === '1' || k === '2' || k === 'enter' || k === ' ')) { chooseUpgrade(k === '2' ? 1 : 0); return; }
   200	      if ((k === 'enter' || k === 'shift') && !keys[k]) boost();
   201	      keys[k] = true;
   202	    });
   203	    window.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => { keys[e.key.toLowerCase()] = false; });
   204	    window.addEventListener('blur', () => { for (const k in keys) keys[k] = false; firePointer = -1; stickPointer = -1; stick.active = false; });
   205	    canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {
   206	      e.preventDefault();
   207	      const p = pointFromEvent(e);
   208	      if (state === 'gameover' || state === 'win') { resetRun(); return; }
   209	      if (state === 'upgrade') { chooseUpgradeAt(p.x, p.y); return; }
   210	      if (isStickZone(p.x, p.y)) {
   211	        stickPointer = e.pointerId; stick.active = true; updateStick(p.x, p.y); canvas.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId); return;
   212	      }
   213	      if (isDashZone(p.x, p.y)) {
   214	        const now = performance.now();
   215	        if (now - lastDashTap < 330) boost();
   216	        lastDashTap = now;
   217	        return;
   218	      }
   219	      if (isFireZone(p.x, p.y)) { firePointer = e.pointerId; canvas.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId); }
   220	    }, { passive: false });
   221	    canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
   222	      if (e.pointerId === stickPointer) { e.preventDefault(); const p = pointFromEvent(e); updateStick(p.x, p.y); }
   223	    }, { passive: false });
   224	    const release = (e) => {
   225	      if (e.pointerId === stickPointer) { stickPointer = -1; stick.active = false; stick.x = 0; stick.y = 0; }
   226	      if (e.pointerId === firePointer) firePointer = -1;
   227	    };
   228	    canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', release);
   229	    canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', release);
   230	    canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', (e) => e.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   231	  }
   232	
   233	  function pointFromEvent(e) {
   234	    const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
   235	    return { x: (e.clientX - r.left) * W / r.width, y: (e.clientY - r.top) * H / r.height };
   236	  }
   237	
   238	  function isStickZone(x, y) { return y > H - 225 && x < W * 0.53; }
   239	  function isFireZone(x, y) { return y > H - 220 && x > W * 0.52 && !isDashZone(x, y); }
   240	  function isDashZone(x, y) { return x > W - 102 && y > H - 286 && y < H - 178; }
   241	
   242	  function updateStick(x, y) {
   243	    const cx = W * 0.19, cy = H - 112, radius = 64;
   244	    let dx = x - cx, dy = y - cy;
   245	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
   246	    if (d > radius) { dx *= radius / d; dy *= radius / d; }
   247	    stick.x = dx / radius; stick.y = dy / radius;
   248	  }
   249	
   250	  function boost() {
   251	    if (state !== 'play' || dashCooldown > 0) return;
   252	    const input = getControlVector();
   253	    let dx = input.x, dy = input.y;
   254	    if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) < 0.1) { dx = Math.cos(ship.angle); dy = Math.sin(ship.angle); }
   255	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
   256	    ship.vx += dx / d * 315; ship.vy += dy / d * 315; ship.dash = 0.25; dashCooldown = 1.25;
   257	    shake = Math.max(shake, 7); burst(ship.x, ship.y, '#ffcf66', 16, 110);
   258	  }
   259	
   260	  function getControlVector() {
   261	    if (stick.active || Math.hypot(stick.x, stick.y) > 0.02) return { x: stick.x, y: stick.y };
   262	    let x = 0, y = 0;
   263	    if (keys.arrowleft || keys.a) x -= 1;
   264	    if (keys.arrowright || keys.d) x += 1;
   265	    if (keys.arrowup || keys.w) y -= 1;
   266	    if (keys.arrowdown || keys.s) y += 1;
   267	    return { x, y };
   268	  }
   269	
   270	  function frame(now) {
   271	    const dt = Math.min(MAX_DT, Math.max(0.001, (now - last) / 1000));
   272	    last = now;
   273	    update(dt);
   274	    draw(now / 1000);
   275	    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   276	  }
   277	
   278	  function update(dt) {
   279	    flash = Math.max(0, flash - dt * 2.8);
   280	    shake = Math.max(0, shake - dt * 18);
   281	    messageTimer = Math.max(0, messageTimer - dt);
   282	    waveBanner = Math.max(0, waveBanner - dt);
   283	    dashCooldown = Math.max(0, dashCooldown - dt);
   284	    updateParticles(dt);
   285	    if (state !== 'play') return;
   286	    waveClearTimer = Math.max(0, waveClearTimer - dt);
   287	    updateShip(dt);
   288	    updateBullets(dt);
   289	    updateAsteroids(dt);
   290	    updateMines(dt);
   291	    updateDrones(dt);
   292	    updateCrystals(dt);
   293	    collisions();
   294	    if (asteroids.length === 0 && waveClearTimer <= 0) {
   295	      score += wave * 120;
   296	      saveBest();
   297	      if (wave >= WIN_WAVE) { state = 'win'; burst(ship.x, ship.y, '#70f6b4', 48, 220); return; }
   298	      state = 'upgrade';
   299	      offers = makeOffers();
   300	      burst(ship.x, ship.y, '#55e6ff', 26, 150);
   301	    }
   302	  }
   303	
   304	  function updateShip(dt) {
   305	    ship.invuln = Math.max(0, ship.invuln - dt);
   306	    ship.dash = Math.max(0, ship.dash - dt);
   307	    const control = getControlVector();
   308	    const mag = clamp(Math.hypot(control.x, control.y), 0, 1);
   309	    const keyboardRotate = (keys.arrowleft || keys.a ? -1 : 0) + (keys.arrowright || keys.d ? 1 : 0);
   310	    if (stick.active || Math.hypot(stick.x, stick.y) > 0.02) {
   311	      if (mag > 0.08) {
   312	        const target = Math.atan2(control.y, control.x);
   313	        ship.angle += clamp(angleDiff(ship.angle, target), -7 * dt, 7 * dt);
   314	      }
   315	      if (mag > 0.04) {
   316	        ship.vx += Math.cos(ship.angle) * ship.thrust * mag * dt;
   317	        ship.vy += Math.sin(ship.angle) * ship.thrust * mag * dt;
   318	      }
   319	    } else {
   320	      if (keyboardRotate) ship.angle += keyboardRotate * 3.8 * dt;
   321	      if (keys.arrowup || keys.w) {
   322	        ship.vx += Math.cos(ship.angle) * ship.thrust * dt;
   323	        ship.vy += Math.sin(ship.angle) * ship.thrust * dt;
   324	      }
   325	      if (keys.arrowdown || keys.s) {
   326	        ship.vx -= Math.cos(ship.angle) * ship.thrust * 0.55 * dt;
   327	        ship.vy -= Math.sin(ship.angle) * ship.thrust * 0.55 * dt;
   328	      }
   329	    }
   330	    const drag = Math.pow(0.992, dt * 60);
   331	    ship.vx *= drag; ship.vy *= drag;
   332	    const max = ship.dash > 0 ? 610 : 300;
   333	    const speed = Math.hypot(ship.vx, ship.vy);
   334	    if (speed > max) { ship.vx = ship.vx / speed * max; ship.vy = ship.vy / speed * max; }
   335	    ship.x += ship.vx * dt; ship.y += ship.vy * dt; wrap(ship, 18);
   336	    ship.fireTimer -= dt;
   337	    if ((firePointer !== -1 || keys[' '] || keys.enter) && ship.fireTimer <= 0) fire();
   338	  }
   339	
   340	  function fire() {
   341	    ship.fireTimer = 1 / ship.fireRate;
   342	    const x = ship.x + Math.cos(ship.angle) * 18, y = ship.y + Math.sin(ship.angle) * 18;
   343	    bullets.push({ x, y, vx: ship.vx + Math.cos(ship.angle) * 500, vy: ship.vy + Math.sin(ship.angle) * 500,
   344	      life: 0.95, r: 3.2, damage: ship.damage });
   345	    burst(x, y, '#d9fbff', 2, 40);
   346	  }
   347	
   348	  function updateBullets(dt) {
   349	    for (let i = bullets.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   350	      const b = bullets[i]; b.x += b.vx * dt; b.y += b.vy * dt; b.life -= dt; wrap(b, 5);
   351	      if (b.life <= 0) bullets.splice(i, 1);
   352	    }
   353	  }
   354	
   355	  function updateAsteroids(dt) {
   356	    for (const a of asteroids) {
   357	      a.x += a.vx * dt; a.y += a.vy * dt; a.rot += a.spin * dt; a.hit = Math.max(0, a.hit - dt); wrap(a, a.r);
   358	    }
   359	  }
   360	
   361	  function updateMines(dt) {
   362	    for (const m of mines) { m.x += m.vx * dt; m.y += m.vy * dt; m.phase += dt * 3; wrap(m, m.r); }
   363	  }
   364	
   365	  function updateDrones(dt) {
   366	    for (const d of drones) {
   367	      d.phase += dt * 3; d.hit = Math.max(0, d.hit - dt);
   368	      const ang = Math.atan2(ship.y - d.y, ship.x - d.x);
   369	      d.vx += Math.cos(ang) * 38 * dt; d.vy += Math.sin(ang) * 38 * dt;
   370	      const speed = Math.hypot(d.vx, d.vy);
   371	      if (speed > 88) { d.vx = d.vx / speed * 88; d.vy = d.vy / speed * 88; }
   372	      d.vx *= Math.pow(0.994, dt * 60); d.vy *= Math.pow(0.994, dt * 60);
   373	      d.x += d.vx * dt; d.y += d.vy * dt; wrap(d, d.r);
   374	    }
   375	  }
   376	
   377	  function updateCrystals(dt) {
   378	    for (const c of crystals) { c.x += c.vx * dt; c.y += c.vy * dt; c.spin += dt * 2; c.phase += dt * 2; wrap(c, c.r); }
   379	  }
   380	
   381	  function collisions() {
   382	    for (let bi = bullets.length - 1; bi >= 0; bi--) {
   383	      const b = bullets[bi]; let hit = false;
   384	      for (let ai = asteroids.length - 1; ai >= 0 && !hit; ai--) {
   385	        const a = asteroids[ai];
   386	        if (circleHit(b, a)) {
   387	          hit = true; asteroids.splice(ai, 1); splitAsteroid(a); score += a.size === 3 ? 30 : a.size === 2 ? 55 : 90;
   388	          burst(a.x, a.y, a.size === 3 ? '#7d8792' : '#9ca6b2', a.size * 5 + 5, 90); shake = Math.max(shake, a.size * 1.7);
   389	        }
   390	      }
   391	      for (let mi = mines.length - 1; mi >= 0 && !hit; mi--) {
   392	        const m = mines[mi];
   393	        if (circleHit(b, m)) { hit = true; mines.splice(mi, 1); score += 75; burst(m.x, m.y, '#ff966b', 15, 100); shake = Math.max(shake, 4); }
   394	      }
   395	      for (let di = drones.length - 1; di >= 0 && !hit; di--) {
   396	        const d = drones[di];
   397	        if (circleHit(b, d)) { hit = true; d.hp -= b.damage; d.hit = 0.16; burst(b.x, b.y, '#d59bff', 5, 45); if (d.hp <= 0) { drones.splice(di, 1); score += 220; burst(d.x, d.y, '#d59bff', 25, 170); } }
   398	      }
   399	      if (hit) bullets.splice(bi, 1);
   400	    }
   401	    if (ship.invuln <= 0) {
   402	      for (let i = asteroids.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) if (circleHit(ship, asteroids[i], 5)) { asteroids.splice(i, 1); hurt(); break; }
   403	      for (let i = mines.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) if (circleHit(ship, mines[i], 5)) { mines.splice(i, 1); hurt(); break; }
   404	      for (const d of drones) if (circleHit(ship, d, 5)) { hurt(); break; }
   405	    }
   406	    for (let i = crystals.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   407	      if (circleHit(ship, crystals[i], 7)) { const c = crystals[i]; crystals.splice(i, 1); ore++; score += 40; message = 'ORE +1'; messageTimer = 0.8; burst(c.x, c.y, '#70f6b4', 12, 75); }
   408	    }
   409	  }
   410	
   411	  function circleHit(a, b, extra = 0) { const rr = a.r + b.r + extra; return dist2(a, b) < rr * rr; }
   412	
   413	  function hurt() {
   414	    ship.shields--; ship.invuln = 1.55; flash = 1; shake = 12; burst(ship.x, ship.y, '#ff6978', 24, 150);
   415	    ship.vx *= -0.4; ship.vy *= -0.4;
   416	    if (ship.shields <= 0) { state = 'gameover'; saveBest(); burst(ship.x, ship.y, '#ff6978', 50, 230); }
   417	  }
   418	
   419	  function makeOffers() {
   420	    const a = upgrades[Math.floor(rng() * upgrades.length)];
   421	    let b = upgrades[Math.floor(rng() * upgrades.length)];
   422	    while (b.id === a.id) b = upgrades[Math.floor(rng() * upgrades.length)];
   423	    return [a, b];
   424	  }
   425	
   426	  function chooseUpgradeAt(x, y) {
   427	    if (y > H * 0.34 && y < H * 0.75) chooseUpgrade(x < W / 2 ? 0 : 1);
   428	  }
   429	
   430	  function chooseUpgrade(index) {
   431	    if (state !== 'upgrade' || !offers[index]) return;
   432	    const id = offers[index].id;
   433	    if (id === 'rapid') ship.fireRate *= 1.22;
   434	    if (id === 'shield') ship.shields = Math.min(3, ship.shields + 1);
   435	    if (id === 'engine') ship.thrust *= 1.24;
   436	    if (id === 'core') ship.damage += 1;
   437	    message = offers[index].name + ' ONLINE'; messageTimer = 1.2;
   438	    wave++; state = 'play'; waveClearTimer = 0.55; beginWave();
   439	  }
   440	
   441	  function burst(x, y, color, count, speed) {
   442	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   443	      const a = Math.random() * TAU, s = Math.random() * speed;
   444	      particles.push({ x, y, vx: Math.cos(a) * s, vy: Math.sin(a) * s, life: Math.random() * 0.45 + 0.22,
   445	        max: 0.67, size: Math.random() * 2.8 + 1, color });
   446	    }
   447	  }
   448	
   449	  function updateParticles(dt) {
   450	    for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   451	      const p = particles[i]; p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt; p.vx *= 0.96; p.vy *= 0.96; p.life -= dt;
   452	      if (p.life <= 0) particles.splice(i, 1);
   453	    }
   454	  }
   455	
   456	  function draw(time) {
   457	    ctx.save();
   458	    const sx = shake ? (Math.random() - 0.5) * shake : 0, sy = shake ? (Math.random() - 0.5) * shake : 0;
   459	    ctx.translate(sx, sy);
   460	    const bg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H); bg.addColorStop(0, '#081a2a'); bg.addColorStop(1, '#030a12');
   461	    ctx.fillStyle = bg; ctx.fillRect(-20, -20, W + 40, H + 40);
   462	    drawStars(time); drawGrid();
   463	    for (const c of crystals) drawCrystal(c, time);
   464	    for (const a of asteroids) drawAsteroid(a);
   465	    for (const m of mines) drawMine(m, time);
   466	    for (const d of drones) drawDrone(d, time);
   467	    for (const b of bullets) drawBullet(b);
   468	    drawParticles();
   469	    if (state === 'play') drawShip(time);
   470	    drawHud();
   471	    if (state === 'upgrade') drawUpgrade();
   472	    else if (state === 'gameover') drawEnd(false);
   473	    else if (state === 'win') drawEnd(true);
   474	    ctx.restore();
   475	  }
   476	
   477	  function drawStars(time) {
   478	    for (const s of stars) {
   479	      ctx.globalAlpha = s.a * (0.75 + Math.sin(time * 1.4 + s.p) * 0.25);
   480	      ctx.fillStyle = '#a9dbef'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, s.r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   481	    }
   482	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   483	  }
   484	
   485	  function drawGrid() {
   486	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(74,154,184,0.055)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
   487	    const gap = 48;
   488	    for (let x = (W % gap) / 2; x < W; x += gap) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, 0); ctx.lineTo(x, H); ctx.stroke(); }
   489	    for (let y = (H % gap) / 2; y < H; y += gap) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, y); ctx.lineTo(W, y); ctx.stroke(); }
   490	  }
   491	
   492	  function drawAsteroid(a) {
   493	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(a.x, a.y); ctx.rotate(a.rot);
   494	    ctx.fillStyle = a.hit > 0 ? '#dfe8ed' : a.size === 3 ? '#4e5c67' : a.size === 2 ? '#667580' : '#8798a3';
   495	    ctx.strokeStyle = a.size === 3 ? '#9aa8b2' : '#b3c1c8'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.4;
   496	    ctx.beginPath();
   497	    for (let i = 0; i < a.chunks.length; i++) {
   498	      const ang = i / a.chunks.length * TAU, rr = a.r * a.chunks[i];
   499	      const x = Math.cos(ang) * rr, y = Math.sin(ang) * rr;
   500	      if (!i) ctx.moveTo(x, y); else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
   501	    }
   502	    ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
   503	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(225,238,242,0.19)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
   504	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-a.r * 0.55, -a.r * 0.18); ctx.lineTo(a.r * 0.28, a.r * 0.38); ctx.lineTo(a.r * 0.55, a.r * 0.12); ctx.stroke();
   505	    ctx.restore();
   506	  }
   507	
   508	  function drawMine(m, time) {
   509	    const pulse = 1 + Math.sin(m.phase) * 0.1;
   510	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(m.x, m.y); ctx.rotate(m.phase * 0.22);
   511	    ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(255,118,103,${0.2 + (Math.sin(m.phase) + 1) * 0.1})`; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   512	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, m.r * 1.8 * pulse, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
   513	    ctx.fillStyle = '#a8454b'; ctx.strokeStyle = '#ff966b'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
   514	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, m.r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
   515	    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { const a = i / 6 * TAU; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(Math.cos(a) * 8, Math.sin(a) * 8); ctx.lineTo(Math.cos(a) * 16, Math.sin(a) * 16); ctx.stroke(); }
   516	    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffe1b2'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 3, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   517	  }
   518	
   519	  function drawDrone(d) {
   520	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(d.x, d.y); ctx.rotate(Math.atan2(d.vy, d.vx));
   521	    ctx.fillStyle = d.hit > 0 ? '#f4e9ff' : '#573b76'; ctx.strokeStyle = '#d59bff'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
   522	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(19, 0); ctx.lineTo(-11, -12); ctx.lineTo(-7, 0); ctx.lineTo(-11, 12); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
   523	    ctx.fillStyle = '#ff84bb'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(4, 0, 4 + Math.sin(d.phase) * 1.2, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   524	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(213,155,255,.45)'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-8, -7); ctx.lineTo(-16, -12); ctx.moveTo(-8, 7); ctx.lineTo(-16, 12); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore();
   525	  }
   526	
   527	  function drawCrystal(c, time) {
   528	    const pulse = 1 + Math.sin(time * 3 + c.phase) * 0.12;
   529	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(c.x, c.y); ctx.rotate(c.spin);
   530	    ctx.shadowColor = '#70f6b4'; ctx.shadowBlur = 12;
   531	    ctx.fillStyle = '#56cf9e'; ctx.strokeStyle = '#b5ffe0'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
   532	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, -12 * pulse); ctx.lineTo(8 * pulse, 0); ctx.lineTo(0, 12 * pulse); ctx.lineTo(-8 * pulse, 0); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
   533	    ctx.shadowBlur = 0; ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(230,255,245,.65)'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, -7); ctx.lineTo(3, 0); ctx.lineTo(0, 5); ctx.lineTo(-2, 0); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   534	  }
   535	
   536	  function drawBullet(b) {
   537	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(b.x, b.y); ctx.rotate(Math.atan2(b.vy, b.vx)); ctx.shadowColor = '#d9fbff'; ctx.shadowBlur = 10;
   538	    ctx.fillStyle = '#e8ffff'; ctx.fillRect(-7, -1.6, 14, 3.2); ctx.restore();
   539	  }
   540	
   541	  function drawShip(time) {
   542	    if (ship.invuln > 0 && Math.floor(time * 18) % 2 === 0) return;
   543	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(ship.x, ship.y); ctx.rotate(ship.angle);
   544	    if (ship.dash > 0 || Math.hypot(ship.vx, ship.vy) > 20) {
   545	      const flame = 9 + Math.random() * 8 + (ship.dash > 0 ? 18 : 0);
   546	      ctx.fillStyle = ship.dash > 0 ? '#ffcf66' : '#55e6ff'; ctx.globalAlpha = 0.8;
   547	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-9, -5); ctx.lineTo(-9 - flame, 0); ctx.lineTo(-9, 5); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   548	    }
   549	    ctx.shadowColor = '#55e6ff'; ctx.shadowBlur = 13; ctx.fillStyle = '#baf6ff'; ctx.strokeStyle = '#55e6ff'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.8;
   550	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(19, 0); ctx.lineTo(-10, -11); ctx.lineTo(-5, 0); ctx.lineTo(-10, 11); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
   551	    ctx.shadowBlur = 0; ctx.fillStyle = '#1b4b61'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(7, 0); ctx.lineTo(-5, -5); ctx.lineTo(-4, 5); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   552	  }
   553	
   554	  function drawParticles() {
   555	    for (const p of particles) { ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(p.life / p.max, 0, 1); ctx.fillStyle = p.color; ctx.fillRect(p.x - p.size / 2, p.y - p.size / 2, p.size, p.size); }
   556	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   557	  }
   558	
   559	  function drawHud() {
   560	    const pad = 18;
   561	    ctx.fillStyle = '#e7f6ff'; ctx.font = '700 13px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.fillText('HULLBREAKER', pad, 25);
   562	    ctx.fillStyle = '#80a9b9'; ctx.font = '600 10px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText('WAVE ' + wave + '  /  ' + WIN_WAVE, pad, 42);
   563	    ctx.textAlign = 'right'; ctx.fillStyle = '#e7f6ff'; ctx.font = '700 15px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText(String(score).padStart(5, '0'), W - pad, 25);
   564	    ctx.fillStyle = '#80a9b9'; ctx.font = '600 10px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText('BEST ' + String(best).padStart(5, '0'), W - pad, 42);
   565	    ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.fillStyle = '#70f6b4'; ctx.font = '600 11px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText('◆ ' + ore, pad, 62);
   566	    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { ctx.strokeStyle = i < ship.shields ? '#55e6ff' : 'rgba(90,125,143,.3)'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(W - pad - 40 + i * 18, 60, 6, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke(); }
   567	    if (state === 'play') {
   568	      ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(185,227,239,.42)'; ctx.font = '600 10px system-ui, sans-serif';
   569	      ctx.fillText('DRAG LEFT PAD  •  HOLD FIRE  •  DOUBLE-TAP DASH', W / 2, H - 18);
   570	      drawControls();
   571	    }
   572	    if (messageTimer > 0 && state === 'play') { ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(messageTimer, 0, 1); ctx.fillStyle = '#baf6ff'; ctx.font = '800 22px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText(message, W / 2, H * 0.24); ctx.globalAlpha = 1; }
   573	    if (flash > 0) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255,74,91,${flash * 0.12})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
   574	  }
   575	
   576	  function drawControls() {
   577	    const cx = W * 0.19, cy = H - 112, r = 65;
   578	    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.55; ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(37,82,101,.52)'; ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(119,210,233,.55)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
   579	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
   580	    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.8; ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(98,196,219,.52)';
   581	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx + stick.x * r * 0.65, cy + stick.y * r * 0.65, 26, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   582	    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.8; ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(118,194,215,.13)'; ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(118,194,215,.45)';
   583	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(W - 60, H - 105, 56, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
   584	    ctx.globalAlpha = firePointer !== -1 ? 1 : 0.78; ctx.fillStyle = firePointer !== -1 ? '#ff8b88' : '#ff6978'; ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffc0b5';
   585	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(W - 60, H - 105, 40, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
   586	    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff7f5'; ctx.font = '800 13px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.fillText('FIRE', W - 60, H - 101);
   587	    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.86; ctx.fillStyle = dashCooldown > 0 ? '#6b7880' : '#d19a47'; ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffdc8e';
   588	    roundRect(W - 94, H - 252, 68, 34, 9); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
   589	    ctx.fillStyle = dashCooldown > 0 ? '#bac3c7' : '#fff5cf'; ctx.font = '800 10px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText(dashCooldown > 0 ? 'CHARGING' : 'DASH ×2', W - 60, H - 230);
   590	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   591	  }
   592	
   593	  function drawUpgrade() {
   594	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(2,10,17,.76)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   595	    ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.fillStyle = '#e7f6ff'; ctx.font = '800 25px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText('WAVE CLEARED', W / 2, H * 0.19);
   596	    ctx.fillStyle = '#80a9b9'; ctx.font = '600 12px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText('CHOOSE ONE UPGRADE  •  TAP A CARD', W / 2, H * 0.24);
   597	    const cards = [{ x: W * 0.06, w: W * 0.41 }, { x: W * 0.53, w: W * 0.41 }];
   598	    for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
   599	      const o = offers[i], c = cards[i], y = H * 0.34, h = H * 0.31;
   600	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(16,39,52,.96)'; ctx.strokeStyle = o.color; ctx.lineWidth = 2; roundRect(c.x, y, c.w, h, 14); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
   601	      ctx.fillStyle = o.color; ctx.font = '900 13px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText(String(i + 1), c.x + c.w / 2, y + 29);
   602	      ctx.fillStyle = '#f2fbff'; ctx.font = '800 13px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText(o.name, c.x + c.w / 2, y + h * 0.5);
   603	      ctx.fillStyle = '#9bb8c2'; ctx.font = '600 11px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText(o.detail, c.x + c.w / 2, y + h * 0.68);
   604	    }
   605	    ctx.fillStyle = '#70f6b4'; ctx.font = '600 11px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText('ORE BANK  ◆ ' + ore, W / 2, H * 0.78);
   606	    ctx.fillStyle = '#6d8d99'; ctx.font = '600 10px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText('KEYBOARD: 1 / 2', W / 2, H * 0.82);
   607	  }
   608	
   609	  function drawEnd(win) {
   610	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(2,10,17,.82)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   611	    ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.fillStyle = win ? '#70f6b4' : '#ff8b88'; ctx.font = '900 29px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText(win ? 'SECTOR CLEAR' : 'HULL BREACH', W / 2, H * 0.31);
   612	    ctx.fillStyle = '#e7f6ff'; ctx.font = '800 18px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText('SCORE  ' + String(score).padStart(5, '0'), W / 2, H * 0.42);
   613	    ctx.fillStyle = '#8aa9b6'; ctx.font = '600 12px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText('BEST  ' + String(best).padStart(5, '0') + '   •   WAVE ' + wave, W / 2, H * 0.48);
   614	    ctx.fillStyle = win ? '#70f6b4' : '#ffcf66'; ctx.font = '800 13px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText('TAP OR PRESS ENTER TO RESTART', W / 2, H * 0.62);
   615	  }
   616	
   617	  function roundRect(x, y, w, h, r) {
   618	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x + r, y); ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r); ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r); ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r); ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r); ctx.closePath();
   619	  }
   620	})();

   130	  function buildLevel() {
   131	    random = makeRandom((0xB17A5E + level * 0x9E3779B9) >>> 0);
   132	    grid = Array.from({ length: ROWS }, (_, y) => Array.from({ length: COLS }, (_, x) => {
   133	      if (x === 0 || y === 0 || x === COLS - 1 || y === ROWS - 1) return 2;
