   135	      return random() < Math.min(0.72, 0.51 + level * 0.022) ? 1 : 0;
   136	    }));
   137	    const safe = [[1, 1], [2, 1], [1, 2], [3, 1], [1, 3]];
   138	    safe.forEach(([x, y]) => { if (inBounds(x, y) && grid[y][x] !== 2) grid[y][x] = 0; });
   139	    powerups = [];
   140	    for (let y = 1; y < ROWS - 1; y += 1) {
   141	      for (let x = 1; x < COLS - 1; x += 1) {
   142	        if (grid[y][x] === 1 && random() < 0.19) powerups.push({ x, y, type: POWERUPS[Math.floor(random() * POWERUPS.length)], visible: false });
   143	      }
   144	    }
   145	    bombs = [];
   146	    enemies = [];
   147	    player = { x: 1, y: 1, fromX: 1, fromY: 1, toX: 1, toY: 1, moveT: 1, moveDur: 0.12, nextDir: null, radius: 2, bombsMax: 1, speed: 1, kick: false, invuln: 0 };
   148	    const candidates = [];
   149	    for (let y = 1; y < ROWS - 1; y += 1) {
   150	      for (let x = 1; x < COLS - 1; x += 1) {
   151	        if (grid[y][x] === 0 && Math.abs(x - 1) + Math.abs(y - 1) > 7) candidates.push({ x, y });
   152	      }
   153	    }
   154	    const enemyCount = Math.min(5, 3 + Math.floor((level - 1) / 2));
   155	    const enemyTypes = ['wanderer', 'hunter', 'bomber', 'hunter', 'bomber'];
   156	    for (let i = 0; i < enemyCount && candidates.length; i += 1) {
   157	      const index = Math.floor(random() * candidates.length);
   158	      const spot = candidates.splice(index, 1)[0];
   159	      enemies.push({ x: spot.x, y: spot.y, type: enemyTypes[i], timer: random() * 0.3, cooldown: 0, bombTimer: 2 + random() * 2, dir: DIRS[Math.floor(random() * DIRS.length)] });
   160	    }
   161	    showBanner('SECTOR ' + String(level).padStart(2, '0'), 1.3);
   162	  }
   163	
   164	  function requestMove(dx, dy) {
   165	    if (state !== 'playing' || !player) return;
   166	    player.nextDir = { dx, dy };
   167	    tryPlayerStep();
   168	  }
   169	
   170	  function tryPlayerStep() {
   171	    if (!player || player.moveT < 1 || !player.nextDir || state !== 'playing') return;
   172	    const dir = player.nextDir;
   173	    player.nextDir = null;
   174	    const x = player.x + dir.dx;
   175	    const y = player.y + dir.dy;
   176	    const bomb = bombAt(x, y);
   177	    if (bomb) {
   178	      if (player.kick && kickBomb(bomb, dir.dx, dir.dy)) {
   179	        startPlayerMove(x, y);
   180	      } else {
   181	        burstAt(x, y, '#ffca5f', 2);
   182	      }
   183	      return;
   184	    }
   185	    if (blocked(x, y, false)) {
   186	      burstAt(player.x + dir.dx * 0.35, player.y + dir.dy * 0.35, '#53747b', 1);
   187	      return;
   188	    }
   189	    startPlayerMove(x, y);
   190	  }
   191	
   192	  function startPlayerMove(x, y) {
   193	    player.fromX = player.x;
   194	    player.fromY = player.y;
   195	    player.toX = x;
   196	    player.toY = y;
   197	    player.moveT = 0;
   198	    player.moveDur = Math.max(0.055, 0.14 / player.speed);
   199	  }
   200	
   201	  function kickBomb(bomb, dx, dy) {
   202	    let x = bomb.x + dx;
   203	    let y = bomb.y + dy;
   204	    if (blocked(x, y)) return false;
   205	    bomb.x = x;
   206	    bomb.y = y;
   207	    bomb.slide = { dx, dy, time: 0.12 };
   208	    burstAt(x, y, '#b38cff', 4);
   209	    beep(280, 0.06, 'triangle');
   210	    return true;
   211	  }
   212	
   213	  function placeBomb() {
   214	    ensureAudio();
   215	    if (state !== 'playing' || !player || player.moveT < 1) return;
   216	    if (bombs.filter((bomb) => bomb.owner === 'player' && !bomb.exploded).length >= player.bombsMax) {
   217	      burstAt(player.x, player.y, '#ff769c', 2);
   218	      return;
   219	    }
   220	    if (bombAt(player.x, player.y)) return;
   221	    bombs.push({ x: player.x, y: player.y, fuse: Math.max(0.92, 1.32 - level * 0.015), radius: player.radius, owner: 'player', exploded: false, phase: random() * Math.PI * 2 });
   222	    burstAt(player.x, player.y, '#ffca5f', 5);
   223	    beep(160, 0.07, 'sine');
   224	  }
   225	
   226	  function placeEnemyBomb(enemy) {
   227	    if (bombAt(enemy.x, enemy.y)) return;
   228	    bombs.push({ x: enemy.x, y: enemy.y, fuse: 1.65, radius: Math.min(3, 1 + Math.floor(level / 3)), owner: 'enemy', exploded: false, phase: random() * Math.PI * 2 });
   229	    burstAt(enemy.x, enemy.y, '#ff6d67', 4);
   230	    beep(110, 0.045, 'square');
   231	  }
   232	
   233	  function detonateBomb(bomb) {
   234	    if (!bomb || bomb.exploded) return;
   235	    bomb.exploded = true;
   236	    bombs = bombs.filter((item) => item !== bomb);
   237	    const cells = [{ x: bomb.x, y: bomb.y }];
   238	    for (const dir of DIRS) {
   239	      for (let distance = 1; distance <= bomb.radius; distance += 1) {
   240	        const x = bomb.x + dir.dx * distance;
   241	        const y = bomb.y + dir.dy * distance;
   242	        if (!inBounds(x, y) || grid[y][x] === 2) break;
   243	        cells.push({ x, y });
   244	        if (grid[y][x] === 1) break;
   245	      }
   246	    }
   247	    for (const cell of cells) {
   248	      if (grid[cell.y][cell.x] === 1) {
   249	        grid[cell.y][cell.x] = 0;
   250	        score += 10;
   251	        const boost = powerups.find((item) => item.x === cell.x && item.y === cell.y);
   252	        if (boost) boost.visible = true;
   253	        burstAt(cell.x, cell.y, '#d7a46c', 8);
   254	      }
   255	      const next = bombAt(cell.x, cell.y);
   256	      if (next) detonateBomb(next);
   257	      for (let i = enemies.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
   258	        if (enemies[i].x === cell.x && enemies[i].y === cell.y) defeatEnemy(i);
   259	      }
   260	      if (player && player.x === cell.x && player.y === cell.y) harmPlayer();
   261	    }
   262	    blasts.push({ cells, life: 0.38, max: 0.38, owner: bomb.owner });
   263	    shake = Math.max(shake, bomb.owner === 'player' ? 6 : 4);
   264	    beep(bomb.owner === 'player' ? 70 : 55, 0.1, 'sawtooth');
   265	    checkClear();
   266	  }
   267	
   268	  function defeatEnemy(index) {
   269	    const enemy = enemies[index];
   270	    if (!enemy) return;
   271	    score += 100;
   272	    burstAt(enemy.x, enemy.y, enemy.type === 'bomber' ? '#ff6d67' : '#68e0d1', 16);
   273	    enemies.splice(index, 1);
   274	    beep(420 + enemies.length * 25, 0.09, 'triangle');
   275	  }
   276	
   277	  function harmPlayer() {
   278	    if (!player || player.invuln > 0 || state !== 'playing') return;
   279	    lives -= 1;
   280	    saveBest();
   281	    player.invuln = 1.1;
   282	    state = lives > 0 ? 'lifeLost' : 'gameover';
   283	    stateTimer = lives > 0 ? 0.85 : 0;
   284	    shake = 12;
   285	    burstAt(player.x, player.y, '#ff769c', 22);
   286	    beep(75, 0.2, 'sawtooth');
   287	    if (state === 'gameover') openScreen();
   288	  }
   289	
   290	  function collectPowerup() {
   291	    const boost = powerups.find((item) => item.visible && item.x === player.x && item.y === player.y);
   292	    if (!boost) return;
   293	    boost.visible = false;
   294	    score += 35;
   295	    if (boost.type === 'radius') player.radius = Math.min(5, player.radius + 1);
   296	    if (boost.type === 'extra') player.bombsMax = Math.min(3, player.bombsMax + 1);
   297	    if (boost.type === 'speed') player.speed = Math.min(1.8, player.speed + 0.25);
   298	    if (boost.type === 'kick') player.kick = true;
   299	    showBanner(boost.type.toUpperCase() + ' BOOST', 0.9);
   300	    burstAt(player.x, player.y, COLORS[boost.type], 14);
   301	    beep(650, 0.12, 'sine');
   302	  }
   303	
   304	  function updatePlayer(dt) {
   305	    if (player.invuln > 0) player.invuln -= dt;
   306	    if (player.moveT < 1) {
   307	      player.moveT = Math.min(1, player.moveT + dt / player.moveDur);
   308	      if (player.moveT >= 1) {
   309	        player.x = player.toX;
   310	        player.y = player.toY;
   311	        collectPowerup();
   312	        checkContact();
   313	        tryPlayerStep();
   314	      }
   315	    } else if (input.held && input.dir && performance.now() - input.lastRepeat > 145) {
   316	      input.lastRepeat = performance.now();
   317	      requestMove(input.dir.dx, input.dir.dy);
   318	    }
   319	  }
   320	
   321	  function getPlayerDrawPosition() {
   322	    if (!player || player.moveT >= 1) return { x: player.x, y: player.y };
   323	    const t = player.moveT * player.moveT * (3 - 2 * player.moveT);
   324	    return { x: player.fromX + (player.toX - player.fromX) * t, y: player.fromY + (player.toY - player.fromY) * t };
   325	  }
   326	
   327	  function enemyStep(enemy, dx, dy) {
   328	    if (Math.abs(dx) + Math.abs(dy) !== 1) return false;
   329	    const x = enemy.x + dx;
   330	    const y = enemy.y + dy;
   331	    if (blocked(x, y) || enemies.some((other) => other !== enemy && other.x === x && other.y === y)) return false;
   332	    enemy.x = x;
   333	    enemy.y = y;
   334	    enemy.dir = { dx, dy };
   335	    return true;
   336	  }
   337	
   338	  function nextStepToward(startX, startY, targetX, targetY) {
   339	    const queue = [{ x: startX, y: startY }];
   340	    const seen = new Set([startX + ',' + startY]);
   341	    const parent = new Map();
   342	    while (queue.length) {
   343	      const current = queue.shift();
   344	      if (current.x === targetX && current.y === targetY) break;
   345	      for (const dir of DIRS) {
   346	        const x = current.x + dir.dx;
   347	        const y = current.y + dir.dy;
   348	        const key = x + ',' + y;
   349	        if (seen.has(key) || blocked(x, y) || enemies.some((other) => other.x === x && other.y === y)) continue;
   350	        seen.add(key);
   351	        parent.set(key, { x: current.x, y: current.y });
   352	        queue.push({ x, y });
   353	      }
   354	    }
   355	    let key = targetX + ',' + targetY;
   356	    if (!seen.has(key)) return null;
   357	    while (parent.has(key)) {
   358	      const parentCell = parent.get(key);
   359	      if (parentCell.x === startX && parentCell.y === startY) break;
   360	      key = parentCell.x + ',' + parentCell.y;
   361	    }
   362	    const step = key.split(',').map(Number);
   363	    return { dx: step[0] - startX, dy: step[1] - startY };
   364	  }
   365	
   366	  function updateEnemies(dt) {
   367	    for (const enemy of enemies.slice()) {
   368	      enemy.timer -= dt;
   369	      enemy.cooldown -= dt;
   370	      enemy.bombTimer -= dt;
   371	      if (enemy.timer > 0 || state !== 'playing') continue;
   372	      enemy.timer = Math.max(0.21, 0.46 - level * 0.012) + random() * 0.12;
   373	      if (enemy.type === 'bomber' && enemy.bombTimer <= 0 && !bombAt(enemy.x, enemy.y)) {
   374	        enemy.bombTimer = Math.max(1.1, 2.4 - level * 0.08);
   375	        placeEnemyBomb(enemy);
   376	      }
   377	      let step = null;
   378	      if (enemy.type === 'wanderer') {
   379	        if (random() < 0.55 && !blocked(enemy.x + enemy.dir.dx, enemy.y + enemy.dir.dy)) step = enemy.dir;
   380	        else step = DIRS[Math.floor(random() * DIRS.length)];
   381	      } else {
   382	        step = nextStepToward(enemy.x, enemy.y, player.x, player.y) || DIRS[Math.floor(random() * DIRS.length)];
   383	        if (enemy.type === 'bomber' && Math.abs(enemy.x - player.x) + Math.abs(enemy.y - player.y) > 6 && random() < 0.3) step = DIRS[Math.floor(random() * DIRS.length)];
   384	      }
   385	      enemyStep(enemy, step.dx, step.dy);
   386	      checkContact();
   387	    }
   388	  }
   389	
   390	  function checkContact() {
   391	    if (!player || state !== 'playing' || player.invuln > 0) return;
   392	    const p = getPlayerDrawPosition();
   393	    if (enemies.some((enemy) => Math.abs(enemy.x - p.x) < 0.55 && Math.abs(enemy.y - p.y) < 0.55)) harmPlayer();
   394	    for (const blast of blasts) {
   395	      if (blast.life <= 0) continue;
   396	      if (blast.cells.some((cell) => cell.x === player.x && cell.y === player.y)) { harmPlayer(); break; }
   397	    }
   398	  }
   399	
   400	  function checkClear() {
   401	    if (state === 'playing' && enemies.length === 0) {
   402	      score += 300;
   403	      saveBest();
   404	      state = 'levelclear';
   405	      stateTimer = 1.15;
   406	      showBanner('ARENA CLEARED  +300', 1.15);
   407	      beep(520, 0.16, 'triangle');
   408	    }
   409	  }
   410	
   411	  function update(dt) {
   412	    const safeDt = Math.min(0.05, dt);
   413	    shake = Math.max(0, shake - safeDt * 22);
   414	    bannerTimer = Math.max(0, bannerTimer - safeDt);
   415	    for (const particle of particles) {
   416	      particle.life -= safeDt;
   417	      particle.x += particle.vx * safeDt;
   418	      particle.y += particle.vy * safeDt;
   419	      particle.vx *= 0.96;
   420	      particle.vy *= 0.96;
   421	    }
   422	    particles = particles.filter((particle) => particle.life > 0);
   423	    for (const blast of blasts) blast.life -= safeDt;
   424	    blasts = blasts.filter((blast) => blast.life > 0);
   425	
   426	    if (state === 'playing') {
   427	      updatePlayer(safeDt);
   428	      for (const bomb of bombs.slice()) {
   429	        bomb.fuse -= safeDt;
   430	        if (bomb.slide) bomb.slide.time -= safeDt;
   431	        if (bomb.fuse <= 0) detonateBomb(bomb);
   432	      }
   433	      updateEnemies(safeDt);
   434	      checkContact();
   435	      checkClear();
   436	    } else if (state === 'lifeLost') {
   437	      stateTimer -= safeDt;
   438	      if (stateTimer <= 0) { state = 'playing'; buildLevel(); }
   439	    } else if (state === 'levelclear') {
   440	      stateTimer -= safeDt;
   441	      if (stateTimer <= 0) { level += 1; state = 'playing'; buildLevel(); }
   442	    }
   443	  }
   444	
   445	  function burstAt(x, y, color, amount) {
   446	    for (let i = 0; i < amount; i += 1) {
   447	      const angle = random() * Math.PI * 2;
   448	      const speed = 0.45 + random() * 1.6;
   449	      particles.push({ x, y, vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed, vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed, color, size: 1.5 + random() * 2.8, life: 0.34 + random() * 0.48, max: 0.82 });
   450	    }
   451	  }
   452	
   453	  function drawBackground() {
   454	    const gradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, height);
   455	    gradient.addColorStop(0, '#0a1a24');
   456	    gradient.addColorStop(0.55, '#07131c');
   457	    gradient.addColorStop(1, '#050d14');
   458	    ctx.fillStyle = gradient;
   459	    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
   460	    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.22;
   461	    ctx.fillStyle = '#68e0d1';
   462	    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i += 1) {
   463	      const x = (i * 97 + 38) % width;
   464	      const y = (i * 61 + 18) % height;
   465	      ctx.fillRect(x, y, 1, 1);
   466	    }
   467	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   468	  }
   469	
   470	  function drawHeader() {
   471	    ctx.fillStyle = '#8ba9ae';
   472	    ctx.font = '800 10px system-ui, sans-serif';
   473	    ctx.letterSpacing = '0.18em';
   474	    ctx.fillText('BLAST', 18, 27);
   475	    ctx.fillStyle = '#68e0d1';
   476	    ctx.fillText('RADIUS', 67, 27);
   477	    ctx.letterSpacing = 'normal';
   478	    ctx.fillStyle = '#47636b';
   479	    ctx.fillRect(18, 37, width - 36, 1);
   480	    ctx.fillStyle = '#d4e5e2';
   481	    ctx.font = '700 16px system-ui, sans-serif';
   482	    ctx.fillText(String(score).padStart(5, '0'), 18, 63);
   483	    ctx.fillStyle = '#78969b';
   484	    ctx.font = '700 9px system-ui, sans-serif';
   485	    ctx.fillText('SCORE', 20, 78);
   486	    ctx.fillStyle = '#d4e5e2';
   487	    ctx.font = '700 14px system-ui, sans-serif';
   488	    ctx.fillText('A' + String(level).padStart(2, '0'), width / 2 - 14, 58);
   489	    ctx.fillStyle = '#78969b';
   490	    ctx.font = '700 9px system-ui, sans-serif';
   491	    ctx.fillText('ARENA', width / 2 - 15, 74);
   492	    ctx.fillStyle = '#78969b';
   493	    ctx.fillText('BEST ' + String(best).padStart(5, '0'), width - 80, 25);
   494	    ctx.fillStyle = '#ff769c';
   495	    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) {
   496	      ctx.globalAlpha = i < lives ? 1 : 0.18;
   497	      ctx.beginPath();
   498	      ctx.arc(width - 28 - i * 16, 56, 5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
   499	      ctx.fill();
   500	    }
   501	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   502	  }
   503	
   504	  function drawArena() {
   505	    ctx.save();
   506	    if (shake > 0) ctx.translate((Math.random() - 0.5) * shake, (Math.random() - 0.5) * shake);
   507	    ctx.shadowColor = '#000b';
   508	    ctx.shadowBlur = 22;
   509	    ctx.fillStyle = '#050d13';
   510	    roundRect(boardX - 8, boardY - 8, boardW + 16, boardH + 16, 16);
   511	    ctx.fill();
   512	    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   513	    for (let y = 0; y < ROWS; y += 1) {
   514	      for (let x = 0; x < COLS; x += 1) {
   515	        const px = boardX + x * tile;
   516	        const py = boardY + y * tile;
   517	        const value = grid[y][x];
   518	        ctx.fillStyle = value === 2 ? '#18323c' : '#0d222b';
   519	        ctx.fillRect(px + 1, py + 1, tile - 2, tile - 2);
   520	        if (value === 0) {
   521	          ctx.fillStyle = (x + y) % 2 ? '#102730' : '#0e242d';
   522	          ctx.fillRect(px + 2, py + 2, tile - 4, tile - 4);
   523	          ctx.fillStyle = '#21404a';
   524	          ctx.globalAlpha = 0.28;
   525	          ctx.fillRect(px + tile * 0.22, py + tile * 0.22, 2, 2);
   526	          ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   527	        } else if (value === 1) {
   528	          const soft = ctx.createLinearGradient(px, py, px + tile, py + tile);
   529	          soft.addColorStop(0, '#52616a');
   530	          soft.addColorStop(0.45, '#344850');
   531	          soft.addColorStop(1, '#243940');
   532	          ctx.fillStyle = soft;
   533	          roundRect(px + 3, py + 3, tile - 6, tile - 6, 5);
   534	          ctx.fill();
   535	          ctx.fillStyle = '#77878b';
   536	          ctx.globalAlpha = 0.35;
   537	          ctx.fillRect(px + tile * 0.25, py + tile * 0.29, tile * 0.48, 2);
   538	          ctx.fillRect(px + tile * 0.25, py + tile * 0.43, tile * 0.3, 2);
   539	          ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   540	        } else {
   541	          ctx.fillStyle = '#294b56';
   542	          ctx.fillRect(px + 4, py + 4, tile - 8, tile - 8);
   543	          ctx.fillStyle = '#4f7a80';
   544	          ctx.globalAlpha = 0.55;
   545	          ctx.fillRect(px + 7, py + 7, tile - 14, 2);
   546	          ctx.fillRect(px + 7, py + 7, 2, tile - 14);
   547	          ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   548	        }
   549	      }
   550	    }
   551	    for (const boost of powerups) if (boost.visible && grid[boost.y][boost.x] === 0) drawPowerup(boost);
   552	    for (const bomb of bombs) drawBomb(bomb);
   553	    for (const blast of blasts) drawBlast(blast);
   554	    for (const enemy of enemies) drawEnemy(enemy);
   555	    drawPlayer();
   556	    for (const particle of particles) drawParticle(particle);
   557	    ctx.restore();
   558	  }
   559	
   560	  function drawPowerup(boost) {
   561	    const p = center(boost.x, boost.y);
   562	    const pulse = 1 + Math.sin(performance.now() / 180 + boost.x) * 0.08;
   563	    ctx.save();
   564	    ctx.translate(p.x, p.y);
   565	    ctx.scale(pulse, pulse);
   566	    ctx.shadowColor = COLORS[boost.type];
   567	    ctx.shadowBlur = 14;
   568	    ctx.fillStyle = COLORS[boost.type];
   569	    ctx.beginPath();
   570	    ctx.moveTo(0, -tile * 0.27);
   571	    ctx.lineTo(tile * 0.23, 0);
   572	    ctx.lineTo(0, tile * 0.27);
   573	    ctx.lineTo(-tile * 0.23, 0);
   574	    ctx.closePath();
   575	    ctx.fill();
   576	    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   577	    ctx.fillStyle = '#0b1b23';
   578	    ctx.font = '900 ' + Math.max(9, tile * 0.25) + 'px system-ui, sans-serif';
   579	    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   580	    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
   581	    ctx.fillText(boost.type === 'radius' ? '+' : boost.type === 'extra' ? '·' : boost.type === 'speed' ? '»' : '↗', 0, 0);
   582	    ctx.restore();
   583	  }
   584	
   585	  function drawBomb(bomb) {
   586	    const p = center(bomb.x, bomb.y);
   587	    const pulse = 1 + Math.sin(performance.now() / 110 + bomb.phase) * 0.08;
   588	    const color = bomb.owner === 'player' ? '#ffca5f' : '#ff6d67';
   589	    ctx.save();
   590	    ctx.translate(p.x, p.y);
   591	    ctx.scale(pulse, pulse);
   592	    ctx.shadowColor = color;
   593	    ctx.shadowBlur = 18;
   594	    ctx.fillStyle = '#08141b';
   595	    ctx.beginPath();
   596	    ctx.arc(0, 2, tile * 0.27, 0, Math.PI * 2);
   597	    ctx.fill();
   598	    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   599	    ctx.strokeStyle = color;
   600	    ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(2, tile * 0.07);
   601	    ctx.stroke();
   602	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#f7e1a4';
   603	    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   604	    ctx.beginPath();
   605	    ctx.moveTo(4, -tile * 0.2);
   606	    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(tile * 0.18, -tile * 0.39, tile * 0.13, -tile * 0.18);
   607	    ctx.stroke();
   608	    ctx.fillStyle = color;
   609	    ctx.beginPath();
   610	    ctx.arc(0, 2, tile * 0.1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
   611	    ctx.fill();
   612	    ctx.restore();
   613	  }
   614	
   615	  function drawBlast(blast) {
   616	    const alpha = Math.min(1, blast.life / 0.12, (blast.max - blast.life) / 0.07 + 0.35);
   617	    ctx.save();
   618	    ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, alpha);
   619	    for (const cell of blast.cells) {
   620	      const px = boardX + cell.x * tile;
   621	      const py = boardY + cell.y * tile;
   622	      const gradient = ctx.createRadialGradient(px + tile / 2, py + tile / 2, 1, px + tile / 2, py + tile / 2, tile * 0.7);
   623	      gradient.addColorStop(0, '#fff6c0');
   624	      gradient.addColorStop(0.28, blast.owner === 'enemy' ? '#ff6d67' : '#ffca5f');
   625	      gradient.addColorStop(1, '#ff704000');
   626	      ctx.fillStyle = gradient;
   627	      ctx.fillRect(px - 2, py - 2, tile + 4, tile + 4);
   628	      ctx.fillStyle = '#fff5b0';
   629	      ctx.globalAlpha *= 0.55;
   630	      ctx.fillRect(px + tile * 0.38, py + tile * 0.08, tile * 0.24, tile * 0.84);
   631	      ctx.globalAlpha /= 0.55;
   632	    }
   633	    ctx.restore();
   634	  }
   635	
   636	  function drawEnemy(enemy) {
   637	    const p = center(enemy.x, enemy.y);
   638	    const color = enemy.type === 'wanderer' ? '#b38cff' : enemy.type === 'hunter' ? '#68e0d1' : '#ff6d67';
   639	    ctx.save();
   640	    ctx.translate(p.x, p.y);
   641	    ctx.shadowColor = color;
   642	    ctx.shadowBlur = 12;
   643	    ctx.fillStyle = color;
   644	    ctx.beginPath();
   645	    if (enemy.type === 'hunter') {
   646	      ctx.moveTo(0, -tile * 0.3); ctx.lineTo(tile * 0.27, 0); ctx.lineTo(0, tile * 0.3); ctx.lineTo(-tile * 0.27, 0);
   647	    } else if (enemy.type === 'bomber') {
   648	      ctx.arc(0, 0, tile * 0.28, 0, Math.PI * 2);
   649	    } else {
   650	      ctx.rect(-tile * 0.24, -tile * 0.24, tile * 0.48, tile * 0.48);
   651	    }
   652	    ctx.fill();
   653	    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   654	    ctx.fillStyle = '#09202a';
   655	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-tile * 0.09, -tile * 0.04, tile * 0.045, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   656	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(tile * 0.09, -tile * 0.04, tile * 0.045, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   657	    ctx.fillStyle = '#d9ffff';
   658	    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.5;
   659	    ctx.fillRect(-tile * 0.12, tile * 0.13, tile * 0.24, 2);
   660	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   661	    ctx.restore();
   662	  }
   663	
   664	  function drawPlayer() {
   665	    if (!player) return;
   666	    const p = getPlayerDrawPosition();
   667	    const point = center(p.x, p.y);
   668	    if (player.invuln > 0 && Math.floor(player.invuln * 14) % 2 === 0) return;
   669	    ctx.save();
   670	    ctx.translate(point.x, point.y);
   671	    ctx.shadowColor = '#68e0d1';
   672	    ctx.shadowBlur = 16;
   673	    ctx.fillStyle = '#68e0d1';
   674	    ctx.beginPath();
   675	    ctx.moveTo(0, -tile * 0.34);
   676	    ctx.lineTo(tile * 0.28, tile * 0.25);
   677	    ctx.lineTo(0, tile * 0.16);
   678	    ctx.lineTo(-tile * 0.28, tile * 0.25);
   679	    ctx.closePath();
   680	    ctx.fill();
   681	    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   682	    ctx.fillStyle = '#0a242c';
   683	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-tile * 0.08, -tile * 0.02, tile * 0.045, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   684	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(tile * 0.08, -tile * 0.02, tile * 0.045, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   685	    ctx.fillStyle = '#c6fff1';
   686	    ctx.fillRect(-tile * 0.1, tile * 0.13, tile * 0.2, 2);
   687	    ctx.restore();
   688	  }
   689	
   690	  function drawParticle(particle) {
   691	    const p = center(particle.x, particle.y);
   692	    ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, particle.life / particle.max);
   693	    ctx.fillStyle = particle.color;
   694	    ctx.beginPath();
   695	    ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, particle.size * Math.max(0.4, particle.life / particle.max), 0, Math.PI * 2);
   696	    ctx.fill();
   697	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   698	  }
   699	
   700	  function drawBanner() {
   701	    if (!bannerTimer) return;
   702	    const alpha = Math.min(1, bannerTimer * 3, 1.1 - bannerTimer + 0.4);
   703	    ctx.save();
   704	    ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, alpha));
   705	    ctx.fillStyle = '#c5e8e4';
   706	    ctx.font = '900 12px system-ui, sans-serif';
   707	    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   708	    ctx.letterSpacing = '0.16em';
   709	    ctx.fillText(banner, width / 2, boardY - 21);
   710	    ctx.restore();
   711	  }
   712	
   713	  function drawStateMessage() {
   714	    if (state === 'lifeLost') {
   715	      ctx.save();
   716	      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff769c';
   717	      ctx.font = '900 14px system-ui, sans-serif';
   718	      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   719	      ctx.fillText('SIGNAL LOST', width / 2, boardY + boardH + 30);
   720	      ctx.restore();
   721	    } else if (state === 'levelclear') {
   722	      ctx.save();
   723	      ctx.fillStyle = '#68e0d1';
   724	      ctx.font = '900 14px system-ui, sans-serif';
   725	      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   726	      ctx.fillText('NEXT ARENA LOADING', width / 2, boardY + boardH + 30);
   727	      ctx.restore();
   728	    }
   729	  }
   730	
   731	  function draw() {
   732	    drawBackground();
   733	    drawHeader();
   734	    drawArena();
   735	    drawBanner();
   736	    drawStateMessage();
   737	  }
   738	
   739	  function frame(now) {
   740	    const dt = (now - lastTime) / 1000;
   741	    lastTime = now;
   742	    update(dt);
   743	    draw();
   744	    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   745	  }
   746	
   747	  function openScreen() {
   748	    screenTitle.textContent = 'RUN ENDED';
   749	    screenCopy.textContent = 'Score ' + score + ' · Best ' + best + ' · Arena ' + level;
   750	    screen.hidden = false;
   751	  }
   752	
   753	  function ensureAudio() {
   754	    if (!audio) {
   755	      try { audio = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)(); } catch (_) { audio = null; }
   756	    }
   757	    if (audio && audio.state === 'suspended') audio.resume().catch(() => {});
   758	  }
   759	
   760	  function beep(frequency, duration, type) {
   761	    if (!audio) return;
   762	    try {
   763	      const oscillator = audio.createOscillator();
   764	      const gain = audio.createGain();
   765	      oscillator.type = type || 'sine';
   766	      oscillator.frequency.value = frequency;
   767	      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, audio.currentTime);
   768	      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.035, audio.currentTime + 0.008);
   769	      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, audio.currentTime + duration);
   770	      oscillator.connect(gain).connect(audio.destination);
   771	      oscillator.start();
   772	      oscillator.stop(audio.currentTime + duration + 0.02);
   773	    } catch (_) {}
   774	  }
   775	
   776	  function pointFromEvent(event) {
   777	    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
   778	    const source = event.touches ? event.touches[0] || event.changedTouches[0] : event;
   779	    return { x: source.clientX - rect.left, y: source.clientY - rect.top };
   780	  }
   781	
   782	  function buttonHit(point) {
   783	    return point.x > width - 112 && point.y > height - 125;
   784	  }
   785	
   786	  function handleDown(event) {
   787	    event.preventDefault();
   788	    ensureAudio();
   789	    const point = pointFromEvent(event);
   790	    if (buttonHit(point)) { placeBomb(); return; }
   791	    input.active = true;
   792	    input.held = true;
   793	    input.dir = null;
   794	    input.x = point.x;
   795	    input.y = point.y;
   796	  }
   797	
   798	  function handleMove(event) {
   799	    if (!input.active) return;
   800	    event.preventDefault();
   801	    const point = pointFromEvent(event);
   802	    const dx = point.x - input.x;
   803	    const dy = point.y - input.y;
   804	    if (Math.abs(dx) + Math.abs(dy) < 12) return;
   805	    input.dir = Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy) ? { dx: dx > 0 ? 1 : -1, dy: 0 } : { dx: 0, dy: dy > 0 ? 1 : -1 };
   806	    input.lastRepeat = performance.now();
   807	    input.x = point.x;
   808	    input.y = point.y;
   809	    requestMove(input.dir.dx, input.dir.dy);
   810	  }
   811	
   812	  function handleUp(event) {
   813	    if (event) event.preventDefault();
   814	    input.active = false;
   815	    input.held = false;
   816	    input.dir = null;
   817	  }
   818	
   819	  function handleKey(event) {
   820	    ensureAudio();
   821	    const key = event.key.toLowerCase();
   822	    if (key === ' ' || key === 'enter') { event.preventDefault(); if (state === 'gameover') newGame(); else placeBomb(); return; }
   823	    const directions = { arrowup: [0, -1], w: [0, -1], arrowdown: [0, 1], s: [0, 1], arrowleft: [-1, 0], a: [-1, 0], arrowright: [1, 0], d: [1, 0] };
   824	    if (directions[key]) { event.preventDefault(); requestMove(directions[key][0], directions[key][1]); }
   825	  }
   826	
   827	  bombButton.addEventListener('click', (event) => { event.preventDefault(); placeBomb(); });
   828	  restart.addEventListener('click', newGame);
   829	  canvas.addEventListener('touchstart', handleDown, { passive: false });
   830	  canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', handleMove, { passive: false });
   831	  canvas.addEventListener('touchend', handleUp, { passive: false });
   832	  canvas.addEventListener('touchcancel', handleUp, { passive: false });
   833	  canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', handleDown);
   834	  canvas.addEventListener('mousemove', handleMove);
   835	  window.addEventListener('mouseup', handleUp);
   836	  window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKey);
   837	  window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
   838	  window.addEventListener('orientationchange', resize);
   839	
   840	  resize();
   841	  newGame();
   842	  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   843	})();

   152	  function spawnWave() {
   153	    waveRng = mulberry32((0xC001D00D ^ Math.imul(wave, 0x45D9F3B)) >>> 0);
   154	    const count = Math.min(20, 4 + wave + Math.floor(wave * .55));
   155	    const types = ['rusher', 'orbiter', 'spitter', 'shielder', 'splitter'];

   157	      const type = types[(i + wave * 2 + Math.floor(i / 3)) % types.length];
   158	      const p = spawnPoint();

   160	    }
   161	    if (wave % 5 === 0) {
   162	      const p = spawnPoint(true);
   163	      enemies.push(makeEnemy('boss', p.x, p.y));
   164	      banner = `SCUZZ // WAVE ${wave}`;
   165	      bannerTimer = 2.4;
   166	    } else {
   167	      banner = `WAVE ${wave}`;
   168	      bannerTimer = 1.6;
   169	    }
   170	    if (wave > 1 && wave % 2 === 0) {
   171	      pickups.push({ x: randomRange(62, 328), y: randomRange(170, 545), r: 14, type: ['spread', 'beam', 'bounce'][((wave / 2) - 1) % 3], spin: 0, pulse: 0 });
   172	    }
   173	  }
   174	
   175	  function spawnPoint(boss = false) {
   176	    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 24; attempt++) {
   177	      const edge = Math.floor(waveRng() * 4);
   178	      const inset = boss ? 50 : 33;
   179	      let x = edge === 0 ? ARENA.left + inset : edge === 1 ? ARENA.right - inset : randomRange(45, 345);
   180	      let y = edge === 2 ? ARENA.top + inset : edge === 3 ? ARENA.bottom - inset : randomRange(115, 615);
   181	      if (dist(x, y, player.x, player.y) > 125 && !insidePillar(x, y, boss ? 40 : 19) && dist(x, y, hazard.x, hazard.y) > hazard.r + 35) return { x, y };
   182	    }
   183	    return { x: 45 + waveRng() * 300, y: 120 + waveRng() * 470 };
   184	  }
   185	
   186	  function makeEnemy(type, x, y, mini = false) {
   187	    const scale = mini ? .65 : 1;
   188	    const hpBase = { rusher: 22, orbiter: 30, spitter: 25, shielder: 42, splitter: 36, boss: 245 }[type] || 18;
   189	    return {
   190	      id: ++enemyId,
   191	      type,
   192	      mini,
   193	      x, y,
   194	      r: (type === 'boss' ? 36 : type === 'shielder' ? 18 : 15) * scale,
   195	      hp: (hpBase + wave * (type === 'boss' ? 6 : 1.8)) * scale,
   196	      maxHp: (hpBase + wave * (type === 'boss' ? 6 : 1.8)) * scale,
   197	      speed: (type === 'rusher' ? 67 : type === 'orbiter' ? 75 : type === 'spitter' ? 54 : type === 'shielder' ? 38 : type === 'splitter' ? 48 : 40) * (mini ? 1.3 : 1),
   198	      cool: randomRange(.55, 1.7),
   199	      phase: randomRange(0, Math.PI * 2),
   200	      orbitRadius: randomRange(112, 155),
   201	      facing: 0,
   202	      hit: 0,
   203	      dead: false,
   204	      bossPatternTimer: 1.4,
   205	      bossPattern: 0,
   206	      charge: 0,
   207	      chargeX: 0,
   208	      chargeY: 0
   209	    };
   210	  }
   211	
   212	  function insidePillar(x, y, radius = 0) {
   213	    return pillars.some(p => dist(x, y, p.x, p.y) < p.r + radius);
   214	  }
   215	
   216	  function resolvePillars(body) {
   217	    for (const p of pillars) {
   218	      const dx = body.x - p.x;
   219	      const dy = body.y - p.y;
   220	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || .001;
   221	      const min = body.r + p.r;
   222	      if (d < min) {
   223	        body.x = p.x + dx / d * min;
   224	        body.y = p.y + dy / d * min;
   225	      }
   226	    }
   227	  }
   228	
   229	  function moveBody(body, vx, vy, dt) {
   230	    body.x += vx * dt;
   231	    body.y += vy * dt;
   232	    body.x = clamp(body.x, ARENA.left + body.r, ARENA.right - body.r);
   233	    body.y = clamp(body.y, ARENA.top + body.r, ARENA.bottom - body.r);
   234	    resolvePillars(body);
   235	  }
   236	
   237	  function getAim() {
   238	    let ax = 0;
   239	    let ay = 0;
   240	    if (sticks.right.active && sticks.right.mag > .12) {
   241	      ax = sticks.right.dx;
   242	      ay = sticks.right.dy;
   243	    } else {
   244	      if (keys.ArrowLeft) ax -= 1;
   245	      if (keys.ArrowRight) ax += 1;
   246	      if (keys.ArrowUp) ay -= 1;
   247	      if (keys.ArrowDown) ay += 1;
   248	    }
   249	    const mag = Math.hypot(ax, ay);
   250	    if (mag > .1) return { x: ax / mag, y: ay / mag, mag };
   251	    return { x: player.aimX, y: player.aimY, mag: 0 };
   252	  }
   253	
   254	  function getMove() {
   255	    let mx = sticks.left.active ? sticks.left.dx : 0;
   256	    let my = sticks.left.active ? sticks.left.dy : 0;
   257	    if (!sticks.left.active) {
   258	      if (keys.KeyA) mx -= 1;
   259	      if (keys.KeyD) mx += 1;
   260	      if (keys.KeyW) my -= 1;
   261	      if (keys.KeyS) my += 1;
   262	    }
   263	    const mag = Math.hypot(mx, my);
   264	    if (mag > 1) return { x: mx / mag, y: my / mag, mag: 1 };
   265	    return { x: mx, y: my, mag };
   266	  }
   267	
   268	  function update(dt) {
   269	    elapsed += dt;
   270	    flash = Math.max(0, flash - dt * 3.2);
   271	    shake = Math.max(0, shake - dt * 2.8);
   272	    bannerTimer = Math.max(0, bannerTimer - dt);
   273	    hintTimer = Math.max(0, hintTimer - dt);
   274	    ui.hint.style.opacity = hintTimer > 0 ? String(Math.min(1, hintTimer)) : '0';
   275	    if (state !== 'playing') {
   276	      updateParticles(dt);
   277	      updateHud();
   278	      return;
   279	    }
   280	
   281	    if (waveTimer > 0) {
   282	      waveTimer -= dt;
   283	      updatePlayer(dt);
   284	      updateParticles(dt);
   285	      updatePickups(dt);
   286	      if (waveTimer <= 0) {
   287	        if (wave >= 15) finishRun(true);
   288	        else { wave++; spawnWave(); }
   289	      }
   290	      updateHud();
   291	      return;
   292	    }
   293	
   294	    updatePlayer(dt);
   295	    updateEnemies(dt);
   296	    updateBullets(dt);
   297	    updateEnemyBullets(dt);
   298	    updateBeams(dt);
   299	    updateParticles(dt);
   300	    updatePickups(dt);
   301	
   302	    if (enemies.length && enemies.every(enemy => enemy.dead)) {
   303	      enemies = enemies.filter(enemy => !enemy.dead);
   304	      waveTimer = 1.45;
   305	      score += wave * 100;
   306	      burst(player.x, player.y, '#ffe28c', 20, 1.2);
   307	      shake = .55;
   308	      if (wave >= 15) banner = 'SCUZZ DOWN // RUN COMPLETE';
   309	      else banner = 'WAVE CLEAR';
   310	      bannerTimer = 1.4;
   311	      best = Math.max(best, score);
   312	      saveBest();
   313	    }
   314	    updateHud();
   315	  }
   316	
   317	  function updatePlayer(dt) {
   318	    player.hurtTimer = Math.max(0, player.hurtTimer - dt);
   319	    player.fireTimer -= dt;
   320	    player.weaponTime = Math.max(0, player.weaponTime - dt);
   321	    if (player.weaponTime === 0) player.weapon = 'spread';
   322	    const move = getMove();
   323	    const speed = player.speed * (dist(player.x, player.y, hazard.x, hazard.y) < hazard.r ? .58 : 1);
   324	    moveBody(player, move.x * speed * move.mag, move.y * speed * move.mag, dt);
   325	    const aim = getAim();
   326	    if (aim.mag > 0) { player.aimX = aim.x; player.aimY = aim.y; }
   327	    const shouldFire = (sticks.right.active && sticks.right.mag > .12) || keys.Space || keys.Enter;
   328	    if (shouldFire && player.fireTimer <= 0) firePlayer();
   329	    player.trailTimer -= dt;
   330	    if (move.mag > .1 && player.trailTimer <= 0) {
   331	      player.trailTimer = .06;
   332	      addParticle(player.x - move.x * 9, player.y - move.y * 9, '#71dce9', 1.5, 0, 0, .34);
   333	    }
   334	    if (dist(player.x, player.y, hazard.x, hazard.y) < hazard.r - 5 && player.hurtTimer <= 0) hurtPlayer(5 * dt);
   335	  }
   336	
   337	  function firePlayer() {
   338	    const a = Math.atan2(player.aimY, player.aimX);
   339	    player.fireTimer = player.weapon === 'beam' ? .11 : player.weapon === 'bounce' ? .2 : .22;
   340	    if (player.weapon === 'beam') {
   341	      fireBeam(a);
   342	      burst(player.x + player.aimX * 14, player.y + player.aimY * 14, '#c5f7ff', 3, .4);
   343	      return;
   344	    }
   345	    const angles = player.weapon === 'spread' ? [a - .22, a, a + .22] : [a];
   346	    for (const angle of angles) {
   347	      const speed = player.weapon === 'bounce' ? 295 : 330;
   348	      bullets.push({ x: player.x + Math.cos(angle) * 16, y: player.y + Math.sin(angle) * 16, vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed, vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed, r: 4, damage: player.weapon === 'bounce' ? 17 : 9, life: 1.4, bounces: player.weapon === 'bounce' ? 3 : 0, color: player.weapon === 'bounce' ? '#ffcb73' : '#b7fbff' });
   349	    }
   350	    burst(player.x + player.aimX * 14, player.y + player.aimY * 14, player.weapon === 'bounce' ? '#ffcb73' : '#b7fbff', 4, .6);
   351	  }
   352	
   353	  function fireBeam(angle) {
   354	    const range = 320;
   355	    beams.push({ x: player.x, y: player.y, angle, range, life: .12 });
   356	    for (const enemy of enemies) {
   357	      if (enemy.dead) continue;
   358	      const dx = enemy.x - player.x;
   359	      const dy = enemy.y - player.y;
   360	      const along = dx * Math.cos(angle) + dy * Math.sin(angle);
   361	      const across = Math.abs(dx * Math.sin(angle) - dy * Math.cos(angle));
   362	      if (along > 0 && along < range && across < enemy.r + 7) damageEnemy(enemy, 10, angle);
   363	    }
   364	  }
   365	
   366	  function updateEnemies(dt) {
   367	    for (const enemy of enemies) {
   368	      if (enemy.dead) continue;
   369	      enemy.cool -= dt;
   370	      enemy.hit = Math.max(0, enemy.hit - dt * 4);
   371	      enemy.phase += dt;
   372	      const dx = player.x - enemy.x;
   373	      const dy = player.y - enemy.y;
   374	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
   375	      const toPlayerX = dx / d;
   376	      const toPlayerY = dy / d;
   377	      enemy.facing = Math.atan2(dy, dx);
   378	      if (enemy.type === 'boss') updateBoss(enemy, dt, toPlayerX, toPlayerY, d);
   379	      else if (enemy.type === 'rusher' || enemy.type === 'splitter' || enemy.mini) {
   380	        moveBody(enemy, toPlayerX * enemy.speed, toPlayerY * enemy.speed, dt);
   381	        if (d < enemy.r + player.r + 3 && enemy.cool <= 0) { hurtPlayer(enemy.mini ? 10 : 13); enemy.cool = .7; }
   382	      } else if (enemy.type === 'orbiter') {
   383	        const orbit = enemy.phase * .9 + enemy.id;
   384	        const targetX = player.x - toPlayerX * enemy.orbitRadius + Math.cos(orbit) * enemy.orbitRadius * .7;
   385	        const targetY = player.y - toPlayerY * enemy.orbitRadius + Math.sin(orbit) * enemy.orbitRadius * .7;
   386	        const tx = targetX - enemy.x;
   387	        const ty = targetY - enemy.y;
   388	        const td = Math.hypot(tx, ty) || 1;
   389	        moveBody(enemy, tx / td * enemy.speed, ty / td * enemy.speed, dt);
   390	        if (enemy.cool <= 0) { enemyShot(enemy, toPlayerX, toPlayerY, 125, 10, '#db9aff'); enemy.cool = 1.9; }
   391	      } else if (enemy.type === 'spitter') {
   392	        const move = d < 165 ? -1 : d > 225 ? 1 : 0;
   393	        const side = Math.sin(enemy.phase * .8 + enemy.id) * .35;
   394	        moveBody(enemy, (toPlayerX * move - toPlayerY * side) * enemy.speed, (toPlayerY * move + toPlayerX * side) * enemy.speed, dt);
   395	        if (enemy.cool <= 0) { lobbedShot(enemy); enemy.cool = 2.25; }
   396	      } else if (enemy.type === 'shielder') {
   397	        const move = d > 130 ? 1 : -0.35;
   398	        moveBody(enemy, toPlayerX * enemy.speed * move, toPlayerY * enemy.speed * move, dt);
   399	        if (enemy.cool <= 0) { enemyShot(enemy, toPlayerX, toPlayerY, 145, 12, '#ff9c67'); enemy.cool = 3.1; }
   400	        if (d < enemy.r + player.r + 2) hurtPlayer(7 * dt);
   401	      }
   402	      if (dist(enemy.x, enemy.y, hazard.x, hazard.y) < hazard.r - enemy.r * .2) enemy.cool -= dt * .25;
   403	    }
   404	  }
   405	
   406	  function updateBoss(enemy, dt, tx, ty, d) {
   407	    if (enemy.charge > 0) {
   408	      enemy.charge -= dt;
   409	      moveBody(enemy, enemy.chargeX * 270, enemy.chargeY * 270, dt);
   410	      if (dist(enemy.x, enemy.y, player.x, player.y) < enemy.r + player.r + 6) hurtPlayer(25);
   411	      return;
   412	    }
   413	    const orbit = Math.sin(enemy.phase * .55) * 0.5;
   414	    moveBody(enemy, (tx - ty * orbit) * enemy.speed, (ty + tx * orbit) * enemy.speed, dt);
   415	    enemy.bossPatternTimer -= dt;
   416	    if (enemy.bossPatternTimer <= 0) {
   417	      enemy.bossPattern = (enemy.bossPattern + 1) % 3;
   418	      if (enemy.bossPattern === 0) {
   419	        enemy.charge = .82;
   420	        enemy.chargeX = tx;
   421	        enemy.chargeY = ty;
   422	        burst(enemy.x, enemy.y, '#ff718a', 12, 1.2);
   423	      } else if (enemy.bossPattern === 1) {
   424	        for (let i = 0; i < 13; i++) {
   425	          const a = i / 13 * Math.PI * 2 + enemy.phase * .3;
   426	          enemyShot(enemy, Math.cos(a), Math.sin(a), 128, 10, '#ff7097');
   427	        }
   428	        burst(enemy.x, enemy.y, '#ff7097', 22, 1.4);
   429	      } else if (enemies.filter(e => !e.dead).length < 31) {
   430	        for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
   431	          const a = i * Math.PI + enemy.phase;
   432	          enemies.push(makeEnemy(i ? 'orbiter' : 'rusher', enemy.x + Math.cos(a) * 47, enemy.y + Math.sin(a) * 47));
   433	        }
   434	        burst(enemy.x, enemy.y, '#c59bff', 14, 1.1);
   435	      }
   436	      enemy.bossPatternTimer = 2.65;
   437	    }
   438	    if (d < enemy.r + player.r + 4) hurtPlayer(18 * dt);
   439	  }
   440	
   441	  function enemyShot(enemy, nx, ny, speed, damage, color) {
   442	    enemyBullets.push({ x: enemy.x + nx * (enemy.r + 6), y: enemy.y + ny * (enemy.r + 6), vx: nx * speed, vy: ny * speed, r: 5, damage, life: 3.2, color, lob: 0 });
   443	  }
   444	
   445	  function lobbedShot(enemy) {
   446	    const dx = player.x - enemy.x;
   447	    const dy = player.y - enemy.y;
   448	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
   449	    enemyBullets.push({ x: enemy.x, y: enemy.y, vx: dx / d * 105, vy: dy / d * 105, r: 7, damage: 17, life: 2.15, lob: 1.1, lobMax: 2.15, color: '#f4c15e' });
   450	    burst(enemy.x, enemy.y, '#f4c15e', 5, .7);
   451	  }
   452	
   453	  function updateBullets(dt) {
   454	    for (let i = bullets.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   455	      const bullet = bullets[i];
   456	      bullet.life -= dt;
   457	      bullet.x += bullet.vx * dt;
   458	      bullet.y += bullet.vy * dt;
   459	      let bounced = false;
   460	      if (bullet.x < ARENA.left + bullet.r || bullet.x > ARENA.right - bullet.r) {
   461	        if (bullet.bounces > 0) { bullet.vx *= -1; bullet.bounces--; bounced = true; }
   462	        else bullet.life = 0;
   463	        bullet.x = clamp(bullet.x, ARENA.left + bullet.r, ARENA.right - bullet.r);
   464	      }
   465	      if (bullet.y < ARENA.top + bullet.r || bullet.y > ARENA.bottom - bullet.r) {
   466	        if (bullet.bounces > 0) { bullet.vy *= -1; bullet.bounces--; bounced = true; }
   467	        else bullet.life = 0;
   468	        bullet.y = clamp(bullet.y, ARENA.top + bullet.r, ARENA.bottom - bullet.r);
   469	      }
   470	      if (insidePillar(bullet.x, bullet.y, bullet.r)) {
   471	        if (bullet.bounces > 0) { bullet.vx *= -1; bullet.vy *= -1; bullet.bounces--; bounced = true; }
   472	        else bullet.life = 0;
   473	      }
   474	      if (bounced) burst(bullet.x, bullet.y, '#ffe3a0', 3, .5);
   475	      for (const enemy of enemies) {
   476	        if (enemy.dead || bullet.life <= 0) continue;
   477	        if (dist(bullet.x, bullet.y, enemy.x, enemy.y) < bullet.r + enemy.r) {
   478	          damageEnemy(enemy, bullet.damage, Math.atan2(bullet.vy, bullet.vx));
   479	          if (bullet.bounces > 0 && !enemy.dead) { bullet.vx *= -1; bullet.vy *= -1; bullet.bounces--; bullet.x += bullet.vx * .02; }
   480	          else bullet.life = 0;
   481	        }
   482	      }
   483	      if (bullet.life <= 0) bullets.splice(i, 1);
   484	    }
   485	  }
   486	
   487	  function updateEnemyBullets(dt) {
   488	    for (let i = enemyBullets.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   489	      const bullet = enemyBullets[i];
   490	      bullet.life -= dt;
   491	      bullet.x += bullet.vx * dt;
   492	      bullet.y += bullet.vy * dt;
   493	      if (bullet.lob) bullet.lob = Math.max(0, bullet.lob - dt);
   494	      if (bullet.life <= 0 || bullet.x < 10 || bullet.x > 380 || bullet.y < 72 || bullet.y > 668 || insidePillar(bullet.x, bullet.y, bullet.r)) {
   495	        enemyBullets.splice(i, 1);
   496	        continue;
   497	      }
   498	      const visualR = bullet.r + (bullet.lob ? Math.sin((1 - bullet.lob / 1.1) * Math.PI) * 8 : 0);
   499	      if (dist(bullet.x, bullet.y, player.x, player.y) < visualR + player.r) {
   500	        hurtPlayer(bullet.damage);
   501	        enemyBullets.splice(i, 1);
   502	      }
   503	    }
   504	  }
   505	
   506	  function updateBeams(dt) {
   507	    for (let i = beams.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   508	      beams[i].life -= dt;
   509	      if (beams[i].life <= 0) beams.splice(i, 1);
   510	    }
   511	  }
   512	
   513	  function updatePickups(dt) {
   514	    for (let i = pickups.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   515	      const p = pickups[i];
   516	      p.spin += dt * 2.5;
   517	      p.pulse += dt * 4;
   518	      if (dist(p.x, p.y, player.x, player.y) < p.r + player.r + 7) {
   519	        player.weapon = p.type;
   520	        player.weaponTime = 13;
   521	        pickups.splice(i, 1);
   522	        banner = `${p.type.toUpperCase()} ONLINE`;
   523	        bannerTimer = 1.1;
   524	        burst(p.x, p.y, weaponColor(p.type), 16, 1.2);
   525	      }
   526	    }
   527	  }
   528	
   529	  function hurtPlayer(amount) {
   530	    if (state !== 'playing' || player.hurtTimer > 0) return;
   531	    player.hp -= amount;
   532	    player.hurtTimer = .28;
   533	    flash = 1;
   534	    shake = Math.min(1.3, shake + .25);
   535	    burst(player.x, player.y, '#ff7582', 9, .9);
   536	    if (player.hp <= 0) finishRun(false);
   537	  }
   538	
   539	  function damageEnemy(enemy, amount, incomingAngle) {
   540	    if (enemy.dead) return;
   541	    if (enemy.type === 'shielder') {
   542	      const front = Math.abs(angleDiff(incomingAngle, enemy.facing + Math.PI)) < 1.05;
   543	      if (front) {
   544	        enemy.hit = .25;
   545	        burst(enemy.x + Math.cos(enemy.facing) * enemy.r, enemy.y + Math.sin(enemy.facing) * enemy.r, '#9ee8ff', 3, .6);
   546	        return;
   547	      }
   548	    }
   549	    if (enemy.type === 'boss') {
   550	      const weakBack = Math.abs(angleDiff(incomingAngle, enemy.facing + Math.PI)) < .88;
   551	      amount *= weakBack ? 2.4 : .45;
   552	      if (weakBack) burst(enemy.x - Math.cos(enemy.facing) * 24, enemy.y - Math.sin(enemy.facing) * 24, '#ffe77a', 4, .8);
   553	    }
   554	    enemy.hp -= amount;
   555	    enemy.hit = .18;
   556	    addParticle(enemy.x, enemy.y, enemy.type === 'boss' ? '#ffe77a' : '#c9f8ff', 3, 0, 0, .45);
   557	    if (enemy.hp <= 0) killEnemy(enemy);
   558	  }
   559	
   560	  function killEnemy(enemy) {
   561	    if (enemy.dead) return;
   562	    enemy.dead = true;
   563	    kills++;
   564	    score += 25 + wave * 4;
   565	    best = Math.max(best, score);
   566	    if (enemy.type === 'splitter' && !enemy.mini) {
   567	      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) enemies.push(makeEnemy('rusher', enemy.x + (i ? 12 : -12), enemy.y + (i ? -8 : 8), true));
   568	    }
   569	    burst(enemy.x, enemy.y, enemyColor(enemy.type), enemy.type === 'boss' ? 42 : 13, enemy.type === 'boss' ? 2.3 : 1);
   570	    shake = Math.min(1.1, shake + (enemy.type === 'boss' ? .75 : .08));
   571	    flash = Math.max(flash, enemy.type === 'boss' ? .7 : .08);
   572	  }
   573	
   574	  function finishRun(win) {
   575	    if (state !== 'playing') return;
   576	    state = win ? 'win' : 'dead';
   577	    best = Math.max(best, score);
   578	    saveBest();
   579	    if (win) {
   580	      burst(player.x, player.y, '#ffe487', 55, 3);
   581	      ui.resultTitle.textContent = 'ARENA CLEARED';
   582	      ui.resultText.textContent = `Three Scuzz falls. SCORE ${score} • BEST ${best}`;
   583	    } else {
   584	      ui.resultTitle.textContent = 'RUN OVER';
   585	      ui.resultText.textContent = `WAVE ${wave} • ${kills} KILLS • SCORE ${score}`;
   586	    }
   587	    ui.result.hidden = false;
   588	    updateHud();
   589	  }
   590	
   591	  function weaponColor(type) { return type === 'beam' ? '#b9f7ff' : type === 'bounce' ? '#ffcf75' : '#b5f4ff'; }
   592	  function enemyColor(type) { return { rusher: '#ff7183', orbiter: '#c58eff', spitter: '#f1bf5b', shielder: '#62d9dc', splitter: '#ff9c67', boss: '#ff799a' }[type] || '#fff'; }
   593	
   594	  function addParticle(x, y, color, size, vx, vy, life = .7) {
   595	    if (particles.length > 280) particles.shift();
   596	    particles.push({ x, y, color, size, vx, vy, life, maxLife: life, gravity: 0 });
   597	  }
   598	
   599	  function burst(x, y, color, count, power = 1) {
   600	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   601	      const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
   602	      const s = (20 + Math.random() * 80) * power;
   603	      addParticle(x, y, color, 1.5 + Math.random() * 3, Math.cos(a) * s, Math.sin(a) * s, .25 + Math.random() * .65);
   604	    }
   605	  }
   606	
   607	  function updateParticles(dt) {
   608	    for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   609	      const p = particles[i];
   610	      p.life -= dt;
   611	      p.x += p.vx * dt;
   612	      p.y += p.vy * dt;
   613	      p.vx *= .97;
   614	      p.vy *= .97;
   615	      if (p.life <= 0) particles.splice(i, 1);
   616	    }
   617	  }
   618	
   619	  function render() {
   620	    const w = canvas.width / dpr;
   621	    const h = canvas.height / dpr;
   622	    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
   623	    ctx.fillStyle = '#070b16';
   624	    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
   625	    ctx.save();
   626	    ctx.translate(viewX, viewY);
   627	    ctx.scale(viewScale, viewScale);
   628	    ctx.save();
   629	    ctx.translate((Math.random() - .5) * shake * 8, (Math.random() - .5) * shake * 8);
   630	    drawArena();
   631	    for (const p of pickups) drawPickup(p);
   632	    for (const b of bullets) drawBullet(b);
   633	    for (const b of enemyBullets) drawEnemyBullet(b);
   634	    for (const enemy of enemies) if (!enemy.dead) drawEnemy(enemy);
   635	    drawPlayer();
   636	    for (const beam of beams) drawBeam(beam);
   637	    drawParticles();
   638	    drawSticks();
   639	    if (bannerTimer > 0) drawBanner();
   640	    ctx.restore();
   641	    ctx.restore();
   642	    if (flash > 0) {
   643	      ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
   644	      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255, 92, 119, ${flash * .12})`;
   645	      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
   646	    }
   647	  }
   648	
   649	  function drawArena() {
   650	    const bg = ctx.createRadialGradient(195, 350, 30, 195, 350, 360);
   651	    bg.addColorStop(0, '#16263a');
   652	    bg.addColorStop(.65, '#0c1728');
   653	    bg.addColorStop(1, '#070b16');
   654	    ctx.fillStyle = bg;
   655	    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, VW, VH);
   656	    ctx.save();
   657	    ctx.beginPath();
   658	    ctx.roundRect(ARENA.left, ARENA.top, ARENA.right - ARENA.left, ARENA.bottom - ARENA.top, 26);
   659	    ctx.clip();
   660	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff08';
   661	    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
   662	    for (let x = ARENA.left; x <= ARENA.right; x += 26) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, ARENA.top); ctx.lineTo(x, ARENA.bottom); ctx.stroke(); }
   663	    for (let y = ARENA.top; y <= ARENA.bottom; y += 26) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ARENA.left, y); ctx.lineTo(ARENA.right, y); ctx.stroke(); }
   664	    const pulse = 1 + Math.sin(elapsed * 2.2) * .04;
   665	    ctx.fillStyle = '#15253a';
   666	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(hazard.x, hazard.y, hazard.r * pulse, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   667	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#5b70d8aa'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke();
   668	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#a3aaff33'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
   669	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(hazard.x, hazard.y, hazard.r * .72, elapsed, elapsed + Math.PI * 1.55); ctx.stroke();
   670	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(hazard.x, hazard.y, hazard.r * .9, elapsed + Math.PI, elapsed + Math.PI * 2.55); ctx.stroke();
   671	    for (const p of pillars) drawPillar(p);
   672	    ctx.restore();
   673	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#6a99ae66';
   674	    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   675	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(ARENA.left, ARENA.top, ARENA.right - ARENA.left, ARENA.bottom - ARENA.top, 26); ctx.stroke();
   676	    ctx.fillStyle = '#8298b522';
   677	    ctx.font = '700 8px system-ui';
   678	    ctx.letterSpacing = '2px';
   679	    ctx.fillText('DANGER POOL', hazard.x - 30, hazard.y + 3);
   680	  }
   681	
   682	  function drawPillar(p) {
   683	    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(p.x - p.r * .35, p.y - p.r * .4, 2, p.x, p.y, p.r * 1.2);
   684	    g.addColorStop(0, '#355064'); g.addColorStop(1, '#182535');
   685	    ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   686	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#80b8c655'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke();
   687	    ctx.fillStyle = '#b6e5ee44'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x - p.r * .35, p.y - p.r * .4, p.r * .2, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   688	  }
   689	
   690	  function drawPlayer() {
   691	    const a = Math.atan2(player.aimY, player.aimX);
   692	    ctx.save();
   693	    ctx.translate(player.x, player.y);
   694	    ctx.rotate(a);
   695	    if (player.hurtTimer > 0 && Math.floor(elapsed * 20) % 2 === 0) ctx.globalAlpha = .45;
   696	    ctx.shadowColor = '#5cf3ff'; ctx.shadowBlur = 14;
   697	    ctx.fillStyle = '#74e6ee'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, player.r, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   698	    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   699	    ctx.fillStyle = '#d9ffff'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(18, 0); ctx.lineTo(5, -7); ctx.lineTo(5, 7); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
   700	    ctx.fillStyle = '#1b3b58'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-3, 0, 6, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   701	    ctx.restore();
   702	  }
   703	
   704	  function drawEnemy(enemy) {
   705	    const color = enemyColor(enemy.type);
   706	    ctx.save();
   707	    ctx.translate(enemy.x, enemy.y);
   708	    ctx.rotate(enemy.facing);
   709	    ctx.globalAlpha = enemy.hit > 0 ? .6 + enemy.hit * 2 : 1;
   710	    ctx.shadowColor = color; ctx.shadowBlur = enemy.type === 'boss' ? 18 : 9;
   711	    ctx.fillStyle = color;
   712	    if (enemy.type === 'rusher' || enemy.mini) {
   713	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(enemy.r, 0); ctx.lineTo(-enemy.r * .7, -enemy.r * .72); ctx.lineTo(-enemy.r * .7, enemy.r * .72); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
   714	    } else if (enemy.type === 'orbiter') {
   715	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, enemy.r, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   716	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#f3d9ff'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, enemy.r * .6, enemy.phase, enemy.phase + Math.PI * 1.4); ctx.stroke();
   717	    } else if (enemy.type === 'spitter') {
   718	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(enemy.r, 0); ctx.lineTo(0, enemy.r); ctx.lineTo(-enemy.r, 0); ctx.lineTo(0, -enemy.r); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
   719	      ctx.fillStyle = '#fff3c6'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(enemy.r * .35, 0, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   720	    } else if (enemy.type === 'shielder') {
   721	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, enemy.r, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   722	      ctx.fillStyle = '#1d3c59'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, enemy.r * .48, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   723	      ctx.fillStyle = enemy.hit > 0 ? '#d5fbff' : '#9beeff'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(enemy.r * .72, 0, enemy.r * .62, -1.05, 1.05); ctx.lineTo(0, 0); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
   724	    } else if (enemy.type === 'splitter') {
   725	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, enemy.r, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   726	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffe0b0'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-8, -8); ctx.lineTo(8, 8); ctx.moveTo(8, -8); ctx.lineTo(-8, 8); ctx.stroke();
   727	    } else if (enemy.type === 'boss') {
   728	      ctx.fillStyle = '#7b3f71'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, enemy.r, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   729	      ctx.fillStyle = '#c95180'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, enemy.r * .68, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   730	      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffe27a'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-enemy.r * .63, 0, 9 + Math.sin(elapsed * 5) * 1.5, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   731	      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff829b'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(enemy.r * .35, -enemy.r * .26, 5, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.arc(enemy.r * .35, enemy.r * .26, 5, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   732	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffef9e'; ctx.lineWidth = 4; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, enemy.r + 5, Math.PI - .65, Math.PI + .65); ctx.stroke();
   733	    }
   734	    ctx.restore();
   735	    if (enemy.type === 'boss' || enemy.hp < enemy.maxHp) {
   736	      const width = enemy.type === 'boss' ? 76 : 28;
   737	      ctx.fillStyle = '#101728'; ctx.fillRect(enemy.x - width / 2, enemy.y - enemy.r - 10, width, 3);
   738	      ctx.fillStyle = enemy.type === 'boss' ? '#ff8e9d' : '#b6e8c6'; ctx.fillRect(enemy.x - width / 2, enemy.y - enemy.r - 10, width * clamp(enemy.hp / enemy.maxHp, 0, 1), 3);
   739	    }
   740	  }
   741	
   742	  function drawBullet(b) {
   743	    ctx.save(); ctx.shadowColor = b.color; ctx.shadowBlur = 9; ctx.fillStyle = b.color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   744	  }
   745	
   746	  function drawEnemyBullet(b) {
   747	    const r = b.r + (b.lob ? Math.sin((1 - b.lob / 1.1) * Math.PI) * 8 : 0);
   748	    ctx.save(); ctx.shadowColor = b.color; ctx.shadowBlur = 10; ctx.fillStyle = b.color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, r, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = '#fff5ceaa'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(b.x - r * .3, b.y - r * .3, r * .25, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   749	  }
   750	
   751	  function drawBeam(beam) {
   752	    const endX = beam.x + Math.cos(beam.angle) * beam.range;
   753	    const endY = beam.y + Math.sin(beam.angle) * beam.range;
   754	    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(beam.life / .12, 0, 1); ctx.lineCap = 'round';
   755	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#73e8ff55'; ctx.lineWidth = 13; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(beam.x, beam.y); ctx.lineTo(endX, endY); ctx.stroke();
   756	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#d9ffff'; ctx.lineWidth = 4; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(beam.x, beam.y); ctx.lineTo(endX, endY); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore();
   757	  }
   758	
   759	  function drawPickup(p) {
   760	    const color = weaponColor(p.type);
   761	    const pulse = Math.sin(p.pulse) * 2;
   762	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(p.x, p.y); ctx.rotate(p.spin); ctx.shadowColor = color; ctx.shadowBlur = 16;
   763	    ctx.strokeStyle = color; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, p.r + pulse, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();
   764	    ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, -9); ctx.lineTo(8, 0); ctx.lineTo(0, 9); ctx.lineTo(-8, 0); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
   765	    ctx.fillStyle = '#193149'; ctx.font = '900 8px system-ui'; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle'; ctx.fillText(p.type === 'beam' ? 'B' : p.type === 'bounce' ? '↗' : 'S', 0, 0);
   766	    ctx.restore();
   767	  }
   768	
   769	  function drawParticles() {
   770	    for (const p of particles) {
   771	      ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(p.life / p.maxLife, 0, 1);
   772	      ctx.fillStyle = p.color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.size * (.4 + p.life / p.maxLife), 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   773	    }
   774	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   775	  }
   776	
   777	  function drawSticks() {
   778	    drawStick(sticks.left, '#7ee9ec');
   779	    drawStick(sticks.right, '#ffcc84');
   780	  }
   781	
   782	  function drawStick(stick, color) {
   783	    const alpha = stick.active ? .46 : .17;
   784	    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = alpha; ctx.strokeStyle = color; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.fillStyle = color;
   785	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(stick.baseX, stick.baseY, 39, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();
   786	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(stick.baseX, stick.baseY, 22, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   787	    ctx.globalAlpha = stick.active ? .75 : .27;
   788	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(stick.x, stick.y, 16, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
   789	    ctx.restore();
   790	  }
   791	
   792	  function drawBanner() {
   793	    const alpha = bannerTimer < .35 ? bannerTimer / .35 : 1;
   794	    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = alpha; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.font = '900 19px system-ui'; ctx.fillStyle = '#f1fbff'; ctx.shadowColor = '#6be7ff'; ctx.shadowBlur = 16; ctx.fillText(banner, VW / 2, 145); ctx.restore();
   795	  }
   796	
   797	  function updateHud() {
   798	    ui.wave.textContent = `WAVE ${Math.min(wave, 15)} / 15`;
   799	    ui.score.textContent = `SCORE ${score}`;
   800	    ui.best.textContent = `BEST ${best}`;
   801	    ui.health.style.width = `${clamp(player ? player.hp / player.maxHp * 100 : 100, 0, 100)}%`;
   802	    ui.weapon.textContent = player && player.weapon !== 'spread' && player.weaponTime > 0 ? `${player.weapon.toUpperCase()} ${Math.ceil(player.weaponTime)}s` : 'SPREAD // READY';
   803	    const boss = enemies.find(enemy => enemy.type === 'boss' && !enemy.dead);
   804	    ui.bossbar.style.opacity = boss ? '1' : '0';
   805	    if (boss) ui.bossFill.style.width = `${clamp(boss.hp / boss.maxHp * 100, 0, 100)}%`;
   806	  }
   807	
   808	  function setStick(stick, point) {
   809	    const dx = point.x - stick.baseX;
   810	    const dy = point.y - stick.baseY;
   811	    const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
   812	    const radius = 43;
   813	    const scale = len > radius ? radius / len : 1;
   814	    stick.x = stick.baseX + dx * scale;
   815	    stick.y = stick.baseY + dy * scale;
   816	    stick.dx = dx / radius;
   817	    stick.dy = dy / radius;
   818	    stick.mag = clamp(len / radius, 0, 1);
   819	  }
   820	
   821	  function resetStick(stick) {
   822	    stick.active = false;
   823	    stick.pointer = null;
   824	    stick.x = stick.baseX;
   825	    stick.y = stick.baseY;
   826	    stick.dx = 0;
   827	    stick.dy = 0;
   828	    stick.mag = 0;
   829	  }
   830	
   831	  function onPointerDown(event) {
   832	    if (state !== 'playing') return;
   833	    const p = pointFromEvent(event);
   834	    if (p.x < 195) {
   835	      if (sticks.left.active) return;
   836	      sticks.left.active = true; sticks.left.pointer = event.pointerId; sticks.left.baseX = clamp(p.x, 57, 145); sticks.left.baseY = clamp(p.y, 150, 628); setStick(sticks.left, p);
   837	    } else {
   838	      if (sticks.right.active) return;
   839	      sticks.right.active = true; sticks.right.pointer = event.pointerId; sticks.right.baseX = clamp(p.x, 245, 333); sticks.right.baseY = clamp(p.y, 150, 628); setStick(sticks.right, p);
   840	    }
   841	    canvas.setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId);
   842	    event.preventDefault();
   843	  }
   844	
   845	  function onPointerMove(event) {
   846	    const p = pointFromEvent(event);
   847	    if (sticks.left.active && sticks.left.pointer === event.pointerId) setStick(sticks.left, p);
   848	    if (sticks.right.active && sticks.right.pointer === event.pointerId) setStick(sticks.right, p);
   849	    event.preventDefault();
   850	  }
   851	
   852	  function onPointerUp(event) {
   853	    if (sticks.left.pointer === event.pointerId) resetStick(sticks.left);
   854	    if (sticks.right.pointer === event.pointerId) resetStick(sticks.right);
   855	    event.preventDefault();
   856	  }
   857	
   858	  function onKey(event, down) {
   859	    if (['ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight', 'Space', 'Enter'].includes(event.code)) event.preventDefault();
   860	    keys[event.code] = down;
   861	    if (down && event.code === 'KeyR' && state !== 'playing') newRun();
   862	  }
   863	
   864	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown, { passive: false });
   865	  canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', onPointerMove, { passive: false });
   866	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', onPointerUp, { passive: false });
   867	  canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', onPointerUp, { passive: false });
   868	  canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', event => event.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   869	  window.addEventListener('keydown', event => onKey(event, true), { passive: false });
   870	  window.addEventListener('keyup', event => onKey(event, false), { passive: false });
   871	  window.addEventListener('resize', resize, { passive: true });
   872	  ui.restart.addEventListener('click', newRun);
   873	
   874	  function loop(now) {
   875	    const dt = Math.min(.034, Math.max(.001, (now - lastTime) / 1000));
   876	    lastTime = now;
   877	    update(dt);
   878	    render();
   879	    requestAnimationFrame(loop);
   880	  }
   881	
   882	  resize();
   883	  newRun();
   884	  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
   885	})();

    22	    #bossbar { position: absolute; left: 50%; top: 106px; width: min(245px, 62vw); height: 7px; transform: translateX(-50%); border: 1px solid #6e5a8d; border-radius: 7px; background: #1d1531; opacity: 0; transition: opacity .2s; }
    23	    #bossbar i { display: block; height: 100%; width: 100%; border-radius: 5px; background: linear-gradient(90deg, #ff7c9d, #ffb05f); }
    24	    #result { position: absolute; inset: 0; display: grid; place-items: center; background: rgba(4, 7, 17, .64); pointer-events: auto; }

    27	    .card h1 { margin: 0 0 6px; color: #aaf4ff; font-size: 25px; letter-spacing: .08em; }
    28	    .card p { margin: 0 0 18px; color: #c4d2e6; font-size: 13px; }
    29	    #restart { min-width: 150px; min-height: 52px; border: 0; border-radius: 15px; background: #8ee8ff; color: #06121d; font-size: 14px; font-weight: 900; letter-spacing: .08em; box-shadow: 0 8px 20px #34c4ef55; }
    30	    #restart:active { transform: translateY(2px); background: #c2f7ff; }
    31	    @media (min-aspect-ratio: 1/1) { #hint { top: 74px; } }
    32	  </style>

   453	  function updateBullets(dt) {
   454	    for (let i = bullets.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   455	      const bullet = bullets[i];
   456	      bullet.life -= dt;
   457	      bullet.x += bullet.vx * dt;
   458	      bullet.y += bullet.vy * dt;
   459	      let bounced = false;
   460	      if (bullet.x < ARENA.left + bullet.r || bullet.x > ARENA.right - bullet.r) {
   461	        if (bullet.bounces > 0) { bullet.vx *= -1; bullet.bounces--; bounced = true; }
   462	        else bullet.life = 0;
   463	        bullet.x = clamp(bullet.x, ARENA.left + bullet.r, ARENA.right - bullet.r);
   464	      }
   465	      if (bullet.y < ARENA.top + bullet.r || bullet.y > ARENA.bottom - bullet.r) {
   466	        if (bullet.bounces > 0) { bullet.vy *= -1; bullet.bounces--; bounced = true; }
   467	        else bullet.life = 0;
   468	        bullet.y = clamp(bullet.y, ARENA.top + bullet.r, ARENA.bottom - bullet.r);
   469	      }
   470	      if (insidePillar(bullet.x, bullet.y, bullet.r)) {
   471	        if (bullet.bounces > 0) { bullet.vx *= -1; bullet.vy *= -1; bullet.bounces--; bounced = true; }

   473	      }

   481	        }
   482	      }

   529	  function hurtPlayer(amount) {
   530	    if (state !== 'playing' || player.hurtTimer > 0) return;
   531	    player.hp -= amount;
   532	    player.hurtTimer = .28;
   533	    flash = 1;

   537	  }

   539	  function damageEnemy(enemy, amount, incomingAngle) {
   540	    if (enemy.dead) return;
   541	    if (enemy.type === 'shielder') {
   542	      const front = Math.abs(angleDiff(incomingAngle, enemy.facing + Math.PI)) < 1.05;
   543	      if (front) {
   544	        enemy.hit = .25;
   545	        burst(enemy.x + Math.cos(enemy.facing) * enemy.r, enemy.y + Math.sin(enemy.facing) * enemy.r, '#9ee8ff', 3, .6);
   546	        return;
   547	      }
   548	    }
   549	    if (enemy.type === 'boss') {
   550	      const weakBack = Math.abs(angleDiff(incomingAngle, enemy.facing + Math.PI)) < .88;
   551	      amount *= weakBack ? 2.4 : .45;
   552	      if (weakBack) burst(enemy.x - Math.cos(enemy.facing) * 24, enemy.y - Math.sin(enemy.facing) * 24, '#ffe77a', 4, .8);
   553	    }
   554	    enemy.hp -= amount;
   555	    enemy.hit = .18;
   556	    addParticle(enemy.x, enemy.y, enemy.type === 'boss' ? '#ffe77a' : '#c9f8ff', 3, 0, 0, .45);
   557	    if (enemy.hp <= 0) killEnemy(enemy);
   558	  }
   559	
   560	  function killEnemy(enemy) {
   561	    if (enemy.dead) return;
   562	    enemy.dead = true;
   563	    kills++;
   564	    score += 25 + wave * 4;
   565	    best = Math.max(best, score);
   566	    if (enemy.type === 'splitter' && !enemy.mini) {
   567	      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) enemies.push(makeEnemy('rusher', enemy.x + (i ? 12 : -12), enemy.y + (i ? -8 : 8), true));
   568	    }
   569	    burst(enemy.x, enemy.y, enemyColor(enemy.type), enemy.type === 'boss' ? 42 : 13, enemy.type === 'boss' ? 2.3 : 1);
   570	    shake = Math.min(1.1, shake + (enemy.type === 'boss' ? .75 : .08));
   571	    flash = Math.max(flash, enemy.type === 'boss' ? .7 : .08);
   572	  }

    83	    const pts = [[x, y], [x - radius, y], [x + radius, y], [x, y - radius], [x, y + radius]];

    86	  function hasLOS(a, b) {
    87	    const d = dist(a.x, a.y, b.x, b.y);
    88	    const steps = Math.ceil(d / 18);
    89	    for (let i = 1; i < steps; i++) {
    90	      const t = i / steps;
    91	      const x = lerp(a.x, b.x, t), y = lerp(a.y, b.y, t);
    92	      if (!floorPoint(x, y) || blockedDoor(x, y, 2)) return false;
    93	    }
    94	    return true;
    95	  }

   177	  function inputVector() {
   178	    let x = 0, y = 0;
   179	    if (keys.w || keys.ArrowUp) y -= 1;
