   190	    player.weapon = (player.weapon + 1) % 3;
   191	    const names = ['PISTOL', 'SPREAD', 'RIFLE'];
   192	    toastMessage(names[player.weapon]);
   193	  }
   194	  function weaponInfo() {
   195	    return [
   196	      { name: 'PISTOL', ammo: '∞', cooldown: .3, damage: 18, speed: 720, spread: 0, count: 1, color: COLORS.cyan },
   197	      { name: 'SPREAD', ammo: player.ammo.spread, cooldown: .72, damage: 10, speed: 570, spread: .24, count: 5, color: COLORS.amber },
   198	      { name: 'RIFLE', ammo: player.ammo.rifle, cooldown: .105, damage: 9, speed: 850, spread: .025, count: 1, color: COLORS.red }
   199	    ][player.weapon];
   200	  }
   201	  function fireWeapon() {
   202	    if (gameState !== 'play' || player.shootTimer > 0) return;
   203	    const weapon = weaponInfo();
   204	    if (player.weapon === 1 && player.ammo.spread <= 0) { toastMessage('NO SHELLS'); return; }
   205	    if (player.weapon === 2 && player.ammo.rifle <= 0) { toastMessage('NO ROUNDS'); return; }
   206	    const target = currentTarget();
   207	    const base = target ? Math.atan2(target.y - player.y, target.x - player.x) : player.face;
   208	    player.face = base;
   209	    for (let i = 0; i < weapon.count; i++) {
   210	      const a = base + (i - (weapon.count - 1) / 2) * weapon.spread;
   211	      bullets.push({ friendly: true, x: player.x + Math.cos(a) * 18, y: player.y + Math.sin(a) * 18,
   212	        vx: Math.cos(a) * weapon.speed, vy: Math.sin(a) * weapon.speed, damage: weapon.damage,
   213	        life: .9, radius: player.weapon === 1 ? 3 : 2.5, color: weapon.color });
   214	    }
   215	    if (player.weapon === 1) player.ammo.spread--;
   216	    if (player.weapon === 2) player.ammo.rifle--;
   217	    player.shootTimer = weapon.cooldown;
   218	    shake = Math.max(shake, player.weapon === 1 ? 5 : 2.5);
   219	    flash = Math.max(flash, .08);
   220	    burst(player.x + Math.cos(base) * 20, player.y + Math.sin(base) * 20, weapon.color, player.weapon === 1 ? 8 : 3, 55);
   221	    for (const e of level.enemies) if (e.alive && dist(player.x, player.y, e.x, e.y) < 580) { e.alert = 5.5; e.state = 'alert'; }
   222	  }
   223	
   224	  function hurt(amount) {
   225	    if (player.invuln > 0 || gameState !== 'play') return;
   226	    player.health -= amount; player.invuln = .55; shake = Math.max(shake, 8); flash = .16;
   227	    burst(player.x, player.y, COLORS.red, 6, 65);
   228	    if (player.health <= 0) {
   229	      player.health = 0; gameState = 'dead'; saveBest(); toast = '';
   230	      burst(player.x, player.y, COLORS.red, 30, 150);
   231	    }
   232	  }
   233	
   234	  function enemyShoot(e, damage, speed, color) {
   235	    const a = Math.atan2(player.y - e.y, player.x - e.x);
   236	    bullets.push({ friendly: false, x: e.x + Math.cos(a) * 18, y: e.y + Math.sin(a) * 18,
   237	      vx: Math.cos(a) * speed, vy: Math.sin(a) * speed, damage, life: 3, radius: 4, color });
   238	    burst(e.x + Math.cos(a) * 17, e.y + Math.sin(a) * 17, color, 3, 45);
   239	  }
   240	
   241	  function updatePlayer(dt) {
   242	    const move = inputVector();
   243	    if (move.power > .02) {
   244	      const speed = 190 * (0.4 + move.power * .6);
   245	      const nx = player.x + move.x * speed * dt, ny = player.y + move.y * speed * dt;
   246	      if (walkable(nx, player.y, 14)) player.x = nx;
   247	      if (walkable(player.x, ny, 14)) player.y = ny;
   248	      if (!currentTarget()) player.face = Math.atan2(move.y, move.x);
   249	    }
   250	    player.shootTimer = Math.max(0, player.shootTimer - dt);
   251	    player.invuln = Math.max(0, player.invuln - dt);
   252	    if (isFiring()) fireWeapon();
   253	    const room = level.rooms.find(r => rectHas(r, player.x, player.y));
   254	    if (room) room.entered = true;
   255	    for (const card of level.cards) {
   256	      if (!card.got && dist(player.x, player.y, card.x, card.y) < 26) {
   257	        card.got = true;
   258	        const door = level.doors.find(d => d.id === card.key);
   259	        if (door) door.open = true;
   260	        addText(card.x, card.y - 20, `${card.key.toUpperCase()} CARD`, door ? door.color : COLORS.white);
   261	        toastMessage(`${card.key.toUpperCase()} DOOR OPEN`);
   262	        burst(card.x, card.y, door ? door.color : COLORS.white, 18, 85);
   263	      }
   264	    }
   265	    for (const p of level.pickups) {
   266	      if (!p.got && dist(player.x, player.y, p.x, p.y) < 24) {
   267	        p.got = true;
   268	        if (p.type === 'med') { player.health = Math.min(100, player.health + 34); toastMessage('MEDKIT +34'); addText(p.x, p.y - 20, '+34', COLORS.green); }
   269	        else if (p.type === 'ammo') { player.ammo.spread += 5; player.ammo.rifle += 16; toastMessage('AMMO CACHE'); addText(p.x, p.y - 20, 'AMMO', COLORS.amber); }
   270	        else if (p.type === 'spreadAmmo') { player.ammo.spread += 7; toastMessage('SHELLS +7'); addText(p.x, p.y - 20, '+7', COLORS.amber); }
   271	        else { player.ammo.rifle += 24; toastMessage('RIFLE ROUNDS'); addText(p.x, p.y - 20, '+24', COLORS.red); }
   272	        burst(p.x, p.y, p.type === 'med' ? COLORS.green : COLORS.amber, 14, 70);
   273	      }
   274	    }
   275	    if (dist(player.x, player.y, level.lift.x, level.lift.y) < 42) {
   276	      const ready = level.cards.every(c => c.got);
   277	      if (ready) {
   278	        levelNo++;
   279	        score = roomsCleared + kills;
   280	        makeLevel(levelNo);
   281	      } else if (toastTime <= .1) toastMessage('THREE CARDS REQUIRED');
   282	    }
   283	  }
   284	
   285	  function updateEnemies(dt) {
   286	    for (const e of level.enemies) {
   287	      if (!e.alive) continue;
   288	      const d = dist(e.x, e.y, player.x, player.y);
   289	      if (e.alert > 0) e.alert -= dt;
   290	      if (d < 240 && hasLOS(e, player)) { e.alert = Math.max(e.alert, 2.2); e.state = 'alert'; }
   291	      if (e.type === 'warden') e.shield = e.alert > 0 ? .92 : .58;
   292	      if (e.alert > 0) {
   293	        e.facing = Math.atan2(player.y - e.y, player.x - e.x);
   294	        if (e.type === 'lunger' || e.type === 'warden') {
   295	          if (d > (e.type === 'warden' ? 100 : 34)) {
   296	            const a = Math.atan2(player.y - e.y, player.x - e.x);
   297	            const speed = e.speed * (e.type === 'warden' ? .75 : 1);
   298	            const nx = e.x + Math.cos(a) * speed * dt, ny = e.y + Math.sin(a) * speed * dt;
   299	            if (walkable(nx, e.y, e.radius)) e.x = nx;
   300	            if (walkable(e.x, ny, e.radius)) e.y = ny;
   301	          } else if (e.type === 'lunger') hurt(22 * dt);
   302	        } else if ((e.type === 'gunner' || e.type === 'turret') && d < 560 && hasLOS(e, player)) {
   303	          e.cooldown -= dt;
   304	          if (e.cooldown <= 0) {
   305	            enemyShoot(e, e.type === 'turret' ? 12 : 8, e.type === 'turret' ? 250 : 220, e.color);
   306	            e.cooldown = e.type === 'turret' ? 1.0 : 1.25;
   307	          }
   308	          if (e.type === 'gunner' && d < 230) {
   309	            const a = Math.atan2(e.y - player.y, e.x - player.x), nx = e.x + Math.cos(a) * e.speed * dt, ny = e.y + Math.sin(a) * e.speed * dt;
   310	            if (walkable(nx, e.y, e.radius)) e.x = nx;
   311	            if (walkable(e.x, ny, e.radius)) e.y = ny;
   312	          }
   313	        }
   314	      } else {
   315	        e.patrol += dt * (e.type === 'warden' ? .8 : .35);
   316	        e.facing = e.patrol + Math.sin(e.patrol * .7) * .25;
   317	        if (e.type === 'warden') {
   318	          const nx = e.homeX + Math.cos(e.patrol) * 35, ny = e.homeY + Math.sin(e.patrol * .8) * 24;
   319	          if (walkable(nx, ny, e.radius)) { e.x = nx; e.y = ny; }
   320	        }
   321	      }
   322	      if (d < e.radius + 15 && e.type !== 'lunger') hurt(12 * dt);
   323	    }
   324	    for (const r of level.rooms) {
   325	      if (!r.entered || r.cleared) continue;
   326	      if (!level.enemies.some(e => e.alive && e.roomId === r.id)) {
   327	        r.cleared = true; roomsCleared++; score = roomsCleared + kills; addText(r.x + r.w / 2, r.y + 26, 'ROOM CLEAR', COLORS.green); toastMessage(`${r.name} CLEAR`);
   328	      }
   329	    }
   330	  }
   331	
   332	  function killEnemy(e) {
   333	    if (!e.alive) return;
   334	    e.alive = false; kills++; score = roomsCleared + kills; shake = Math.max(shake, 4);
   335	    addText(e.x, e.y - 20, '+1', COLORS.green); burst(e.x, e.y, e.color, e.type === 'warden' ? 24 : 14, 110);
   336	  }
   337	  function updateBullets(dt) {
   338	    for (let i = bullets.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   339	      const b = bullets[i]; b.x += b.vx * dt; b.y += b.vy * dt; b.life -= dt;
   340	      if (b.life <= 0 || !floorPoint(b.x, b.y) || blockedDoor(b.x, b.y, b.radius)) { bullets.splice(i, 1); continue; }
   341	      if (b.friendly) {
   342	        let hit = false;
   343	        for (const e of level.enemies) {
   344	          if (!e.alive || dist(b.x, b.y, e.x, e.y) > e.radius + b.radius) continue;
   345	          hit = true;
   346	          const shotAngle = Math.atan2(b.vy, b.vx);
   347	          if (e.type === 'warden' && e.shield > .7 && Math.abs(angleDiff(shotAngle, e.facing)) < 1.18 && Math.random() < e.shield) {
   348	            burst(b.x, b.y, COLORS.blue, 5, 40); addText(e.x, e.y - 26, 'BLOCK', COLORS.blue);
   349	          } else {
   350	            e.hp -= b.damage; burst(b.x, b.y, b.color, 3, 32); if (e.hp <= 0) killEnemy(e);
   351	          }
   352	          break;
   353	        }
   354	        if (hit) bullets.splice(i, 1);
   355	      } else if (dist(b.x, b.y, player.x, player.y) < 16) {
   356	        hurt(b.damage); burst(b.x, b.y, b.color, 5, 45); bullets.splice(i, 1);
   357	      }
   358	    }
   359	  }
   360	  function updateEffects(dt) {
   361	    for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   362	      const p = particles[i]; p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt; p.vx *= .93; p.vy *= .93; p.life -= dt;
   363	      if (p.life <= 0) particles.splice(i, 1);
   364	    }
   365	    for (let i = texts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { texts[i].y -= 22 * dt; texts[i].life -= dt; if (texts[i].life <= 0) texts.splice(i, 1); }
   366	    toastTime = Math.max(0, toastTime - dt); shake = Math.max(0, shake - dt * 17); flash = Math.max(0, flash - dt);
   367	  }
   368	
   369	  function update(dt) {
   370	    time += dt;
   371	    if (gameState === 'play') { updatePlayer(dt); if (gameState === 'play') updateEnemies(dt); if (gameState === 'play') updateBullets(dt); }
   372	    updateEffects(dt);
   373	    camera.x = lerp(camera.x, player.x, Math.min(1, dt * 5)); camera.y = lerp(camera.y, player.y, Math.min(1, dt * 5));
   374	  }
   375	
   376	  function roundRect(x, y, w, h, r) {
   377	    const rr = Math.min(r, w / 2, h / 2); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x + rr, y); ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, rr); ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, rr); ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, rr); ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, rr); ctx.closePath();
   378	  }
   379	  function worldText(text, x, y, size, color, align = 'center') {
   380	    ctx.font = `700 ${size}px system-ui, sans-serif`; ctx.textAlign = align; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle'; ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.fillText(text, x, y);
   381	  }
   382	  function drawWorld() {
   383	    ctx.fillStyle = '#0d171c'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 1200, 820);
   384	    for (const c of level.corridors) { ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.floor; ctx.fillRect(c.x, c.y, c.w, c.h); ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.wall; ctx.lineWidth = 6; ctx.strokeRect(c.x, c.y, c.w, c.h); }
   385	    for (const r of level.rooms) {
   386	      ctx.fillStyle = r.tint === 0 ? COLORS.floor : r.tint === 1 ? '#182831' : '#1a2930'; ctx.fillRect(r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h);
   387	      ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.wall; ctx.lineWidth = 8; ctx.strokeRect(r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h);
   388	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#263b45'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.strokeRect(r.x + 12, r.y + 12, r.w - 24, r.h - 24);
   389	      worldText(r.name, r.x + 20, r.y + 25, 10, r.cleared ? COLORS.green : '#58727b', 'left');
   390	    }
   391	    for (const d of level.decor) { ctx.fillStyle = d.c; ctx.fillRect(d.x, d.y, d.w, d.h); }
   392	    for (const d of level.doors) {
   393	      if (d.open) { ctx.strokeStyle = d.color; ctx.globalAlpha = .35; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.strokeRect(d.x, d.y, d.w, d.h); ctx.globalAlpha = 1; }
   394	      else { ctx.fillStyle = '#10191e'; ctx.fillRect(d.x, d.y, d.w, d.h); ctx.strokeStyle = d.color; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.strokeRect(d.x, d.y, d.w, d.h); }
   395	    }
   396	    const liftPulse = 1 + Math.sin(time * 3) * .12;
   397	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(level.lift.x, level.lift.y); ctx.rotate(Math.PI / 4); ctx.scale(liftPulse, liftPulse);
   398	    ctx.fillStyle = '#293d45'; ctx.fillRect(-34, -34, 68, 68); ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.cyan; ctx.lineWidth = 4; ctx.strokeRect(-30, -30, 60, 60); ctx.restore();
   399	    worldText('LIFT', level.lift.x, level.lift.y, 11, COLORS.cyan);
   400	    for (const c of level.cards) if (!c.got) {
   401	      const bob = Math.sin(time * 4 + c.bob) * 4; ctx.save(); ctx.translate(c.x, c.y + bob); ctx.rotate(Math.PI / 4);
   402	      ctx.fillStyle = level.doors.find(d => d.id === c.key).color; ctx.shadowColor = ctx.fillStyle; ctx.shadowBlur = 14; ctx.fillRect(-10, -10, 20, 20); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; ctx.restore();
   403	    }
   404	    for (const p of level.pickups) if (!p.got) {
   405	      const bob = Math.sin(time * 3 + p.bob) * 3; ctx.save(); ctx.translate(p.x, p.y + bob); ctx.fillStyle = p.type === 'med' ? COLORS.green : p.type === 'rifleAmmo' ? COLORS.red : COLORS.amber; ctx.strokeStyle = '#081116'; ctx.lineWidth = 3;
   406	      if (p.type === 'med') { ctx.fillRect(-11, -11, 22, 22); ctx.fillStyle = '#0b1a19'; ctx.fillRect(-3, -8, 6, 16); ctx.fillRect(-8, -3, 16, 6); }
   407	      else { ctx.fillRect(-12, -8, 24, 16); ctx.strokeRect(-12, -8, 24, 16); ctx.fillStyle = '#152027'; ctx.fillRect(-7, -3, 4, 6); ctx.fillRect(1, -3, 4, 6); }
   408	      ctx.restore();
   409	    }
   410	    for (const b of bullets) { ctx.fillStyle = b.color; ctx.shadowColor = b.color; ctx.shadowBlur = 8; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.radius, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; }
   411	    for (const e of level.enemies) if (e.alive) drawEnemy(e);
   412	    for (const p of particles) { ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(p.life / p.max, 0, 1); ctx.fillStyle = p.color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.size, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1; }
   413	    const target = currentTarget();
   414	    if (target) { ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.cyan; ctx.globalAlpha = .45; ctx.lineWidth = 1.5; ctx.setLineDash([5, 8]); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(player.x, player.y); ctx.lineTo(target.x, target.y); ctx.stroke(); ctx.setLineDash([]); ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.white; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(target.x, target.y, target.radius + 9 + Math.sin(time * 8) * 2, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke(); }
   415	    drawPlayer();
   416	    for (const t of texts) { ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(t.life, 0, 1); worldText(t.text, t.x, t.y, 12, t.color); ctx.globalAlpha = 1; }
   417	  }
   418	  function drawPlayer() {
   419	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(player.x, player.y); ctx.rotate(player.face); ctx.globalAlpha = player.invuln > 0 && Math.floor(time * 18) % 2 ? .45 : 1;
   420	    ctx.fillStyle = '#0b151a'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 18, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   421	    ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.cyan; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 13, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   422	    ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.white; ctx.fillRect(8, -4, 13, 8); ctx.fillStyle = '#0d3e47'; ctx.fillRect(-6, -5, 7, 10); ctx.restore();
   423	  }
   424	  function drawEnemy(e) {
   425	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(e.x, e.y); ctx.rotate(e.facing);
   426	    ctx.fillStyle = '#081116'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, e.radius + 4, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   427	    ctx.fillStyle = e.color;
   428	    if (e.type === 'lunger') { ctx.rotate(Math.PI / 4); ctx.fillRect(-11, -11, 22, 22); ctx.fillStyle = '#24171a'; ctx.fillRect(2, -4, 13, 8); }
   429	    else if (e.type === 'gunner') { ctx.fillRect(-13, -13, 26, 26); ctx.fillStyle = '#321b28'; ctx.fillRect(5, -4, 17, 8); }
   430	    else if (e.type === 'turret') { ctx.rotate(Math.PI / 4); ctx.fillRect(-14, -14, 28, 28); ctx.rotate(-Math.PI / 4); ctx.fillStyle = '#281b3d'; ctx.fillRect(4, -4, 19, 8); }
   431	    else { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, -19); ctx.lineTo(17, -9); ctx.lineTo(14, 13); ctx.lineTo(0, 20); ctx.lineTo(-14, 13); ctx.lineTo(-17, -9); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff1b3'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke(); ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.blue; ctx.globalAlpha = e.shield; ctx.lineWidth = 6; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 27, -1.1, 1.1); ctx.stroke(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1; }
   432	    ctx.restore();
   433	    if (e.hp < e.maxHp) { ctx.fillStyle = '#091217'; ctx.fillRect(e.x - 20, e.y - e.radius - 12, 40, 4); ctx.fillStyle = e.type === 'warden' ? COLORS.amber : COLORS.red; ctx.fillRect(e.x - 20, e.y - e.radius - 12, 40 * clamp(e.hp / e.maxHp, 0, 1), 4); }
   434	    if (e.alert > 0) worldText('!', e.x, e.y - e.radius - 20, 14, COLORS.red);
   435	  }
   436	
   437	  function panel(x, y, w, h, fill = 'rgba(10,22,28,.88)', stroke = '#304954') {
   438	    roundRect(x, y, w, h, 12); ctx.fillStyle = fill; ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = stroke; ctx.lineWidth = 1; ctx.stroke();
   439	  }
   440	  function drawHUD() {
   441	    panel(12, 12, W - 24, 55);
   442	    ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle'; ctx.font = '800 13px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.cyan; ctx.fillText('DESCENT', 26, 29); ctx.font = '700 11px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.muted; ctx.fillText(`LV ${String(levelNo).padStart(2, '0')}`, 26, 49);
   443	    ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.font = '800 13px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.white; ctx.fillText(`SCORE ${fmt(score)}`, W * .52, 30); ctx.font = '700 10px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.muted; ctx.fillText(`BEST ${fmt(best)}`, W * .52, 49);
   444	    ctx.textAlign = 'right'; ctx.font = '800 13px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.amber; ctx.fillText(`K ${String(kills).padStart(2, '0')}`, W - 26, 29); ctx.font = '700 11px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.green; ctx.fillText(`${roomsCleared} ROOMS`, W - 26, 49);
   445	    panel(12, 76, W - 24, 31, 'rgba(10,22,28,.72)', '#203943');
   446	    const weapon = weaponInfo(); ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.font = '800 11px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillStyle = weapon.color; ctx.fillText(`${weapon.name}  ${weapon.ammo}`, 24, 92);
   447	    ctx.textAlign = 'right'; ctx.fillStyle = player.health > 35 ? COLORS.green : COLORS.red; ctx.fillText(`HP ${String(Math.ceil(player.health)).padStart(3, '0')}`, W - 24, 92);
   448	    ctx.fillStyle = '#233941'; ctx.fillRect(24, 99, W - 48, 3); ctx.fillStyle = player.health > 35 ? COLORS.green : COLORS.red; ctx.fillRect(24, 99, (W - 48) * player.health / 100, 3);
   449	    const cards = level.cards.map(c => c.got ? c.key : '·').join('  '); ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.font = '700 10px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.muted; ctx.fillText(`CARDS  ${cards}`, W / 2, 125);
   450	    ctx.font = '600 10px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillStyle = '#9db6b5'; ctx.fillText('MOVE  •  HOLD FIRE  •  COLLECT CARDS  •  REACH LIFT', W / 2, 147);
   451	  }
   452	  function drawControls() {
   453	    const s = touch.stick, f = touch.fire;
   454	    ctx.globalAlpha = .82; ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(19,42,49,.82)'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, s.r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = '#44666e'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke();
   455	    const knobX = s.x + pointer.moveX * 30, knobY = s.y + pointer.moveY * 30; ctx.fillStyle = pointer.moveId !== null ? COLORS.cyan : '#6e9295'; ctx.globalAlpha = pointer.moveId !== null ? .9 : .55; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(knobX, knobY, 24, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   456	    ctx.globalAlpha = pointer.fire ? .95 : .72; ctx.fillStyle = pointer.fire ? COLORS.red : '#7d4350'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(f.x, f.y, f.r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = '#da7180'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1; worldText('FIRE', f.x, f.y, 14, COLORS.white);
   457	    panel(touch.swap.x, touch.swap.y, touch.swap.w, touch.swap.h, 'rgba(17,33,40,.92)', '#46616a'); ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.font = '800 11px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillStyle = weaponInfo().color; ctx.fillText(`${weaponInfo().name}  ⇄`, touch.swap.x + touch.swap.w / 2, touch.swap.y + 15); ctx.font = '600 9px system-ui, sans-serif'; ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.muted; ctx.fillText('TAP TO SWAP', touch.swap.x + touch.swap.w / 2, touch.swap.y + 31);
   458	  }
   459	  function drawOverlay() {
   460	    if (toastTime > 0 && toast) { const w = Math.min(W - 42, 280); panel((W - w) / 2, H * .2, w, 38, 'rgba(12,27,32,.92)', '#48636a'); worldText(toast, W / 2, H * .2 + 19, 12, COLORS.white); }
   461	    if (gameState === 'dead') {
   462	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(4,10,13,.78)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); panel(28, H * .32, W - 56, 190, 'rgba(12,24,29,.97)', COLORS.red);
   463	      worldText('RUN OVER', W / 2, H * .32 + 44, 26, COLORS.red); worldText(`SCORE  ${fmt(score)}`, W / 2, H * .32 + 82, 14, COLORS.white); worldText(`BEST  ${fmt(best)}`, W / 2, H * .32 + 107, 12, COLORS.muted); panel(W / 2 - 78, H * .32 + 130, 156, 42, '#263b43', COLORS.cyan); worldText('TAP TO RESTART', W / 2, H * .32 + 151, 11, COLORS.cyan);
   464	    }
   465	  }
   466	  function render() {
   467	    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0); ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.bg; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   468	    const zoom = Math.min(.78, Math.max(.58, Math.min(W / 510, H / 900)));
   469	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(W / 2 + (Math.random() - .5) * shake, H / 2 + (Math.random() - .5) * shake); ctx.scale(zoom, zoom); ctx.translate(-camera.x, -camera.y); drawWorld(); ctx.restore();
   470	    drawHUD(); drawControls(); drawOverlay();
   471	    if (flash > 0) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255,80,100,${flash * 1.8})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
   472	  }
   473	  function frame(now) {
   474	    const dt = Math.min(.033, (now - last) / 1000 || .016); last = now; update(dt); render(); requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   475	  }
   476	
   477	  function pointerPos(e) { const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect(); return { x: e.clientX - r.left, y: e.clientY - r.top }; }
   478	  function hitCircle(p, c) { return dist(p.x, p.y, c.x, c.y) <= c.r + 10; }
   479	  function hitRect(p, r) { return p.x >= r.x - 8 && p.x <= r.x + r.w + 8 && p.y >= r.y - 8 && p.y <= r.y + r.h + 8; }
   480	  function handlePointerDown(e) {
   481	    e.preventDefault(); const p = pointerPos(e); canvas.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);
   482	    if (gameState === 'dead') { startRun(); return; }
   483	    if (hitRect(p, touch.swap)) { cycleWeapon(); return; }
   484	    if (hitCircle(p, touch.fire)) { pointer.fireId = e.pointerId; pointer.fire = true; return; }
   485	    if (hitCircle(p, touch.stick)) { pointer.moveId = e.pointerId; updateStick(p); }
   486	  }
   487	  function updateStick(p) {
   488	    let dx = p.x - touch.stick.x, dy = p.y - touch.stick.y, l = Math.hypot(dx, dy); if (l > 42) { dx *= 42 / l; dy *= 42 / l; }
   489	    pointer.moveX = dx / 42; pointer.moveY = dy / 42;
   490	  }
   491	  function handlePointerMove(e) { if (pointer.moveId === e.pointerId) { e.preventDefault(); updateStick(pointerPos(e)); } }
   492	  function handlePointerUp(e) { e.preventDefault(); if (pointer.moveId === e.pointerId) { pointer.moveId = null; pointer.moveX = 0; pointer.moveY = 0; } if (pointer.fireId === e.pointerId) { pointer.fireId = null; pointer.fire = false; } }
   493	
   494	  window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
   495	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown, { passive: false });
   496	  canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', handlePointerMove, { passive: false });
   497	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', handlePointerUp, { passive: false });
   498	  canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', handlePointerUp, { passive: false });
   499	  canvas.addEventListener('contextmenu', e => e.preventDefault());
   500	  canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', e => e.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   501	  window.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
   502	    const k = e.key.length === 1 ? e.key.toLowerCase() : e.key; keys[k] = true;
   503	    if ([' ', 'ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight'].includes(e.key)) e.preventDefault();
   504	    if (gameState === 'dead' && (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ')) startRun();
   505	    if (gameState === 'play' && (k === 'q' || k === 'e' || k === '1' || k === '2' || k === '3')) { if (k === 'q' || k === 'e') cycleWeapon(); else player.weapon = Number(k) - 1; }
   506	  });
   507	  window.addEventListener('keyup', e => { const k = e.key.length === 1 ? e.key.toLowerCase() : e.key; keys[k] = false; });
   508	  resize(); loadBest(); startRun(); requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   509	})();

WASD / arrows move; Space fires; Q/E or 1/2/3 swaps weapons.
Touch: drag the left stick, hold FIRE, tap the weapon chip to swap.
Collect the three colored cards, clear rooms, and reach the lift.
Death ends the run; tap or press Enter to restart instantly.
Best score persists locally in the browser.

    69	  function mulberry32(seed) {
    70	    return () => {
    71	      seed |= 0;
    72	      seed = (seed + 0x6D2B79F5) | 0;
    73	      let t = Math.imul(seed ^ (seed >>> 15), 1 | seed);
    74	      t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
    75	      return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    76	    };
    77	  }
    78	
    79	  function clamp(v, lo, hi) { return Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, v)); }
    80	  function lerp(a, b, t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }
    81	  function dist(a, b) { return Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y); }
    82	  function angleTo(a, b) { return Math.atan2(b.y - a.y, b.x - a.x); }
    83	  function normAngle(a) {
    84	    while (a > Math.PI) a -= TAU;
    85	    while (a < -Math.PI) a += TAU;
    86	    return a;
    87	  }
    88	  function approachAngle(a, b, amount) { return a + clamp(normAngle(b - a), -amount, amount); }

   104	  function addWall(walls, x, y, w, h, kind) {
   105	    if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return;
   106	    walls.push({ x, y, w, h, kind: kind || 'barrier' });

   109	  function makeArena(levelNumber) {
   110	    const rnd = mulberry32((runSeed + Math.imul(levelNumber, 0x45d9f3b)) >>> 0);
   111	    const walls = [];
   112	    const slits = [];
   113	    const addSafe = (x, y, w, h, kind) => {
   114	      if (x < 54 || y < 54 || x + w > WORLD.w - 54 || y + h > WORLD.h - 54) return;
   115	      addWall(walls, x, y, w, h, kind);
   116	    };
   117	
   118	    const crossY = levelNumber === 1 ? 750 : 610 + Math.floor(rnd() * 330);
   119	    const gapX = 220 + Math.floor(rnd() * 340);
   120	    const gapW = 84 + Math.floor(rnd() * 42);
   121	    addSafe(64, crossY, gapX - 64, 24, 'slit');
   122	    addSafe(gapX + gapW, crossY, 836 - gapX - gapW, 24, 'slit');
   123	    slits.push({ x: gapX, y: crossY + 12, w: gapW, vertical: false });
   124	
   125	    if (levelNumber === 1) {
   126	      addSafe(282, 230, 24, 360, 'alley');

   134	      addSafe(vx, vy, 24, vh, 'alley');
   135	      if (rnd() > 0.35) addSafe(vx, vy + vh + 112, 24, 220 + Math.floor(rnd() * 180), 'alley');
   136	
   137	      const slitX = 140 + Math.floor(rnd() * 600);
   138	      const slitY = 250 + Math.floor(rnd() * 700);
   139	      const slitGap = 80 + Math.floor(rnd() * 35);
   140	      addSafe(slitX, 80, 22, slitY - 80, 'slit');
   141	      addSafe(slitX, slitY + slitGap, 22, 1220 - slitY - slitGap, 'slit');
   142	      slits.push({ x: slitX + 11, y: slitY, w: 0, h: slitGap, vertical: true });
   143	
   144	      const cornerX = 120 + Math.floor(rnd() * 590);
   145	      const cornerY = 850 + Math.floor(rnd() * 220);
   146	      addSafe(cornerX, cornerY, 250, 22, 'corner');
   147	      addSafe(cornerX, cornerY - 175, 22, 197, 'corner');
   148	      if (levelNumber > 2) {
   149	        const cornerX2 = 570 + Math.floor(rnd() * 130);
   150	        addSafe(cornerX2, 360, 200, 22, 'corner');

   152	      }
   153	    }
   154

   157	    let attempts = 0;
   158	    while (pickups.length < pickupCount && attempts++ < 100) {
   159	      const p = { x: 100 + rnd() * 700, y: 160 + rnd() * 1080 };

   161	    }
   162	
   163	    return { walls, slits, pickups, seed: runSeed + levelNumber };
   164	  }

   166	  function isBlockedIn(p, walls, radius) {
   167	    if (p.x - radius < WORLD.pad || p.x + radius > WORLD.w - WORLD.pad || p.y - radius < WORLD.pad || p.y + radius > WORLD.h - WORLD.pad) return true;
   168	    for (const wall of walls) if (circleRect(p.x, p.y, radius, wall)) return true;
   169	    return false;
   170	  }
   171	
   172	  function circleRect(x, y, r, rect) {
   173	    const nx = clamp(x, rect.x, rect.x + rect.w);
   174	    const ny = clamp(y, rect.y, rect.y + rect.h);
   175	    return Math.hypot(x - nx, y - ny) < r;
   176	  }

   203	  function createLevel() {
   204	    arena = makeArena(level);
   205	    const spawnCandidates = [
   206	      { x: 450, y: 160 }, { x: 130, y: 170 }, { x: 770, y: 180 },
   207	      { x: 120, y: 540 }, { x: 780, y: 540 }, { x: 770, y: 1100 }, { x: 150, y: 1040 }
   208	    ];
   209	    enemies = [];
   210	    const enemyCount = Math.min(4, level);
   211	    const behaviors = ['camper', 'flanker', 'rico', 'flanker'];
   212	    for (let i = 0; i < enemyCount; i++) {
   213	      let spawn = spawnCandidates[i % spawnCandidates.length];
   214	      for (let n = 0; n < spawnCandidates.length; n++) {
   215	        const candidate = spawnCandidates[(i + n) % spawnCandidates.length];
   216	        if (!isBlockedIn(candidate, arena.walls, 25) && dist(candidate, player) > 380 && enemies.every(e => dist(candidate, e) > 150)) { spawn = candidate; break; }
   217	      }
   218	      const color = behaviors[i] === 'camper' ? COLORS.camper : behaviors[i] === 'rico' ? COLORS.rico : COLORS.flanker;
   219	      enemies.push({
   220	        x: spawn.x, y: spawn.y, angle: i % 2 ? Math.PI / 2 : 0, turret: Math.PI / 2,
   221	        radius: 22, alive: true, behavior: behaviors[i], color,
   222	        cooldown: 0.8 + i * 0.45, phase: i * 1.7, anchor: { x: spawn.x, y: spawn.y },
   223	        flash: 0, stuck: 0
   224	      });
   225	    }
   226	    player.x = 450;
   227	    player.y = 1220;
   228	    player.angle = -Math.PI / 2;
   229	    player.turret = -Math.PI / 2;

   245	  function isBlocked(x, y, radius) {
   246	    return isBlockedIn({ x, y }, arena.walls, radius);
   247	  }

   249	  function moveTank(tank, amount, dt) {
   250	    const forward = (amount.left + amount.right) * 0.5;
   251	    const turn = (amount.right - amount.left) * 0.5;
   252	    tank.angle = normAngle(tank.angle + turn * 1.9 * dt);
   253	    const speed = tank === player ? 72 : 52;
   254	    const nx = tank.x + Math.cos(tank.angle) * forward * speed * dt;
   255	    const ny = tank.y + Math.sin(tank.angle) * forward * speed * dt;
   256	    let moved = false;
   257	    if (!isBlocked(nx, tank.y, tank.radius)) { tank.x = nx; moved = true; }
   258	    if (!isBlocked(tank.x, ny, tank.radius)) { tank.y = ny; moved = true; }
   259	    if (!moved && Math.abs(forward) > 0.1) tank.stuck = (tank.stuck || 0) + dt;
   260	    else tank.stuck = 0;
   261	  }
   262	
   263	  function playerInput() {
   264	    let forward = 0;
   265	    let turn = 0;
   266	    if (keys.w || keys.ArrowUp) forward += 1;
   267	    if (keys.s || keys.ArrowDown) forward -= 1;
   268	    if (keys.a || keys.ArrowLeft) turn -= 1;
   269	    if (keys.d || keys.ArrowRight) turn += 1;
   270	    if (drivePointer && pointers.has(drivePointer)) {
   271	      const p = pointers.get(drivePointer);
   272	      const dx = clamp(p.x - p.sx, -110, 110);
   273	      const dy = clamp(p.y - p.sy, -110, 110);
   274	      const nx = dx / 110;
   275	      const ny = dy / 110;
   276	      forward = clamp(-ny, -1, 1);
   277	      turn = clamp(nx, -1, 1);
   278	    }
   279	    return { left: clamp(forward - turn * 0.75, -1, 1), right: clamp(forward + turn * 0.75, -1, 1) };
   280	  }
   281	
   282	  function updatePlayer(dt) {
   283	    if (!player.alive) {
   284	      respawnTimer -= dt;
   285	      if (respawnTimer <= 0 && lives > 0) {
   286	        player.x = 450;
   287	        player.y = 1220;
   288	        player.angle = -Math.PI / 2;
   289	        player.turret = -Math.PI / 2;
   290	        player.alive = true;
   291	        player.shield = 1.1;
   292	        levelBanner = `REDEPLOYED // ${lives} LIVES`;
   293	        bannerTimer = 1.3;
   294	      }
   295	      return;
   296	    }
   297	    player.shield = Math.max(0, player.shield - dt);
   298	    moveTank(player, playerInput(), dt);
   299	    if (player.mineCount < 4) {
   300	      for (let i = arena.pickups.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   301	        if (dist(player, arena.pickups[i]) < 30) {
   302	          arena.pickups.splice(i, 1);
   303	          player.mineCount++;
   304	          levelBanner = 'MINE ACQUIRED';
   305	          bannerTimer = 0.9;
   306	          burst(player.x, player.y, COLORS.mine, 9, 80);
   307	        }
   308	      }
   309	    }
   310	  }
   311	
   312	  function updateAI(tank, dt) {
   313	    if (!tank.alive || !player.alive) return;
   314	    tank.cooldown -= dt;
   315	    tank.flash = Math.max(0, tank.flash - dt);
   316	    const d = dist(tank, player);
   317	    const direct = angleTo(tank, player);
   318	    let goal = tank.anchor;
   319	    let speedFactor = 0.45;
   320	
   321	    if (tank.behavior === 'camper') {
   322	      goal = tank.anchor;
   323	      speedFactor = d > 270 ? 0.7 : 0.22;
   324	    } else if (tank.behavior === 'flanker') {
   325	      const side = Math.sin(tank.phase) < 0 ? -1 : 1;
   326	      const orbit = { x: player.x - Math.sin(direct) * 230 * side, y: player.y + Math.cos(direct) * 230 * side };
   327	      goal = orbit;
   328	      speedFactor = 0.85;
   329	      tank.phase += dt * 0.16;
   330	    } else {
   331	      const desired = 380;
   332	      const side = Math.cos(tank.phase) < 0 ? -1 : 1;
   333	      goal = { x: player.x + Math.cos(direct) * (d < desired ? 120 : -80) + Math.sin(direct) * 190 * side, y: player.y + Math.sin(direct) * (d < desired ? 120 : -80) - Math.cos(direct) * 190 * side };
   334	      speedFactor = 0.58;
   335	      tank.phase += dt * 0.23;
   336	    }
   337	
   338	    if (tank.stuck > 0.45) tank.phase += 1.8;
   339	    const desiredBody = angleTo(tank, goal);
   340	    const error = normAngle(desiredBody - tank.angle);
   341	    const turn = clamp(error * 2.6, -1, 1);
   342	    const forward = Math.abs(error) > 1.7 ? 0.05 : speedFactor;
   343	    moveTank(tank, { left: clamp(forward - turn * 0.72, -1, 1), right: clamp(forward + turn * 0.72, -1, 1) }, dt);
   344	
   345	    let shotAngle = direct;
   346	    const bank = bankShot(tank, player);
   347	    if (tank.behavior === 'rico' && bank) shotAngle = bank.angle;
   348	    else if (!lineHitsWall(tank, player) && d < 780) shotAngle = direct;
   349	    else if (bank) shotAngle = bank.angle;
   350	    tank.turret = approachAngle(tank.turret, shotAngle, dt * (tank.behavior === 'rico' ? 1.25 : 1.8));
   351	
   352	    const fireRange = tank.behavior === 'camper' ? 760 : 700;
   353	    if (tank.cooldown <= 0 && d < fireRange && Math.abs(normAngle(tank.turret - shotAngle)) < 0.24) {
   354	      fireShell(tank, tank.turret);
   355	      tank.cooldown = tank.behavior === 'camper' ? 1.7 : tank.behavior === 'rico' ? 2.05 : 1.35;
   356	    }
   357	  }
   358	
   359	  function lineHitsWall(a, b) {
   360	    const length = dist(a, b);
   361	    const steps = Math.max(2, Math.ceil(length / 20));
   362	    for (let i = 1; i < steps; i++) {
   363	      const t = i / steps;
   364	      const x = lerp(a.x, b.x, t);
   365	      const y = lerp(a.y, b.y, t);
   366	      for (const wall of arena.walls) if (x > wall.x - 3 && x < wall.x + wall.w + 3 && y > wall.y - 3 && y < wall.y + wall.h + 3) return true;
   367	    }
   368	    return false;
   369	  }
   370	
   371	  function bankShot(shooter, target) {
   372	    let bestShot = null;
   373	    for (const wall of arena.walls) {
   374	      const horizontal = wall.w >= wall.h;
   375	      const edges = horizontal ? [wall.y - 1, wall.y + wall.h + 1] : [wall.x - 1, wall.x + wall.w + 1];
   376	      for (const edge of edges) {
   377	        const virtualTarget = horizontal ? { x: target.x, y: edge * 2 - target.y } : { x: edge * 2 - target.x, y: target.y };
   378	        const length = dist(shooter, virtualTarget);
   379	        if (length > 1000) continue;
   380	        const angle = angleTo(shooter, virtualTarget);
   381	        const hit = { x: shooter.x + Math.cos(angle) * length * 0.51, y: shooter.y + Math.sin(angle) * length * 0.51 };
   382	        if (horizontal ? Math.abs(hit.y - edge) > 36 : Math.abs(hit.x - edge) > 36) continue;
   383	        if (lineHitsWall(shooter, virtualTarget) && (!bestShot || length < bestShot.length)) bestShot = { angle, length };
   384	      }
   385	    }
   386	    return bestShot;
   387	  }
   388	
   389	  function fireShell(owner, angle) {
   390	    const muzzle = { x: owner.x + Math.cos(angle) * 30, y: owner.y + Math.sin(angle) * 30 };
   391	    shells.push({ x: muzzle.x, y: muzzle.y, vx: Math.cos(angle) * 560, vy: Math.sin(angle) * 560, r: 5, bounces: 0, owner, life: 3.4, color: owner === player ? COLORS.shell : owner.color });
   392	    burst(muzzle.x, muzzle.y, owner === player ? COLORS.playerHot : owner.color, 5, 55);
   393	    shake = Math.max(shake, owner === player ? 2.5 : 1.2);
   394	  }
   395	
   396	  function updateShells(dt) {
   397	    for (let i = shells.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   398	      const shell = shells[i];
   399	      shell.life -= dt;
   400	      if (shell.life <= 0) { shells.splice(i, 1); continue; }
   401	      shell.x += shell.vx * dt;
   402	      shell.y += shell.vy * dt;
   403	      let normal = null;
   404	      if (shell.x < WORLD.pad + shell.r) { shell.x = WORLD.pad + shell.r; normal = { x: 1, y: 0 }; }
   405	      else if (shell.x > WORLD.w - WORLD.pad - shell.r) { shell.x = WORLD.w - WORLD.pad - shell.r; normal = { x: -1, y: 0 }; }
   406	      else if (shell.y < WORLD.pad + shell.r) { shell.y = WORLD.pad + shell.r; normal = { x: 0, y: 1 }; }
   407	      else if (shell.y > WORLD.h - WORLD.pad - shell.r) { shell.y = WORLD.h - WORLD.pad - shell.r; normal = { x: 0, y: -1 }; }
   408	      if (!normal) {
   409	        for (const wall of arena.walls) {
   410	          if (circleRect(shell.x, shell.y, shell.r, wall)) { normal = wallNormal(shell, wall); break; }
   411	        }
   412	      }
   413	      if (normal) {
   414	        if (shell.bounces >= 2) { burst(shell.x, shell.y, shell.color, 8, 85); shells.splice(i, 1); continue; }
   415	        const dot = shell.vx * normal.x + shell.vy * normal.y;
   416	        shell.vx -= 2 * dot * normal.x;
   417	        shell.vy -= 2 * dot * normal.y;
   418	        shell.x += normal.x * 4;
   419	        shell.y += normal.y * 4;
   420	        shell.bounces++;
   421	        burst(shell.x, shell.y, COLORS.shell, 10, 105);
   422	        shake = Math.max(shake, 3.2);
   423	      }
   424	      let hit = false;
   425	      if (shell.owner !== player && player.alive && player.shield <= 0 && dist(shell, player) < player.radius + shell.r) {
   426	        hitPlayer(); hit = true;
   427	      } else if (shell.owner === player) {
   428	        for (const enemy of enemies) {
   429	          if (enemy.alive && dist(shell, enemy) < enemy.radius + shell.r) { hitEnemy(enemy); hit = true; break; }
   430	        }
   431	      }
   432	      if (hit) shells.splice(i, 1);
   433	    }
   434	  }
   435	
   436	  function wallNormal(shell, wall) {
   437	    const left = Math.abs(shell.x - wall.x);
   438	    const right = Math.abs(shell.x - (wall.x + wall.w));
   439	    const top = Math.abs(shell.y - wall.y);
   440	    const bottom = Math.abs(shell.y - (wall.y + wall.h));
   441	    const min = Math.min(left, right, top, bottom);
   442	    if (min === left) return { x: -1, y: 0 };
   443	    if (min === right) return { x: 1, y: 0 };
   444	    if (min === top) return { x: 0, y: -1 };
   445	    return { x: 0, y: 1 };
   446	  }
   447	
   448	  function hitPlayer() {
   449	    if (!player.alive) return;
   450	    player.alive = false;
   451	    lives--;
   452	    respawnTimer = 1.05;
   453	    shells.length = 0;
   454	    burst(player.x, player.y, COLORS.player, 30, 210);
   455	    shake = 16;
   456	    updateScore();
   457	    if (lives <= 0) {
   458	      state = 'fail';
   459	      levelBanner = 'HULL LOST';
   460	      bannerTimer = 999;
   461	      saveBest();
   462	    } else {
   463	      levelBanner = `HULL LOST // ${lives} LIVES`;
   464	      bannerTimer = 1.2;
   465	    }
   466	  }
   467	
   468	  function hitEnemy(enemy) {
   469	    enemy.alive = false;
   470	    kills++;
   471	    updateScore();
   472	    burst(enemy.x, enemy.y, enemy.color, 30, 210);
   473	    shake = Math.max(shake, 9);
   474	    if (enemies.every(e => !e.alive)) {
   475	      state = 'clear';
   476	      clearTimer = 1.45;
   477	      levelBanner = 'SECTOR SECURED';
   478	      bannerTimer = 999;
   479	      shells.length = 0;
   480	      saveBest();
   481	    }
   482	  }
   483	
   484	  function updateMines(dt) {
   485	    for (let i = deployedMines.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   486	      const mine = deployedMines[i];
   487	      mine.arm -= dt;
   488	      if (mine.arm > 0) continue;
   489	      let triggered = null;
   490	      for (const enemy of enemies) if (enemy.alive && dist(mine, enemy) < 32) { triggered = enemy; break; }
   491	      if (triggered) { hitEnemy(triggered); burst(mine.x, mine.y, COLORS.mine, 36, 240); deployedMines.splice(i, 1); }
   492	    }
   493	  }
   494	
   495	  function dropMine() {
   496	    if (state !== 'play' || !player.alive || player.mineCount <= 0) return;
   497	    player.mineCount--;
   498	    deployedMines.push({ x: player.x - Math.cos(player.angle) * 18, y: player.y - Math.sin(player.angle) * 18, arm: 0.55, pulse: 0 });
   499	    levelBanner = 'MINE DEPLOYED';
   500	    bannerTimer = 0.8;
   501	    burst(player.x, player.y, COLORS.mine, 8, 70);
   502	  }
   503	
   504	  function burst(x, y, color, count, force) {
   505	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   506	      const a = Math.random() * TAU;
   507	      const speed = force * (0.25 + Math.random() * 0.9);
   508	      sparks.push({ x, y, vx: Math.cos(a) * speed, vy: Math.sin(a) * speed, life: 0.24 + Math.random() * 0.52, max: 0.8, size: 1.5 + Math.random() * 3, color });
   509	    }
   510	    for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(8, Math.ceil(count / 4)); i++) debris.push({ x, y, r: 3 + Math.random() * 7, life: .38 + Math.random() * .25, color });
   511	  }
   512	
   513	  function updateFx(dt) {
   514	    for (let i = sparks.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   515	      const p = sparks[i];
   516	      p.life -= dt;
   517	      p.x += p.vx * dt;
   518	      p.y += p.vy * dt;
   519	      p.vx *= 0.94;
   520	      p.vy *= 0.94;
   521	      if (p.life <= 0) sparks.splice(i, 1);
   522	    }
   523	    for (let i = debris.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   524	      debris[i].life -= dt;
   525	      debris[i].r += dt * 30;
   526	      if (debris[i].life <= 0) debris.splice(i, 1);
   527	    }
   528	    shake = Math.max(0, shake - dt * 25);
   529	    aimFlash = Math.max(0, aimFlash - dt);
   530	    bannerTimer = Math.max(0, bannerTimer - dt);
   531	  }
   532	
   533	  function getCamera() {
   534	    const visibleW = W / scale;
   535	    const visibleH = H / scale;
   536	    const focus = player && player.alive ? player : { x: 450, y: 700 };
   537	    return { x: clamp(focus.x - visibleW * 0.5, 0, WORLD.w - visibleW), y: clamp(focus.y - visibleH * 0.56, 0, WORLD.h - visibleH) };
   538	  }
   539	
   540	  function worldPoint(sx, sy) {
   541	    const visible = getCamera();
   542	    return { x: visible.x + sx / scale, y: visible.y + sy / scale };
   543	  }
   544	
   545	  function draw() {
   546	    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
   547	    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
   548	    ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.ink;
   549	    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   550	    camera = getCamera();
   551	    const sx = shake ? (Math.random() - 0.5) * shake : 0;
   552	    const sy = shake ? (Math.random() - 0.5) * shake : 0;
   553	
   554	    ctx.save();
   555	    ctx.translate(sx, sy);
   556	    ctx.scale(scale, scale);
   557	    ctx.translate(-camera.x, -camera.y);
   558	    drawArena();
   559	    for (const pickup of arena.pickups) drawPickup(pickup);
   560	    for (const mine of deployedMines) drawMine(mine);
   561	    for (const shell of shells) drawShell(shell);
   562	    for (const p of sparks) drawSpark(p);
   563	    for (const p of debris) drawDebris(p);
   564	    for (const enemy of enemies) if (enemy.alive) drawTank(enemy, false);
   565	    if (player.alive || state !== 'play') drawTank(player, true);
   566	    if (aimFlash > 0 && player.alive) {
   567	      ctx.save();
   568	      ctx.globalAlpha = aimFlash * 3;
   569	      ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.playerHot;
   570	      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   571	      ctx.setLineDash([8, 9]);
   572	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(player.x, player.y); ctx.lineTo(lastAim.x, lastAim.y); ctx.stroke();
   573	      ctx.restore();
   574	    }
   575	    ctx.restore();
   576	    drawScreenFx();
   577	    drawUi();
   578	  }
   579	
   580	  function drawArena() {
   581	    ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.field;
   582	    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, WORLD.w, WORLD.h);
   583	    ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.grid;
   584	    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
   585	    const left = Math.floor(camera.x / 48) * 48 - 48;
   586	    const right = camera.x + W / scale + 48;
   587	    const top = Math.floor(camera.y / 48) * 48 - 48;
   588	    const bottom = camera.y + H / scale + 48;
   589	    for (let x = left; x < right; x += 48) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, top); ctx.lineTo(x, bottom); ctx.stroke(); }
   590	    for (let y = top; y < bottom; y += 48) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(left, y); ctx.lineTo(right, y); ctx.stroke(); }
   591	    ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.gridBright;
   592	    ctx.lineWidth = 3;
   593	    ctx.strokeRect(WORLD.pad, WORLD.pad, WORLD.w - WORLD.pad * 2, WORLD.h - WORLD.pad * 2);
   594	    for (const wall of arena.walls) {
   595	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.4)'; ctx.fillRect(wall.x + 7, wall.y + 8, wall.w, wall.h);
   596	      ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.wall; ctx.fillRect(wall.x, wall.y, wall.w, wall.h);
   597	      ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.wallEdge;
   598	      ctx.fillRect(wall.x, wall.y, wall.w, Math.min(3, wall.h));
   599	      ctx.fillRect(wall.x, wall.y, Math.min(3, wall.w), wall.h);
   600	    }
   601	    for (const slit of arena.slits) {
   602	      ctx.save();
   603	      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.58;
   604	      ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.mine;
   605	      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   606	      ctx.setLineDash([3, 8]);
   607	      ctx.beginPath();
   608	      if (slit.vertical) { ctx.moveTo(slit.x, slit.y + 15); ctx.lineTo(slit.x, slit.y + slit.h - 15); }
   609	      else { ctx.moveTo(slit.x + 15, slit.y); ctx.lineTo(slit.x + slit.w - 15, slit.y); }
   610	      ctx.stroke();
   611	      ctx.restore();
   612	    }
   613	  }
   614	
   615	  function drawTank(tank, isPlayer) {
   616	    ctx.save();
   617	    ctx.translate(tank.x, tank.y);
   618	    ctx.rotate(tank.angle);
   619	    ctx.globalAlpha = tank.alive ? 1 : 0.25;
   620	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.5)'; ctx.fillRect(-24, -13, 48, 32);
   621	    ctx.fillStyle = '#15232c'; ctx.fillRect(-23, -19, 46, 10); ctx.fillRect(-23, 9, 46, 10);
   622	    ctx.fillStyle = tank.flash > 0 ? '#ffffff' : tank.color;
   623	    ctx.fillRect(-18, -14, 36, 28);
   624	    ctx.fillStyle = isPlayer ? COLORS.playerHot : '#ffe0bc';
   625	    ctx.fillRect(-7, -9, 15, 18);
   626	    ctx.strokeStyle = tank.color;
   627	    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   628	    ctx.strokeRect(-18, -14, 36, 28);
   629	    ctx.restore();
   630	
   631	    ctx.save();
   632	    ctx.translate(tank.x, tank.y);
   633	    ctx.rotate(tank.turret);
   634	    ctx.strokeStyle = isPlayer ? COLORS.playerHot : '#ffd2aa';
   635	    ctx.lineWidth = 8;
   636	    ctx.lineCap = 'square';
   637	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, 0); ctx.lineTo(31, 0); ctx.stroke();
   638	    ctx.fillStyle = tank.color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 12, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   639	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#e9fbfa'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke();
   640	    ctx.restore();
   641	
   642	    if (!isPlayer) {
   643	      ctx.save();
   644	      ctx.translate(tank.x, tank.y - 34);
   645	      ctx.fillStyle = tank.color;
   646	      ctx.font = 'bold 11px ui-monospace, monospace';
   647	      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   648	      ctx.fillText(tank.behavior === 'camper' ? 'CAMP' : tank.behavior === 'rico' ? 'RICO' : 'FLANK', 0, 0);
   649	      ctx.restore();
   650	    }
   651	    if (isPlayer && player.shield > 0) {
   652	      ctx.save(); ctx.translate(player.x, player.y); ctx.globalAlpha = 0.22 + Math.sin(performance.now() * 0.012) * 0.1; ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.playerHot; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 31, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore();
   653	    }
   654	  }
   655	
   656	  function drawShell(shell) {
   657	    ctx.save();
   658	    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.2; ctx.fillStyle = shell.color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(shell.x, shell.y, 13, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   659	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.fillStyle = shell.color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(shell.x, shell.y, shell.r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   660	    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(shell.x - 1, shell.y - 1, 2, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   661	    ctx.restore();
   662	  }
   663	
   664	  function drawPickup(p) {
   665	    const pulse = 1 + Math.sin(performance.now() * 0.005 + p.x) * 0.12;
   666	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(p.x, p.y); ctx.rotate(performance.now() * 0.001);
   667	    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.2; ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.mine; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 23 * pulse, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   668	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.mine; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 12 * pulse, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
   669	    ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.mine; ctx.fillRect(-5, -5, 10, 10); ctx.restore();
   670	  }
   671	
   672	  function drawMine(mine) {
   673	    const pulse = 1 + Math.sin(performance.now() * 0.007 + mine.x) * 0.16;
   674	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(mine.x, mine.y); ctx.globalAlpha = mine.arm > 0 ? 0.38 : 1;
   675	    ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.mine; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 8 * pulse, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   676	    ctx.strokeStyle = COLORS.mine; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 16 * pulse, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore();
   677	  }
   678	
   679	  function drawSpark(p) {
   680	    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(p.life / p.max, 0, 1); ctx.fillStyle = p.color; ctx.fillRect(p.x, p.y, p.size, p.size); ctx.restore();
   681	  }
   682	
   683	  function drawDebris(p) {
   684	    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(p.life * 2, 0, 1); ctx.strokeStyle = p.color; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore();
   685	  }
   686	
   687	  function drawScreenFx() {
   688	    const gradient = ctx.createRadialGradient(W * .5, H * .46, Math.min(W, H) * .18, W * .5, H * .5, Math.max(W, H) * .72);
   689	    gradient.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
   690	    gradient.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,.52)');
   691	    ctx.fillStyle = gradient; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   692	    if (state === 'fail') { ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(80,7,20,.23)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
   693	  }
   694	
   695	  function drawUi() {
   696	    ctx.save();
   697	    ctx.textBaseline = 'top';
   698	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(5,13,22,.82)';
   699	    ctx.fillRect(12, 12, Math.min(228, W - 24), 70);
   700	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(111,224,220,.42)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1; ctx.strokeRect(12.5, 12.5, Math.min(228, W - 25), 70);
   701	    ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.playerHot; ctx.font = 'bold 13px ui-monospace, monospace'; ctx.fillText('IRONCLAD // ALLEY', 23, 22);
   702	    ctx.fillStyle = '#a6bac3'; ctx.font = '11px ui-monospace, monospace'; ctx.fillText(`SECTOR ${String(level).padStart(2, '0')}   SCORE ${String(score).padStart(2, '0')}`, 23, 43);
   703	    ctx.fillStyle = '#78909b'; ctx.fillText(`KILLS ${String(kills).padStart(2, '0')}   BEST ${String(best).padStart(2, '0')}`, 23, 59);
   704	
   705	    const statX = W - 96;
   706	    ctx.textAlign = 'right'; ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.playerHot; ctx.font = 'bold 12px ui-monospace, monospace'; ctx.fillText('LIVES', statX, 19);
   707	    ctx.fillStyle = lives > 1 ? COLORS.mine : COLORS.enemy; ctx.font = 'bold 21px ui-monospace, monospace'; ctx.fillText('◆'.repeat(Math.max(0, lives)), statX, 37);
   708	    ctx.fillStyle = COLORS.mine; ctx.font = 'bold 12px ui-monospace, monospace'; ctx.fillText(`MINES ${player ? player.mineCount : 0}`, statX, 65);
   709	    ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   710	
   711	    if (bannerTimer > 0 && levelBanner) {
   712	      ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.font = 'bold 21px ui-monospace, monospace'; ctx.fillStyle = state === 'fail' ? COLORS.enemy : COLORS.playerHot; ctx.fillText(levelBanner, W * .5, H * .32);
   713	      if (state === 'fail') { ctx.font = '12px ui-monospace, monospace'; ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd1d4'; ctx.fillText('TAP OR PRESS ENTER TO RESTART', W * .5, H * .32 + 34); }

   734	
   735	  function isMineButton(x, y) { return x > W - 115 && y > H - 125; }
   736	
   737	  function fireAtScreen(x, y) {
   738	    if (state === 'fail') { resetRun(); return; }
   739	    if (state !== 'play' || !player.alive) return;
   740	    lastAim = worldPoint(x, y);
   741	    player.turret = angleTo(player, lastAim);
   742	    fireShell(player, player.turret);
   743	    aimFlash = 0.28;
   744	  }
   745	
   746	  function pointerDown(e) {
   747	    e.preventDefault();
   748	    const p = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, sx: e.clientX, sy: e.clientY, start: performance.now(), mine: false };
   749	    if (state === 'fail') { resetRun(); return; }
   750	    if (isMineButton(p.x, p.y)) { dropMine(); p.mine = true; pointers.set(e.pointerId, p); return; }
   751	    pointers.set(e.pointerId, p);
   752	    if (p.y > H * 0.5) drivePointer = e.pointerId;
   753	    else fireAtScreen(p.x, p.y);
   754	    canvas.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);
   755	  }
   756	
   757	  function pointerMove(e) {
   758	    e.preventDefault();
   759	    const p = pointers.get(e.pointerId); if (!p) return;
   760	    p.x = e.clientX; p.y = e.clientY;
   761	  }
   762	
   763	  function pointerUp(e) {
   764	    e.preventDefault();
   765	    const p = pointers.get(e.pointerId); if (!p) return;
   766	    const moved = Math.hypot(p.x - p.sx, p.y - p.sy);
   767	    if (!p.mine && drivePointer === e.pointerId && moved < 16 && performance.now() - p.start < 360) fireAtScreen(p.x, p.y);
   768	    if (drivePointer === e.pointerId) drivePointer = null;
   769	    pointers.delete(e.pointerId);
   770	    canvas.releasePointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);
   771	  }
   772	
   773	  function keyDown(e) {
   774	    if (['ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight', ' ', 'Enter'].includes(e.key)) e.preventDefault();
   775	    if (state === 'fail' && (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ' || e.key.toLowerCase() === 'r')) { resetRun(); return; }
   776	    keys[e.key] = true;
   777	    keys[e.key.toLowerCase()] = true;
   778	    if (!e.repeat && (e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'Enter')) fireAtScreen(W * .5, H * .35);
   779	    if (!e.repeat && (e.key.toLowerCase() === 'm' || e.key.toLowerCase() === 'q')) dropMine();
   780	  }
   781	
   782	  function keyUp(e) { keys[e.key] = false; keys[e.key.toLowerCase()] = false; }
   783	
   784	  function tick(now) {
   785	    const dt = Math.min(0.034, Math.max(0.001, (now - lastTime) / 1000));
   786	    lastTime = now;
   787	    if (state === 'play') {
   788	      updatePlayer(dt);
   789	      for (const enemy of enemies) updateAI(enemy, dt);
   790	      updateShells(dt);
   791	      updateMines(dt);
   792	    } else if (state === 'clear') {
   793	      clearTimer -= dt;
   794	      if (clearTimer <= 0) { level++; state = 'play'; createLevel(); }
   795	    }
   796	    updateFx(dt);
   797	    draw();
   798	    requestAnimationFrame(tick);
   799	  }
   800	
   801	  resize();
   802	  window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
   803	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', pointerDown, { passive: false });
   804	  canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', pointerMove, { passive: false });
   805	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', pointerUp, { passive: false });
   806	  canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', pointerUp, { passive: false });
   807	  canvas.addEventListener('touchstart', e => e.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   808	  canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', e => e.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   809	  canvas.addEventListener('touchend', e => e.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   810	  window.addEventListener('keydown', keyDown, { passive: false });
   811	  window.addEventListener('keyup', keyUp);
   812	  resetRun();
   813	  requestAnimationFrame(tick);
   814	})();

Ironclad Alley is a portrait slow-tank duel against seeded AI arenas.
Drag the lower field to drive; drag direction controls tracks and turning.
Tap anywhere to aim and fire; shells ricochet twice off walls.
Tap the MINE button after collecting a mine pickup; keyboard: WASD/arrows, space, M.
Three lives per run; clear arenas to raise the level, with best score persisted locally.
