   208	    view.ox = (rect.width - W * view.scale) / 2;
   209	    view.oy = (rect.height - H * view.scale) / 2;
   210	    app.classList.toggle('landscape', rect.width > rect.height * 1.08);
   211	  }
   212	
   213	  function buildScoreStrip() {
   214	    scoreStrip.innerHTML = '';
   215	    for (let i = 0; i < 18; i++) {
   216	      const cell = document.createElement('div');
   217	      cell.className = 'score-cell';
   218	      cell.textContent = String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0');
   219	      cell.dataset.index = i;
   220	      scoreStrip.appendChild(cell);
   221	    }
   222	  }
   223	
   224	  function updateHUD() {
   225	    if (!hole || !ball) return;
   226	    holeStat.textContent = `${String(currentHole + 1).padStart(2, '0')}/18`;
   227	    strokeStat.textContent = `${hole.shotCount} · ${hole.par}`;
   228	    gimmickValue.textContent = hole.gimmick.name;
   229	    [...scoreStrip.children].forEach((cell, i) => {
   230	      cell.className = 'score-cell';
   231	      if (course.scores[i] != null) { cell.classList.add('done'); cell.textContent = course.scores[i]; }
   232	      else { cell.textContent = String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0'); }
   233	      if (i === currentHole && phase === 'play') cell.classList.add('current');
   234	    });
   235	    hint.textContent = ball.moving ? 'Ricochet in progress · watch the surfaces' : 'Drag from the orb · arrows aim · space shoots';
   236	  }
   237	
   238	  function frame(now) {
   239	    const raw = Math.min(0.05, Math.max(0, (now - lastFrame) / 1000));
   240	    lastFrame = now;
   241	    elapsed += raw;
   242	    update(raw);
   243	    render();
   244	    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   245	  }
   246	
   247	  function update(dt) {
   248	    if (toastTimer > 0) { toastTimer -= dt * 1000; if (toastTimer <= 0) toast.classList.remove('show'); }
   249	    shake = Math.max(0, shake - dt * 2.8);
   250	    particles.forEach(p => { p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt; p.vx *= Math.pow(.04, dt); p.vy *= Math.pow(.04, dt); p.life -= dt; });
   251	    particles = particles.filter(p => p.life > 0);
   252	    if (phase !== 'play' || !ball.moving) return;
   253	    const steps = Math.min(5, Math.max(1, Math.ceil(dt * 100)));
   254	    const step = dt / steps;
   255	    for (let i = 0; i < steps; i++) {
   256	      if (!ball.moving || phase !== 'play') break;
   257	      simulate(step);
   258	    }
   259	    updateHUD();
   260	  }
   261	
   262	  function simulate(dt) {
   263	    ball.shotTime += dt;
   264	    ball.boostCooldown = Math.max(0, ball.boostCooldown - dt);
   265	    ball.gimmickCooldown = Math.max(0, ball.gimmickCooldown - dt);
   266	    applyGimmick(dt);
   267	    const before = { x: ball.x, y: ball.y };
   268	    ball.x += ball.vx * dt;
   269	    ball.y += ball.vy * dt;
   270	    trail.push({ x: before.x, y: before.y, life: 1 });
   271	    if (trail.length > 17) trail.shift();
   272	    trail.forEach(p => p.life -= dt * 2.4);
   273	    const hit = collideWalls();
   274	    if (hit) { shake = Math.min(1, shake + .18); burst(ball.x, ball.y, hit === 'gate' ? palette.yellow : palette.mint, 4, 90); }
   275	    applySurfaces(dt);
   276	    checkBoosts();
   277	    if (distance(ball, hole.cup) < hole.cup.r + 4 && hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) < 620) { sinkBall(); return; }
   278	    const speed = hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
   279	    if (speed < 17 && ball.shotTime > .24) stopBall();
   280	  }
   281	
   282	  function collideWalls() {
   283	    let impact = null;
   284	    for (const seg of currentSegments()) {
   285	      const hit = collideSegment(seg);
   286	      if (hit) impact = seg.kind;
   287	    }
   288	    for (const b of hole.bumpers) {
   289	      const dx = ball.x - b.x;
   290	      const dy = ball.y - b.y;
   291	      const d = hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
   292	      const rr = ball.r + b.r;
   293	      if (d < rr) {
   294	        const nx = dx / d;
   295	        const ny = dy / d;
   296	        ball.x = b.x + nx * rr;
   297	        ball.y = b.y + ny * rr;
   298	        const toward = ball.vx * nx + ball.vy * ny;
   299	        if (toward < 0) { ball.vx -= 1.9 * toward * nx; ball.vy -= 1.9 * toward * ny; ball.vx *= .98; ball.vy *= .98; }
   300	        impact = 'bumper';
   301	      }
   302	    }
   303	    return impact;
   304	  }
   305	
   306	  function collideSegment(seg) {
   307	    const sx = seg.x2 - seg.x1;
   308	    const sy = seg.y2 - seg.y1;
   309	    const l2 = sx * sx + sy * sy || 1;
   310	    const t = clamp(((ball.x - seg.x1) * sx + (ball.y - seg.y1) * sy) / l2, 0, 1);
   311	    const px = seg.x1 + t * sx;
   312	    const py = seg.y1 + t * sy;
   313	    let nx = ball.x - px;
   314	    let ny = ball.y - py;
   315	    let d = hypot(nx, ny);
   316	    if (d < .001) { const n = unit(-sy, sx); nx = n.x; ny = n.y; d = 1; }
   317	    const radius = ball.r + (seg.kind === 'rail' ? 5 : 6);
   318	    if (d >= radius) return false;
   319	    nx /= d; ny /= d;
   320	    ball.x = px + nx * radius;
   321	    ball.y = py + ny * radius;
   322	    const dot = ball.vx * nx + ball.vy * ny;
   323	    if (dot < 0) {
   324	      const bounce = seg.kind === 'gate' ? 1.02 : 1.06;
   325	      ball.vx = (ball.vx - 2 * dot * nx) * bounce;
   326	      ball.vy = (ball.vy - 2 * dot * ny) * bounce;
   327	    }
   328	    return true;
   329	  }
   330	
   331	  function applySurfaces(dt) {
   332	    let drag = .42;
   333	    for (const r of hole.ice) if (inside(r, ball.x, ball.y)) drag = .985;
   334	    for (const r of hole.sand) if (inside(r, ball.x, ball.y)) drag = .08;
   335	    ball.vx *= Math.pow(drag, dt);
   336	    ball.vy *= Math.pow(drag, dt);
   337	  }
   338	
   339	  function applyGimmick(dt) {
   340	    const g = hole.gimmick;
   341	    if (!g) return;
   342	    if (g.type === 'vortex') {
   343	      const dx = g.x - ball.x, dy = g.y - ball.y, d = hypot(dx, dy);
   344	      if (d < g.r && d > 8) { const pull = (1 - d / g.r) * 270; ball.vx += dx / d * pull * dt; ball.vy += dy / d * pull * dt; }
   345	    } else if (g.type === 'wind') {
   346	      const d = hypot(ball.x - g.x, ball.y - g.y);
   347	      if (d < g.r) { const strength = (1 - d / g.r) * .9; ball.vx += g.wind.x * strength * dt; ball.vy += g.wind.y * strength * dt; }
   348	    } else if (g.type === 'magnet') {
   349	      const dx = g.x - ball.x, dy = g.y - ball.y, d = hypot(dx, dy);
   350	      if (d < g.r && d > 9) { const push = (d / g.r) * 200; ball.vx -= dx / d * push * dt; ball.vy -= dy / d * push * dt; }
   351	    } else if (g.type === 'portal' && g.cooldown <= 0) {
   352	      if (distance(ball, g.a) < g.a.r) { teleport(g.b); g.cooldown = 1; }
   353	      else if (distance(ball, g.b) < g.b.r) { teleport(g.a); g.cooldown = 1; }
   354	    } else if (g.type === 'bounce' && g.cooldown <= 0 && distance(ball, g) < g.r) {
   355	      const n = unit(ball.x - g.x, ball.y - g.y);
   356	      const dot = ball.vx * n.x + ball.vy * n.y;
   357	      const reflectedX = ball.vx - 2 * dot * n.x;
   358	      const reflectedY = ball.vy - 2 * dot * n.y;
   359	      ball.vx = reflectedX * 1.12;
   360	      ball.vy = reflectedY * 1.12;
   361	      g.cooldown = .35; burst(ball.x, ball.y, palette.violet, 18, 190); shake = .8;
   362	    } else if (g.type === 'slingshot' && g.cooldown <= 0 && distance(ball, g) < g.r) {
   363	      const n = unit(ball.x - g.x, ball.y - g.y);
   364	      ball.vx += n.x * 560; ball.vy += n.y * 560; g.cooldown = .6; burst(ball.x, ball.y, palette.coral, 18, 220); shake = .7;
   365	    }
   366	  }
   367	
   368	  function teleport(target) {
   369	    ball.x = target.x; ball.y = target.y; ball.vx *= 1.05; ball.vy *= 1.05; burst(target.x, target.y, palette.cyan, 20, 160); showToast('PHASE SHIFT', 650);
   370	  }
   371	
   372	  function inside(r, x, y) { return x >= r.x && x <= r.x + r.w && y >= r.y && y <= r.y + r.h; }
   373	
   374	  function checkBoosts() {
   375	    if (ball.boostCooldown > 0) return;
   376	    for (const pad of hole.boosts) {
   377	      if (inside(pad, ball.x, ball.y)) {
   378	        const direction = pad.angle ? { x: 0, y: -1 } : unit(ball.vx || aimDir.x, ball.vy || aimDir.y);
   379	        ball.vx += direction.x * 360; ball.vy += direction.y * 360; ball.boostCooldown = .5;
   380	        burst(ball.x, ball.y, palette.yellow, 18, 180); showToast('BOOST', 500); shake = .55;
   381	      }
   382	    }
   383	  }
   384	
   385	  function stopBall() {
   386	    ball.vx = 0; ball.vy = 0; ball.moving = false;
   387	    if (hole.shotCount >= 12) finishHole(Math.min(12, hole.par + 6), true);
   388	    updateHUD();
   389	  }
   390	
   391	  function sinkBall() {
   392	    if (!ball.moving) return;
   393	    ball.moving = false; ball.vx = 0; ball.vy = 0; ball.x = hole.cup.x; ball.y = hole.cup.y;
   394	    burst(ball.x, ball.y, palette.mint, 32, 240); shake = 1;
   395	    setTimeout(() => finishHole(null, false), 280);
   396	  }
   397	
   398	  function finishHole(forcedScore = null, forced = false) {
   399	    if (phase !== 'play') return;
   400	    phase = 'holeComplete';
   401	    const score = forcedScore == null ? hole.shotCount : forcedScore;
   402	    course.scores[currentHole] = score;
   403	    course.total = course.scores.reduce((sum, value) => sum + (value || 0), 0);
   404	    updateHUD();
   405	    const delta = score - hole.par;
   406	    modalEyebrow.textContent = forced ? 'CAP REACHED' : (delta <= 0 ? 'CLEAN LINE' : 'HOLE CLEARED');
   407	    modalTitle.textContent = forced ? 'The range moved on.' : (delta <= 0 ? 'Sweet pocket.' : 'Nice ricochet.');
   408	    modalCopy.textContent = forced ? 'The scorecard logged a +6 finish.' : `${hole.gimmick.name.toLowerCase()} survived your line.`;
   409	    result.innerHTML = `<div><strong>${score}</strong><span>strokes</span></div><div><strong>${fmtDelta(delta)}</strong><span>vs par</span></div><div><strong>${course.total}</strong><span>running</span></div>`;
   410	    action.textContent = currentHole === 17 ? 'SEE FINAL CARD' : 'NEXT HOLE';
   411	    bestLine.textContent = '';
   412	    modal.hidden = false;
   413	    showToast(delta <= 0 ? 'UNDER PAR' : `+${delta} ON THE CARD`, 850);
   414	  }
   415	
   416	  function finishCourse() {
   417	    phase = 'courseComplete';
   418	    const delta = course.total - course.parTotal;
   419	    const medal = delta <= 0 ? 'GOLD' : delta <= 8 ? 'SILVER' : 'BRONZE';
   420	    const best = readBest();
   421	    const isBest = !best || course.total < best.total;
   422	    if (isBest) writeBest({ total: course.total, par: course.parTotal, seed: course.seed });
   423	    modalEyebrow.textContent = '18-HOLE CARD';
   424	    modalTitle.textContent = `${medal} MEDAL`;
   425	    modalCopy.textContent = isBest ? 'New best card. The range remembers.' : 'The range is ready for another line.';
   426	    result.innerHTML = `<div><strong>${course.total}</strong><span>strokes</span></div><div><strong>${fmtDelta(delta)}</strong><span>vs par</span></div><div><strong>${course.parTotal}</strong><span>course par</span></div>`;
   427	    action.textContent = 'NEXT COURSE';
   428	    const record = isBest ? course.total : best.total;
   429	    bestLine.textContent = `BEST CARD · ${record} STROKES`;
   430	    modal.hidden = false;
   431	    updateHUD();
   432	  }
   433	
   434	  function onModalAction() {
   435	    if (phase === 'holeComplete') {
   436	      if (currentHole === 17) finishCourse();
   437	      else startHole(currentHole + 1);
   438	    } else if (phase === 'courseComplete') nextCourse();
   439	  }
   440	
   441	  function nextCourse() {
   442	    const seed = randomSeed();
   443	    try { localStorage.setItem('ricochet-range-last-seed', String(seed)); } catch (_) {}
   444	    course = createCourse(seed);
   445	    startHole(0);
   446	  }
   447	
   448	  function randomSeed() {
   449	    try { const a = new Uint32Array(1); crypto.getRandomValues(a); return a[0] || Date.now(); } catch (_) { return (Date.now() ^ Math.floor(Math.random() * 0xffffffff)) >>> 0; }
   450	  }
   451	
   452	  function readBest() { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(bestKey) || 'null'); } catch (_) { return null; } }
   453	  function writeBest(card) { try { localStorage.setItem(bestKey, JSON.stringify(card)); } catch (_) {} }
   454	
   455	  function showToast(text, duration) {
   456	    toast.textContent = text; toastTimer = duration; toast.classList.add('show');
   457	  }
   458	  function hideModal() { modal.hidden = true; }
   459	  function getWorld(clientX, clientY) {
   460	    const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
   461	    return { x: (clientX - r.left - view.ox) / view.scale - camera.x, y: (clientY - r.top - view.oy) / view.scale - camera.y };
   462	  }
   463	  function midpoint() {
   464	    const values = [...pointers.values()];
   465	    return { x: (values[0].x + values[1].x) / 2, y: (values[0].y + values[1].y) / 2 };
   466	  }
   467	  function onPointerDown(e) {
   468	    e.preventDefault();
   469	    canvas.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);
   470	    pointers.set(e.pointerId, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });
   471	    if (pointers.size >= 2) { aim = null; pan = { last: midpoint() }; return; }
   472	    if (phase !== 'play' || ball.moving) return;
   473	    const p = getWorld(e.clientX, e.clientY);
   474	    if (distance(p, ball) <= 72) { aim = { id: e.pointerId, point: p }; updateAim(p); }
   475	  }
   476	  function onPointerMove(e) {
   477	    e.preventDefault();
   478	    if (!pointers.has(e.pointerId)) return;
   479	    pointers.set(e.pointerId, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });
   480	    if (pointers.size >= 2) {
   481	      if (!pan) pan = { last: midpoint() };
   482	      const m = midpoint();
   483	      camera.x += (m.x - pan.last.x) / view.scale;
   484	      camera.y += (m.y - pan.last.y) / view.scale;
   485	      camera.x = clamp(camera.x, -90, 90); camera.y = clamp(camera.y, -100, 100);
   486	      pan.last = m; aim = null; return;
   487	    }
   488	    if (aim && aim.id === e.pointerId && phase === 'play' && !ball.moving) updateAim(getWorld(e.clientX, e.clientY));
   489	  }
   490	  function onPointerUp(e) {
   491	    e.preventDefault();
   492	    const wasAim = aim && aim.id === e.pointerId;
   493	    pointers.delete(e.pointerId);
   494	    if (pointers.size < 2) pan = null;
   495	    if (wasAim) { const p = getWorld(e.clientX, e.clientY); updateAim(p); if (aimPower > 16) shoot(aimDir, aimPower); aim = null; }
   496	  }
   497	  function updateAim(point) {
   498	    if (!ball) return;
   499	    const dx = ball.x - point.x, dy = ball.y - point.y;
   500	    const power = hypot(dx, dy);
   501	    if (power < 4) return;
   502	    aimPower = clamp(power * 1.15, 0, 360);
   503	    aimDir = unit(dx, dy);
   504	  }
   505	  function shoot(dir = aimDir, power = aimPower) {
   506	    if (phase !== 'play' || ball.moving || power < 16) return;
   507	    hole.shotCount += 1;
   508	    ball.vx = dir.x * (power * 4.15);
   509	    ball.vy = dir.y * (power * 4.15);
   510	    ball.moving = true; ball.shotTime = 0;
   511	    trail = [];
   512	    burst(ball.x, ball.y, palette.mint, 8, 90);
   513	    showToast(`SHOT ${hole.shotCount}`, 450);
   514	    updateHUD();
   515	  }
   516	  function onKeyDown(e) {
   517	    const key = e.key.toLowerCase();
   518	    if (['arrowup', 'arrowdown', 'arrowleft', 'arrowright', ' ', 'enter'].includes(key)) e.preventDefault();
   519	    if ((key === 'r' || key === 'escape') && phase !== 'courseComplete') { startHole(currentHole); return; }
   520	    if (key === 'enter' && phase !== 'play') { onModalAction(); return; }
   521	    if (phase !== 'play' || ball.moving) return;
   522	    const dirs = { arrowup: [0, -1], w: [0, -1], arrowdown: [0, 1], s: [0, 1], arrowleft: [-1, 0], a: [-1, 0], arrowright: [1, 0], d: [1, 0] };
   523	    if (dirs[key]) { aimDir = unit(dirs[key][0], dirs[key][1]); aimPower = 230; showToast('AIM LOCKED', 350); }
   524	    if (key === ' ' || key === 'enter') shoot(aimDir, aimPower);
   525	  }
   526	
   527	  function firstWallHit(origin, direction) {
   528	    let nearest = null;
   529	    for (const seg of currentSegments()) {
   530	      const hit = raySegment(origin, direction, seg);
   531	      if (hit && hit.t > 8 && (!nearest || hit.t < nearest.t)) nearest = { ...hit, seg };
   532	    }
   533	    return nearest;
   534	  }
   535	  function raySegment(o, d, s) {
   536	    const rx = d.x, ry = d.y;
   537	    const sx = s.x2 - s.x1, sy = s.y2 - s.y1;
   538	    const qx = s.x1 - o.x, qy = s.y1 - o.y;
   539	    const cross = rx * sy - ry * sx;
   540	    if (Math.abs(cross) < .0001) return null;
   541	    const t = (qx * sy - qy * sx) / cross;
   542	    const u = (qx * ry - qy * rx) / cross;
   543	    if (t >= 0 && u >= 0 && u <= 1) return { t, x: o.x + rx * t, y: o.y + ry * t };
   544	    return null;
   545	  }
   546	  function previewPath() {
   547	    const first = firstWallHit(ball, aimDir);
   548	    if (!first) return { first: { x: ball.x + aimDir.x * 260, y: ball.y + aimDir.y * 260 }, second: null };
   549	    const sx = first.seg.x2 - first.seg.x1, sy = first.seg.y2 - first.seg.y1;
   550	    const normal = unit(-sy, sx);
   551	    const dot = aimDir.x * normal.x + aimDir.y * normal.y;
   552	    const reflected = unit(aimDir.x - 2 * dot * normal.x, aimDir.y - 2 * dot * normal.y);
   553	    return { first, second: { x: first.x + reflected.x * 160, y: first.y + reflected.y * 160 }, reflected };
   554	  }
   555	
   556	  function burst(x, y, color, count = 10, force = 100) {
   557	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   558	      const a = Math.random() * TAU, s = force * (.35 + Math.random() * .65);
   559	      particles.push({ x, y, vx: Math.cos(a) * s, vy: Math.sin(a) * s, life: .34 + Math.random() * .42, max: .7, r: 2 + Math.random() * 3, color });
   560	    }
   561	    if (particles.length > 220) particles.splice(0, particles.length - 220);
   562	  }
   563	
   564	  function render() {
   565	    const dpr = view.dpr;
   566	    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
   567	    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, view.width, view.height);
   568	    const bg = ctx.createRadialGradient(view.width * .5, view.height * .4, 0, view.width * .5, view.height * .4, Math.max(view.width, view.height) * .75);
   569	    bg.addColorStop(0, '#103238'); bg.addColorStop(1, '#040d11');
   570	    ctx.fillStyle = bg; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, view.width, view.height);
   571	    const sx = (Math.random() - .5) * shake * 5;
   572	    const sy = (Math.random() - .5) * shake * 5;
   573	    ctx.save();
   574	    ctx.translate(view.ox + sx, view.oy + sy);
   575	    ctx.scale(view.scale, view.scale);
   576	    ctx.translate(camera.x, camera.y);
   577	    drawBoard();
   578	    drawHole();
   579	    ctx.restore();
   580	  }
   581	
   582	  function drawBoard() {
   583	    pathRoundRect(ctx, 24, 24, W - 48, H - 48, 42);
   584	    ctx.fillStyle = palette.board; ctx.fill();
   585	    ctx.save();
   586	    ctx.clip();
   587	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(154,249,213,.045)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
   588	    for (let x = 45; x < W - 20; x += 34) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, 20); ctx.lineTo(x, H - 20); ctx.stroke(); }
   589	    for (let y = 50; y < H; y += 34) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(20, y); ctx.lineTo(W - 20, y); ctx.stroke(); }
   590	    hole.stars.forEach(s => { ctx.globalAlpha = s.a; ctx.fillStyle = palette.mint; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, s.r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); });
   591	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   592	    ctx.restore();
   593	    pathRoundRect(ctx, 48, 58, W - 96, H - 116, 28);
   594	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(154,249,213,.32)'; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.setLineDash([12, 12]); ctx.stroke(); ctx.setLineDash([]);
   595	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(154,249,213,.5)'; ctx.font = '700 10px ui-sans-serif, sans-serif'; ctx.letterSpacing = '2px'; ctx.fillText(`RANGE // ${String(course.seed).slice(-4)}`, 70, 91);
   596	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(144,178,173,.5)'; ctx.fillText(`HOLE ${String(currentHole + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`, 630, 91);
   597	  }
   598	
   599	  function drawHole() {
   600	    drawSurfaceRects(hole.sand, palette.sand, 'SAND');
   601	    drawSurfaceRects(hole.ice, palette.ice, 'ICE');
   602	    hole.boosts.forEach(drawBoost);
   603	    drawGimmick(hole.gimmick);
   604	    currentSegments().forEach(seg => drawWall(seg));
   605	    hole.bumpers.forEach(drawBumper);
   606	    drawCup();
   607	    if (aim && !ball.moving && phase === 'play') drawAim();
   608	    trail.forEach((p, i) => { if (p.life <= 0) return; ctx.globalAlpha = p.life * .22; ctx.fillStyle = palette.mint; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, 6 + i * .35, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); });
   609	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   610	    particles.forEach(p => { ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(p.life / p.max, 0, 1); ctx.fillStyle = p.color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r * (0.7 + p.life), 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); });
   611	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   612	    drawBall();
   613	  }
   614	
   615	  function drawSurfaceRects(list, color, label) {
   616	    list.forEach(r => {
   617	      pathRoundRect(ctx, r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, 15);
   618	      const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(r.x, r.y, r.x + r.w, r.y + r.h);
   619	      g.addColorStop(0, color + '38'); g.addColorStop(1, color + '12');
   620	      ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = color + '8a'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke();
   621	      ctx.save(); ctx.clip(); ctx.strokeStyle = color + '35'; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   622	      for (let x = r.x - r.h; x < r.x + r.w; x += 12) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, r.y + r.h); ctx.lineTo(x + r.h, r.y); ctx.stroke(); }
   623	      ctx.restore();
   624	      ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.globalAlpha = .76; ctx.font = '800 9px ui-sans-serif, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText(label, r.x + 10, r.y + 18); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   625	    });
   626	  }
   627	
   628	  function drawBoost(r) {
   629	    pathRoundRect(ctx, r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, 10); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,214,110,.16)'; ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = palette.yellow; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke();
   630	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(r.x + r.w / 2, r.y + r.h / 2); ctx.rotate(r.angle); ctx.fillStyle = palette.yellow;
   631	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-25, 0); ctx.lineTo(-5, -12); ctx.lineTo(-5, -5); ctx.lineTo(25, -5); ctx.lineTo(25, 5); ctx.lineTo(-5, 5); ctx.lineTo(-5, 12); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   632	  }
   633	
   634	  function drawWall(s) {
   635	    ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.45)'; ctx.lineWidth = s.kind === 'rail' ? 16 : 18; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(s.x1, s.y1 + 5); ctx.lineTo(s.x2, s.y2 + 5); ctx.stroke();
   636	    ctx.strokeStyle = s.kind === 'gate' ? palette.yellow : s.kind === 'rail' ? palette.mint : palette.cyan; ctx.lineWidth = s.kind === 'rail' ? 8 : 10; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(s.x1, s.y1); ctx.lineTo(s.x2, s.y2); ctx.stroke();
   637	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.35)'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(s.x1, s.y1 - 2); ctx.lineTo(s.x2, s.y2 - 2); ctx.stroke();
   638	  }
   639	
   640	  function drawBumper(b) {
   641	    const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(b.x - b.r * .25, b.y - b.r * .3, 2, b.x, b.y, b.r);
   642	    grad.addColorStop(0, '#f0ffff'); grad.addColorStop(.18, palette.violet); grad.addColorStop(1, '#443e78');
   643	    ctx.shadowColor = palette.violet; ctx.shadowBlur = 16; ctx.fillStyle = grad; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   644	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.5)'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke();
   645	  }
   646	
   647	  function drawGimmick(g) {
   648	    if (!g) return;
   649	    ctx.save();
   650	    if (g.type === 'portal') { drawPortal(g.a, palette.cyan); drawPortal(g.b, palette.coral); }
   651	    else {
   652	      const color = g.type === 'vortex' ? palette.cyan : g.type === 'wind' ? palette.yellow : g.type === 'magnet' ? palette.coral : g.type === 'bounce' ? palette.violet : palette.coral;
   653	      const x = g.x, y = g.y;
   654	      ctx.globalAlpha = .11; ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, g.r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.globalAlpha = .75; ctx.strokeStyle = color; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.setLineDash([7, 10]); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, g.r, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke(); ctx.setLineDash([]);
   655	      ctx.globalAlpha = .7; ctx.strokeStyle = color; ctx.lineWidth = 3;
   656	      if (g.type === 'vortex') for (let r = g.r - 18; r > 15; r -= 21) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, r, elapsed * (1 + r / 90), elapsed * (1 + r / 90) + Math.PI * 1.35); ctx.stroke(); }
   657	      if (g.type === 'wind') { for (let i = -1; i <= 1; i++) { const yy = y + i * 30; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x - 58, yy); ctx.lineTo(x + 40, yy + g.wind.y * .1); ctx.lineTo(x + 29, yy + g.wind.y * .1 - 7); ctx.moveTo(x + 40, yy + g.wind.y * .1); ctx.lineTo(x + 29, yy + g.wind.y * .1 + 7); ctx.stroke(); } }
   658	      if (g.type === 'magnet') { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, 25, Math.PI * .15, Math.PI * .85); ctx.stroke(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, 25, Math.PI * 1.15, Math.PI * 1.85); ctx.stroke(); }
   659	      if (g.type === 'bounce' || g.type === 'slingshot') { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, 22 + Math.sin(elapsed * 5) * 5, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x - 12, y); ctx.lineTo(x + 12, y); ctx.moveTo(x, y - 12); ctx.lineTo(x, y + 12); ctx.stroke(); }
   660	      ctx.globalAlpha = .8; ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.font = '800 9px ui-sans-serif, sans-serif'; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.fillText(g.name, x, y + g.r + 17); ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   661	    }
   662	    ctx.restore();
   663	  }
   664	
   665	  function drawPortal(p, color) {
   666	    ctx.globalAlpha = .13; ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r + 10, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.globalAlpha = .9; ctx.strokeStyle = color; ctx.lineWidth = 5; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r + Math.sin(elapsed * 4) * 3, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke(); ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r - 10, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
   667	  }
   668	
   669	  function drawCup() {
   670	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.55)'; ctx.lineWidth = 8; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(hole.cup.x, hole.cup.y, hole.cup.r, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
   671	    ctx.fillStyle = '#031013'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(hole.cup.x, hole.cup.y, hole.cup.r - 5, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   672	    ctx.strokeStyle = palette.yellow; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(hole.cup.x, hole.cup.y, hole.cup.r + 4, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
   673	    ctx.strokeStyle = palette.yellow; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(hole.cup.x + 2, hole.cup.y - 2); ctx.lineTo(hole.cup.x + 2, hole.cup.y - 60); ctx.stroke();
   674	    ctx.fillStyle = palette.yellow; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(hole.cup.x + 3, hole.cup.y - 58); ctx.lineTo(hole.cup.x + 35, hole.cup.y - 48); ctx.lineTo(hole.cup.x + 3, hole.cup.y - 37); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
   675	  }
   676	
   677	  function drawAim() {
   678	    const p = aim.point;
   679	    const drag = hypot(ball.x - p.x, ball.y - p.y);
   680	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(154,249,213,.5)'; ctx.lineWidth = 4; ctx.setLineDash([10, 10]); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ball.x, ball.y); ctx.lineTo(p.x, p.y); ctx.stroke(); ctx.setLineDash([]);
   681	    ctx.strokeStyle = palette.mint; ctx.lineWidth = 4; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ball.x, ball.y); ctx.lineTo(ball.x + aimDir.x * Math.min(290, aimPower * 1.1), ball.y + aimDir.y * Math.min(290, aimPower * 1.1)); ctx.stroke();
   682	    const preview = previewPath();
   683	    ctx.globalAlpha = .65; ctx.strokeStyle = palette.yellow; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.setLineDash([4, 10]); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ball.x, ball.y); ctx.lineTo(preview.first.x, preview.first.y); if (preview.second) ctx.lineTo(preview.second.x, preview.second.y); ctx.stroke(); ctx.setLineDash([]); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   684	    ctx.strokeStyle = palette.yellow; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, 25 + clamp(drag, 0, 160) * .18, -Math.PI / 2, -Math.PI / 2 + TAU * clamp(drag / 250, 0, 1)); ctx.stroke();
   685	  }
   686	
   687	  function drawBall() {
   688	    if (!ball) return;
   689	    const glow = ctx.createRadialGradient(ball.x - 5, ball.y - 6, 1, ball.x, ball.y, 30);
   690	    glow.addColorStop(0, '#ffffff'); glow.addColorStop(.23, palette.mint); glow.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(154,249,213,0)');
   691	    ctx.fillStyle = glow; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, 31, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   692	    const ballGrad = ctx.createRadialGradient(ball.x - 5, ball.y - 6, 2, ball.x, ball.y, ball.r);
   693	    ballGrad.addColorStop(0, '#ffffff'); ballGrad.addColorStop(.35, '#d4fff0'); ballGrad.addColorStop(1, '#48b991');
   694	    ctx.fillStyle = ballGrad; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, ball.r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   695	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#e9fff6'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.stroke();
   696	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.85)'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ball.x - 5, ball.y - 6, 3, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   697	  }
   698	
   699	  init();
   700	})();

Drag from the ball away from the target, then release to shoot; the dotted line previews the first ricochet.
Two-finger drag pans the hole. Keyboard: arrows/WASD aim, Space/Enter shoot, R restart the hole.
Each course has 18 seeded holes with par, moving gates, sand, ice, boosts, and one gimmick per hole.
At 12 strokes a hole auto-finishes at +6; after hole 18, choose NEXT COURSE to reseed.
Best 18-hole card is persisted locally in the browser.

   391	  function sinkBall() {
   392	    if (!ball.moving) return;
   393	    ball.moving = false; ball.vx = 0; ball.vy = 0; ball.x = hole.cup.x; ball.y = hole.cup.y;
   394	    burst(ball.x, ball.y, palette.mint, 32, 240); shake = 1;
   395	    setTimeout(() => finishHole(null, false), 280);
   396	  }

   398	  function finishHole(forcedScore = null, forced = false) {
   399	    if (phase !== 'play') return;
   400	    phase = 'holeComplete';
   401	    const score = forcedScore == null ? hole.shotCount : forcedScore;
   402	    course.scores[currentHole] = score;
   403	    course.total = course.scores.reduce((sum, value) => sum + (value || 0), 0);
   404	    updateHUD();
   405	    const delta = score - hole.par;
   406	    modalEyebrow.textContent = forced ? 'CAP REACHED' : (delta <= 0 ? 'CLEAN LINE' : 'HOLE CLEARED');
   407	    modalTitle.textContent = forced ? 'The range moved on.' : (delta <= 0 ? 'Sweet pocket.' : 'Nice ricochet.');
   408	    modalCopy.textContent = forced ? 'The scorecard logged a +6 finish.' : `${hole.gimmick.name.toLowerCase()} survived your line.`;
   409	    result.innerHTML = `<div><strong>${score}</strong><span>strokes</span></div><div><strong>${fmtDelta(delta)}</strong><span>vs par</span></div><div><strong>${course.total}</strong><span>running</span></div>`;
   410	    action.textContent = currentHole === 17 ? 'SEE FINAL CARD' : 'NEXT HOLE';
   411	    bestLine.textContent = '';
   412	    modal.hidden = false;
   413	    showToast(delta <= 0 ? 'UNDER PAR' : `+${delta} ON THE CARD`, 850);
   414	  }
   415	
   416	  function finishCourse() {
   417	    phase = 'courseComplete';
   418	    const delta = course.total - course.parTotal;
   419	    const medal = delta <= 0 ? 'GOLD' : delta <= 8 ? 'SILVER' : 'BRONZE';
   420	    const best = readBest();
   421	    const isBest = !best || course.total < best.total;
   422	    if (isBest) writeBest({ total: course.total, par: course.parTotal, seed: course.seed });
   423	    modalEyebrow.textContent = '18-HOLE CARD';
   424	    modalTitle.textContent = `${medal} MEDAL`;
   425	    modalCopy.textContent = isBest ? 'New best card. The range remembers.' : 'The range is ready for another line.';
   426	    result.innerHTML = `<div><strong>${course.total}</strong><span>strokes</span></div><div><strong>${fmtDelta(delta)}</strong><span>vs par</span></div><div><strong>${course.parTotal}</strong><span>course par</span></div>`;
   427	    action.textContent = 'NEXT COURSE';
   428	    const record = isBest ? course.total : best.total;
   429	    bestLine.textContent = `BEST CARD · ${record} STROKES`;
   430	    modal.hidden = false;
   431	    updateHUD();
   432	  }
   433	
   434	  function onModalAction() {
   435	    if (phase === 'holeComplete') {
   436	      if (currentHole === 17) finishCourse();
   437	      else startHole(currentHole + 1);
   438	    } else if (phase === 'courseComplete') nextCourse();
   439	  }
   440	
   441	  function nextCourse() {
   442	    const seed = randomSeed();
   443	    try { localStorage.setItem('ricochet-range-last-seed', String(seed)); } catch (_) {}
   444	    course = createCourse(seed);
   445	    startHole(0);
   446	  }
   447	
   448	  function randomSeed() {
   449	    try { const a = new Uint32Array(1); crypto.getRandomValues(a); return a[0] || Date.now(); } catch (_) { return (Date.now() ^ Math.floor(Math.random() * 0xffffffff)) >>> 0; }
   450	  }
   451	
   452	  function readBest() { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(bestKey) || 'null'); } catch (_) { return null; } }
   453	  function writeBest(card) { try { localStorage.setItem(bestKey, JSON.stringify(card)); } catch (_) {} }
   454	
   455	  function showToast(text, duration) {
   456	    toast.textContent = text; toastTimer = duration; toast.classList.add('show');
   457	  }
   458	  function hideModal() { modal.hidden = true; }
   459	  function getWorld(clientX, clientY) {
   460	    const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
   461	    return { x: (clientX - r.left - view.ox) / view.scale - camera.x, y: (clientY - r.top - view.oy) / view.scale - camera.y };
   462	  }
   463	  function midpoint() {
   464	    const values = [...pointers.values()];
   465	    return { x: (values[0].x + values[1].x) / 2, y: (values[0].y + values[1].y) / 2 };
   466	  }
   467	  function onPointerDown(e) {
   468	    e.preventDefault();
   469	    canvas.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);
   470	    pointers.set(e.pointerId, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });
   471	    if (pointers.size >= 2) { aim = null; pan = { last: midpoint() }; return; }
   472	    if (phase !== 'play' || ball.moving) return;
   473	    const p = getWorld(e.clientX, e.clientY);
   474	    if (distance(p, ball) <= 72) { aim = { id: e.pointerId, point: p }; updateAim(p); }
   475	  }
   476	  function onPointerMove(e) {
   477	    e.preventDefault();
   478	    if (!pointers.has(e.pointerId)) return;
   479	    pointers.set(e.pointerId, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });
   480	    if (pointers.size >= 2) {
   481	      if (!pan) pan = { last: midpoint() };
   482	      const m = midpoint();
   483	      camera.x += (m.x - pan.last.x) / view.scale;
   484	      camera.y += (m.y - pan.last.y) / view.scale;
   485	      camera.x = clamp(camera.x, -90, 90); camera.y = clamp(camera.y, -100, 100);
   486	      pan.last = m; aim = null; return;
   487	    }
   488	    if (aim && aim.id === e.pointerId && phase === 'play' && !ball.moving) updateAim(getWorld(e.clientX, e.clientY));
   489	  }
   490	  function onPointerUp(e) {
   491	    e.preventDefault();
   492	    const wasAim = aim && aim.id === e.pointerId;
   493	    pointers.delete(e.pointerId);
   494	    if (pointers.size < 2) pan = null;
   495	    if (wasAim) { const p = getWorld(e.clientX, e.clientY); updateAim(p); if (aimPower > 16) shoot(aimDir, aimPower); aim = null; }
   496	  }
   497	  function updateAim(point) {
   498	    if (!ball) return;
   499	    const dx = ball.x - point.x, dy = ball.y - point.y;
   500	    const power = hypot(dx, dy);
   501	    if (power < 4) return;
   502	    aimPower = clamp(power * 1.15, 0, 360);
   503	    aimDir = unit(dx, dy);
   504	  }
   505	  function shoot(dir = aimDir, power = aimPower) {
   506	    if (phase !== 'play' || ball.moving || power < 16) return;
   507	    hole.shotCount += 1;
   508	    ball.vx = dir.x * (power * 4.15);
   509	    ball.vy = dir.y * (power * 4.15);
   510	    ball.moving = true; ball.shotTime = 0;
   511	    trail = [];
   512	    burst(ball.x, ball.y, palette.mint, 8, 90);
   513	    showToast(`SHOT ${hole.shotCount}`, 450);
   514	    updateHUD();
   515	  }
   516	  function onKeyDown(e) {
   517	    const key = e.key.toLowerCase();
   518	    if (['arrowup', 'arrowdown', 'arrowleft', 'arrowright', ' ', 'enter'].includes(key)) e.preventDefault();
   519	    if ((key === 'r' || key === 'escape') && phase !== 'courseComplete') { startHole(currentHole); return; }
   520	    if (key === 'enter' && phase !== 'play') { onModalAction(); return; }
   521	    if (phase !== 'play' || ball.moving) return;
   522	    const dirs = { arrowup: [0, -1], w: [0, -1], arrowdown: [0, 1], s: [0, 1], arrowleft: [-1, 0], a: [-1, 0], arrowright: [1, 0], d: [1, 0] };
   523	    if (dirs[key]) { aimDir = unit(dirs[key][0], dirs[key][1]); aimPower = 230; showToast('AIM LOCKED', 350); }
   524	    if (key === ' ' || key === 'enter') shoot(aimDir, aimPower);
   525	  }
   526	
   527	  function firstWallHit(origin, direction) {
   528	    let nearest = null;
   529	    for (const seg of currentSegments()) {
   530	      const hit = raySegment(origin, direction, seg);
   531	      if (hit && hit.t > 8 && (!nearest || hit.t < nearest.t)) nearest = { ...hit, seg };
   532	    }
   533	    return nearest;
   534	  }
   535	  function raySegment(o, d, s) {
   536	    const rx = d.x, ry = d.y;
   537	    const sx = s.x2 - s.x1, sy = s.y2 - s.y1;
   538	    const qx = s.x1 - o.x, qy = s.y1 - o.y;
   539	    const cross = rx * sy - ry * sx;
   540	    if (Math.abs(cross) < .0001) return null;
   541	    const t = (qx * sy - qy * sx) / cross;
   542	    const u = (qx * ry - qy * rx) / cross;
   543	    if (t >= 0 && u >= 0 && u <= 1) return { t, x: o.x + rx * t, y: o.y + ry * t };
   544	    return null;
   545	  }
   546	  function previewPath() {
   547	    const first = firstWallHit(ball, aimDir);
   548	    if (!first) return { first: { x: ball.x + aimDir.x * 260, y: ball.y + aimDir.y * 260 }, second: null };
   549	    const sx = first.seg.x2 - first.seg.x1, sy = first.seg.y2 - first.seg.y1;
   550	    const normal = unit(-sy, sx);
   551	    const dot = aimDir.x * normal.x + aimDir.y * normal.y;
   552	    const reflected = unit(aimDir.x - 2 * dot * normal.x, aimDir.y - 2 * dot * normal.y);
   553	    return { first, second: { x: first.x + reflected.x * 160, y: first.y + reflected.y * 160 }, reflected };
   554	  }
   555	
   556	  function burst(x, y, color, count = 10, force = 100) {
   557	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   558	      const a = Math.random() * TAU, s = force * (.35 + Math.random() * .65);
   559	      particles.push({ x, y, vx: Math.cos(a) * s, vy: Math.sin(a) * s, life: .34 + Math.random() * .42, max: .7, r: 2 + Math.random() * 3, color });
   560	    }
   561	    if (particles.length > 220) particles.splice(0, particles.length - 220);
   562	  }

    77	  function readBest() {
    78	    try { return Number(localStorage.getItem('horde-meridian-best') || 0); } catch (error) { return 0; }
    79	  }

    81	  function saveBest(value) {
    82	    try { localStorage.setItem('horde-meridian-best', String(value)); } catch (error) { /* local storage is optional */ }
    83	  }

    85	  function resetSeed() {
    86	    seed = 0x4d657269;
    87	  }

    89	  function random() {
    90	    seed |= 0;
    91	    seed = (seed + 0x6D2B79F5) | 0;
    92	    let t = Math.imul(seed ^ seed >>> 15, 1 | seed);
    93	    t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, 61 | t)) ^ t;
    94	    return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    95	  }
    96	
    97	  function clamp(value, min, max) {
    98	    return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));
    99	  }
   100	
   101	  function distanceSquared(ax, ay, bx, by) {
   102	    const dx = ax - bx;
   103	    const dy = ay - by;
   104	    return dx * dx + dy * dy;
   105	  }

   107	  function formatTime(seconds) {
   108	    const total = Math.max(0, Math.floor(seconds));
   109	    const minutes = String(Math.floor(total / 60)).padStart(2, '0');
   110	    const secs = String(total % 60).padStart(2, '0');
   111	    return minutes + ':' + secs;
   112	  }

   114	  function resize() {
   115	    const rect = shell.getBoundingClientRect();
   116	    width = Math.max(1, rect.width);
   117	    height = Math.max(1, rect.height);
   118	    const deviceScale = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
   119	    renderScale = Math.min(deviceScale, 960 / Math.max(width, height));
   120	    canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.floor(width * renderScale));
   121	    canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.floor(height * renderScale));
   122	    ctx.setTransform(renderScale, 0, 0, renderScale, 0, 0);
   123	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;
   124	    updateOrientation();
   125	  }
   126	
   127	  function updateOrientation() {
   128	    rotate.hidden = window.innerWidth <= window.innerHeight;
   129	  }

   131	  function seedField() {
   132	    fieldMarks = [];
   133	    resetSeed();
   134	    for (let i = 0; i < 170; i += 1) {
   135	      fieldMarks.push({
   136	        x: (random() - 0.5) * WORLD * 1.25,
   137	        y: (random() - 0.5) * WORLD * 1.25,
   138	        r: 2 + random() * 6,
   139	        kind: Math.floor(random() * 3),
   140	        hue: random()
   141	      });
   142	    }
   143	  }

   147	    enemies = [];
   148	    gems = [];
   149	    shots = [];
   150	    pulses = [];
   151	    particles = [];
   152	    enemyId = 1;
   153	    player = {

   160	      magnet: 92,
   161	      damage: 1,
   162	      orbit: 0,
   163	      lance: 1,
   164	      pulse: 0,
   165	      wisp: 0,
   166	      fireClock: 0.2,
   167	      wispClock: 0.6,
   168	      pulseClock: 1,
   169	      orbitAngle: 0,
   170	      orbitClock: 0

   204	  function say(text, color) {
   205	    run.toast = { text: text, color: color || '#baf9db', life: 2.1 };

   208	  function getInputVector() {
   209	    let x = 0;
   210	    let y = 0;
   211	    if (input.keys.has('a') || input.keys.has('arrowleft')) x -= 1;
   212	    if (input.keys.has('d') || input.keys.has('arrowright')) x += 1;
   213	    if (input.keys.has('w') || input.keys.has('arrowup')) y -= 1;
   214	    if (input.keys.has('s') || input.keys.has('arrowdown')) y += 1;
   215	    if (x || y) {
   216	      const length = Math.sqrt(x * x + y * y) || 1;
   217	      return { x: x / length, y: y / length };
   218	    }
   219	    return { x: input.dx, y: input.dy };
   220	  }
   221	
   222	  function localPointer(event) {
   223	    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
   224	    return { x: event.clientX - rect.left, y: event.clientY - rect.top };
   225	  }
   226	
   227	  function pointerDown(event) {
   228	    if (run.mode !== 'playing' || input.active) return;
   229	    const point = localPointer(event);
   230	    input.pointerId = event.pointerId;
   231	    input.active = true;
   232	    input.baseX = point.x;
   233	    input.baseY = point.y;
   234	    input.dx = 0;
   235	    input.dy = 0;
   236	    try { canvas.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId); } catch (error) { /* older browsers */ }
   237	    event.preventDefault();
   238	  }
   239	
   240	  function pointerMove(event) {
   241	    if (!input.active || event.pointerId !== input.pointerId) return;
   242	    const point = localPointer(event);
   243	    const maxDistance = 70;
   244	    let x = point.x - input.baseX;
   245	    let y = point.y - input.baseY;
   246	    const length = Math.sqrt(x * x + y * y) || 1;
   247	    if (length > maxDistance) {
   248	      x = x / length * maxDistance;
   249	      y = y / length * maxDistance;
   250	    }
   251	    input.dx = x / maxDistance;
   252	    input.dy = y / maxDistance;
   253	    event.preventDefault();
   254	  }
   255	
   256	  function pointerUp(event) {
   257	    if (event.pointerId !== input.pointerId) return;
   258	    input.active = false;
   259	    input.pointerId = null;
   260	    input.dx = 0;
   261	    input.dy = 0;
   262	    event.preventDefault();
   263	  }
   264	
   265	  function keyDown(event) {
   266	    const key = event.key.toLowerCase();
   267	    if (['w', 'a', 's', 'd', 'arrowup', 'arrowdown', 'arrowleft', 'arrowright', ' ', 'enter'].includes(key)) {
   268	      input.keys.add(key);
   269	      event.preventDefault();
   270	    }
   271	    if ((key === ' ' || key === 'enter') && (run.mode === 'dead' || run.mode === 'won')) resetRun();
   272	  }
   273	
   274	  function keyUp(event) {
   275	    input.keys.delete(event.key.toLowerCase());
   276	  }
   277	
   278	  function spawnEnemy(requestedType, elite, location) {
   279	    if (enemies.length > 180 && requestedType !== 'boss') return null;
   280	    let type = requestedType;
   281	    if (!type) {
   282	      const roll = random();
   283	      const late = run.time / 600;
   284	      if (roll < 0.48 - late * 0.1) type = 'drifter';
   285	      else if (roll < 0.74) type = 'sprinter';
   286	      else if (roll < 0.9) type = 'tank';
   287	      else type = 'exploder';
   288	    }
   289	    const templates = {
   290	      drifter: { r: 13, hp: 4, speed: 35, damage: 8, value: 1, color: '#65d5c3' },
   291	      sprinter: { r: 10, hp: 6, speed: 82, damage: 11, value: 1, color: '#ffc361' },
   292	      tank: { r: 20, hp: 27, speed: 25, damage: 18, value: 3, color: '#a8a8e8' },
   293	      exploder: { r: 14, hp: 10, speed: 54, damage: 26, value: 2, color: '#ff756a' },
   294	      boss: { r: 52, hp: 1850, speed: 22, damage: 38, value: 0, color: '#d4a1ff' }
   295	    };
   296	    const base = templates[type] || templates.drifter;
   297	    const eliteFactor = elite ? 3.1 : 1;
   298	    const difficulty = 1 + run.time / 520;
   299	    let x;
   300	    let y;
   301	    if (location) {
   302	      x = location.x;
   303	      y = location.y;
   304	    } else {
   305	      const angle = random() * TAU;
   306	      const radius = type === 'boss' ? 545 : 430 + random() * 260;
   307	      x = player.x + Math.cos(angle) * radius;
   308	      y = player.y + Math.sin(angle) * radius;
   309	    }
   310	    const edge = WORLD / 2 - 35;
   311	    const enemy = {
   312	      id: enemyId++,
   313	      type: type,
   314	      elite: Boolean(elite),
   315	      boss: type === 'boss',
   316	      x: clamp(x, -edge, edge),
   317	      y: clamp(y, -edge, edge),
   318	      r: base.r * (elite ? 1.24 : 1),
   319	      hp: base.hp * difficulty * eliteFactor,
   320	      maxHp: base.hp * difficulty * eliteFactor,
   321	      speed: base.speed * (type === 'boss' ? 1 : difficulty * (elite ? .93 : 1)),
   322	      damage: base.damage * (elite ? 1.25 : 1),
   323	      value: base.value * (elite ? 4 : 1),
   324	      color: base.color,
   325	      phase: random() * TAU,
   326	      hitFlash: 0,
   327	      attackClock: random() * .3
   328	    };
   329	    enemies.push(enemy);
   330	    return enemy;
   331	  }
   332	
   333	  function spawnBurst(count) {
   334	    const amount = Math.min(count, 18);
   335	    for (let i = 0; i < amount; i += 1) spawnEnemy();
   336	  }
   337	
   338	  function spawnElite(minute) {
   339	    const type = minute % 3 === 0 ? 'exploder' : (minute % 2 ? 'tank' : 'sprinter');
   340	    spawnEnemy(type, true);
   341	    spawnBurst(3 + Math.min(5, minute));
   342	    run.shake = Math.max(run.shake, 6);
   343	    say('ELITE SPIKE · MINUTE ' + minute, '#ffc86b');
   344	  }
   345	
   346	  function spawnBoss() {
   347	    run.bossSpawned = true;
   348	    spawnEnemy('boss', true);
   349	    spawnBurst(10);
   350	    run.shake = 18;
   351	    say('THE MERIDIAN CORE DESCENDS', '#ff897e');
   352	    setHint('BREAK THE CORE · KEEP MOVING');
   353	  }
   354	
   355	  function nearestEnemy(maxDistance) {
   356	    let found = null;
   357	    let best = maxDistance * maxDistance;
   358	    for (const enemy of enemies) {
   359	      const d = distanceSquared(player.x, player.y, enemy.x, enemy.y);
   360	      if (d < best) { best = d; found = enemy; }
   361	    }
   362	    return found;
   363	  }
   364	
   365	  function updatePlayer(dt) {
   366	    const vector = getInputVector();
   367	    const length = Math.sqrt(vector.x * vector.x + vector.y * vector.y);
   368	    if (length > 0.02) {
   369	      const speed = player.speed * Math.min(1, length);
   370	      player.x += vector.x / length * speed * dt;
   371	      player.y += vector.y / length * speed * dt;
   372	      run.moveSpark -= dt;
   373	      if (run.moveSpark <= 0) {
   374	        run.moveSpark = .07;
   375	        spawnParticle(player.x - vector.x * 8, player.y - vector.y * 8, '#5fbda7', 1, 12, 2.2);
   376	      }
   377	    }
   378	    const edge = WORLD / 2 - 38;
   379	    player.x = clamp(player.x, -edge, edge);
   380	    player.y = clamp(player.y, -edge, edge);
   381	  }
   382	
   383	  function updateSpawning(dt) {
   384	    run.spawnClock -= dt;
   385	    if (run.spawnClock > 0) return;
   386	    const pressure = Math.min(1, run.time / 600);
   387	    const pack = random() < .16 + pressure * .2 ? 2 : 1;
   388	    spawnBurst(pack);
   389	    run.spawnClock = Math.max(.18, 1.08 - pressure * .78) * (.75 + random() * .35);
   390	  }
   391	
   392	  function updateMilestones() {
   393	    if (run.time >= run.nextWave) {
   394	      run.wave += 1;
   395	      run.nextWave += 30;
   396	      spawnBurst(4 + Math.min(8, run.wave));
   397	      say('WAVE ' + String(run.wave).padStart(2, '0'), '#8edfff');
   398	    }
   399	    const minute = Math.floor(run.time / 60);
   400	    if (minute > run.lastMinute && minute < 10) {
   401	      run.lastMinute = minute;
   402	      spawnElite(minute);
   403	    }
   404	    if (run.time >= 600 && !run.bossSpawned) spawnBoss();
   405	  }
   406	
   407	  function updateWeapons(dt) {
   408	    const target = nearestEnemy(820);
   409	    const damage = player.damage;
   410	    player.fireClock -= dt;
   411	    if (player.lance > 0 && player.fireClock <= 0) {
   412	      if (target) {
   413	        const angle = Math.atan2(target.y - player.y, target.x - player.x);
   414	        const extra = Math.min(2, Math.floor((player.lance - 1) / 3));
   415	        for (let i = 0; i <= extra; i += 1) {
   416	          const spread = (i - extra / 2) * .13;
   417	          const shotAngle = angle + spread;
   418	          shots.push({
   419	            kind: 'lance',
   420	            x: player.x,
   421	            y: player.y,
   422	            vx: Math.cos(shotAngle) * 500,
   423	            vy: Math.sin(shotAngle) * 500,
   424	            r: 4,
   425	            life: 1.7,
   426	            damage: 8 * damage * (1 + player.lance * .16)
   427	          });
   428	          spawnParticle(player.x + Math.cos(shotAngle) * 12, player.y + Math.sin(shotAngle) * 12, '#b6fff0', 1, 24, 2.5);
   429	        }
   430	      }
   431	      player.fireClock = Math.max(.18, .62 - player.lance * .045);
   432	    }
   433	    player.wispClock -= dt;
   434	    if (player.wisp > 0 && player.wispClock <= 0 && target) {
   435	      const angle = Math.atan2(target.y - player.y, target.x - player.x);
   436	      shots.push({
   437	        kind: 'wisp',
   438	        x: player.x + Math.cos(angle) * 18,
   439	        y: player.y + Math.sin(angle) * 18,
   440	        vx: Math.cos(angle) * 220,
   441	        vy: Math.sin(angle) * 220,
   442	        r: 7,
   443	        life: 3.8,
   444	        damage: 12 * damage * (1 + player.wisp * .22)
   445	      });
   446	      player.wispClock = Math.max(.4, 1.3 - player.wisp * .08);
   447	    }
   448	    player.pulseClock -= dt;
   449	    if (player.pulse > 0 && player.pulseClock <= 0) {
   450	      pulses.push({ r: 0, max: 125 + player.pulse * 20, age: 0, damage: 15 * damage * (1 + player.pulse * .16), hit: new Set() });
   451	      player.pulseClock = Math.max(.9, 2.5 - player.pulse * .13);
   452	      run.shake = Math.max(run.shake, 4);
   453	    }
   454	    if (player.orbit > 0) {
   455	      player.orbitAngle += dt * (1.2 + player.orbit * .12);
   456	      player.orbitClock -= dt;
   457	      if (player.orbitClock <= 0) {
   458	        const count = Math.min(7, 1 + player.orbit);
   459	        for (let i = 0; i < count; i += 1) {
   460	          const angle = player.orbitAngle + i * TAU / count;
   461	          const bladeX = player.x + Math.cos(angle) * (38 + player.orbit * 3);
   462	          const bladeY = player.y + Math.sin(angle) * (38 + player.orbit * 3);
   463	          for (const enemy of enemies) {
   464	            if (distanceSquared(bladeX, bladeY, enemy.x, enemy.y) < (enemy.r + 11) * (enemy.r + 11) && (enemy.orbitHit || 0) < run.time - .22) {
   465	              enemy.orbitHit = run.time;
   466	              damageEnemy(enemy, 13 * damage * (1 + player.orbit * .2));
   467	            }
   468	          }
   469	        }
   470	        player.orbitClock = .08;
   471	      }
   472	    }
   473	  }
   474	
   475	  function updatePulses(dt) {
   476	    for (let i = pulses.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
   477	      const pulse = pulses[i];
   478	      pulse.age += dt;
   479	      pulse.r += (pulse.max / .48) * dt;
   480	      for (const enemy of enemies) {
   481	        if (!pulse.hit.has(enemy.id) && distanceSquared(player.x, player.y, enemy.x, enemy.y) <= (pulse.r + enemy.r) * (pulse.r + enemy.r)) {
   482	          pulse.hit.add(enemy.id);
   483	          damageEnemy(enemy, pulse.damage);
   484	        }
   485	      }
   486	      if (pulse.r >= pulse.max || pulse.age > .65) pulses.splice(i, 1);
   487	    }
   488	  }
   489	
   490	  function updateShots(dt) {
   491	    for (let i = shots.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
   492	      const shot = shots[i];
   493	      shot.life -= dt;
   494	      if (shot.kind === 'wisp') {
   495	        const target = nearestEnemy(560);
   496	        if (target) {
   497	          const dx = target.x - shot.x;
   498	          const dy = target.y - shot.y;
   499	          const length = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) || 1;
   500	          const desiredX = dx / length * 235;
   501	          const desiredY = dy / length * 235;
   502	          shot.vx += (desiredX - shot.vx) * Math.min(1, dt * 3.8);
   503	          shot.vy += (desiredY - shot.vy) * Math.min(1, dt * 3.8);
   504	        }
   505	      }
   506	      shot.x += shot.vx * dt;
   507	      shot.y += shot.vy * dt;
   508	      let hit = false;
   509	      for (let enemyIndex = enemies.length - 1; enemyIndex >= 0; enemyIndex -= 1) {
   510	        const enemy = enemies[enemyIndex];
   511	        if (distanceSquared(shot.x, shot.y, enemy.x, enemy.y) < (shot.r + enemy.r) * (shot.r + enemy.r)) {
   512	          damageEnemy(enemy, shot.damage);
   513	          spawnParticle(shot.x, shot.y, shot.kind === 'wisp' ? '#bd8dff' : '#e5fff7', 5, 38, 2.4);
   514	          hit = true;
   515	          break;
   516	        }
   517	      }
   518	      if (hit || shot.life <= 0 || Math.abs(shot.x - player.x) > 980 || Math.abs(shot.y - player.y) > 980) shots.splice(i, 1);
   519	    }
   520	  }
   521	
   522	  function updateEnemies(dt) {
   523	    for (let i = enemies.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
   524	      const enemy = enemies[i];
   525	      enemy.hitFlash = Math.max(0, enemy.hitFlash - dt);
   526	      enemy.attackClock -= dt;
   527	      const dx = player.x - enemy.x;
   528	      const dy = player.y - enemy.y;
   529	      const distance = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) || 1;
   530	      let speed = enemy.speed;
   531	      if (enemy.type === 'sprinter') speed *= 1 + Math.sin(run.time * 4 + enemy.phase) * .14;
   532	      if (enemy.type === 'boss') speed *= 1 + Math.sin(run.time * .9) * .12;
   533	      if (distance > player.r + enemy.r * .72) {
   534	        enemy.x += dx / distance * speed * dt;
   535	        enemy.y += dy / distance * speed * dt;
   536	      } else if (enemy.attackClock <= 0) {
   537	        enemy.attackClock = enemy.boss ? .55 : .72;
   538	        if (enemy.type === 'exploder') {
   539	          explodeEnemy(enemy);
   540	          enemies.splice(i, 1);
   541	        } else {
   542	          hurtPlayer(enemy.damage);
   543	        }
   544	      }
   545	    }
   546	  }
   547	
   548	  function updateGems(dt) {
   549	    for (let i = gems.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
   550	      const gem = gems[i];
   551	      const dx = player.x - gem.x;
   552	      const dy = player.y - gem.y;
   553	      const distance = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) || 1;
   554	      if (distance < player.magnet) {
   555	        const pull = clamp(1 - distance / player.magnet, .15, 1);
   556	        gem.vx += dx / distance * (420 + pull * 330) * dt;
   557	        gem.vy += dy / distance * (420 + pull * 330) * dt;
   558	      }
   559	      gem.vx *= Math.pow(.035, dt);
   560	      gem.vy *= Math.pow(.035, dt);
   561	      gem.x += gem.vx * dt;
   562	      gem.y += gem.vy * dt;
   563	      if (distance < player.r + 12) {
   564	        run.xp += gem.value;
   565	        spawnParticle(gem.x, gem.y, '#8fe7ff', 5, 30, 2);
   566	        gems.splice(i, 1);
   567	        checkLevel();
   568	        if (run.mode !== 'playing') return;
   569	      }
   570	    }
   571	  }
   572	
   573	  function checkLevel() {
   574	    if (run.xp < run.xpNext || run.mode !== 'playing') return;
   575	    run.xp -= run.xpNext;
   576	    run.level += 1;
   577	    run.xpNext = Math.floor(13 + run.level * 7 + run.level * run.level * .9);
   578	    offerDraft();
   579	  }
   580

   584	      { key: 'lance', icon: '↗', title: 'Bolt Lance +' + player.lance, desc: 'Fire faster; every third rank adds another bolt.', apply: function () { player.lance += 1; } },

   593	  function offerDraft() {
   594	    run.mode = 'draft';
   595	    run.choices = [];

   613	  }

   628	  function hurtPlayer(amount) {
   629	    if (run.mode !== 'playing') return;
   630	    player.hp = Math.max(0, player.hp - amount);
   631	    run.shake = Math.max(run.shake, 8);

   633	    if (player.hp <= 0) endRun(false);

   636	  function explodeEnemy(enemy) {
   637	    const radius = enemy.r * 2.9;
   638	    const damage = enemy.damage * .8;
   639	    if (distanceSquared(player.x, player.y, enemy.x, enemy.y) < (radius + player.r) * (radius + player.r)) hurtPlayer(damage);
   640	    spawnParticle(enemy.x, enemy.y, '#ff735f', 23, 120, 4);
   641	    run.shake = Math.max(run.shake, 7);
   642	    run.kills += 1;
   643	    dropGem(enemy);
   644	  }

   670	  function dropGem(enemy) {
   671	    const count = enemy.elite ? 3 : 1;
   672	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i += 1) {

   677	        vy: (random() - .5) * 55,
   678	        value: enemy.elite ? 4 : enemy.value
   679	      });
   680	    }
   681	  }

   683	  function endRun(won) {

   705	  function spawnParticle(x, y, color, count, speed, size) {
   706	    const amount = Math.min(count || 1, 28);
   707	    for (let i = 0; i < amount && particles.length < 310; i += 1) {
   708	      const angle = random() * TAU;
   709	      const velocity = speed * (.35 + random() * .65);
   710	      particles.push({
   711	        x: x,
   712	        y: y,
   713	        vx: Math.cos(angle) * velocity,
   714	        vy: Math.sin(angle) * velocity,
   715	        life: .26 + random() * .5,
   716	        maxLife: .75,
   717	        size: size * (.65 + random() * .6),
   718	        color: color
   719	      });
   720	    }
   721	  }

   723	  function updateEffects(dt) {
   724	    run.shake = Math.max(0, run.shake - dt * 18);
   725	    if (run.toast) {
   726	      run.toast.life -= dt;
   727	      if (run.toast.life <= 0) run.toast = null;
   728	    }
   729	    for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
   730	      const particle = particles[i];
   731	      particle.life -= dt;
   732	      particle.x += particle.vx * dt;
   733	      particle.y += particle.vy * dt;
   734	      particle.vx *= Math.pow(.06, dt);
   735	      particle.vy *= Math.pow(.06, dt);
   736	      if (particle.life <= 0) particles.splice(i, 1);
   737	    }
   738	  }

   740	  function worldPoint(x, y, cameraX, cameraY) {

   744	  function roundedRect(x, y, w, h, radius) {
   745	    const r = Math.min(radius, Math.abs(w) / 2, Math.abs(h) / 2);
   746	    ctx.beginPath();
   747	    ctx.moveTo(x + r, y);
   748	    ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r);
   749	    ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r);
   750	    ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r);
   751	    ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r);
   752	    ctx.closePath();
   753	  }

   986	    ctx.save();
   987	    ctx.translate(point.x, point.y);
   988	    ctx.rotate(run.time * .7);
   989	    ctx.shadowBlur = 22;
   990	    ctx.shadowColor = '#6df0bf';
   991	    ctx.fillStyle = '#122e35';
   992	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#a4ffe2';
   993	    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   994	    ctx.beginPath();
   995	    ctx.arc(0, 0, player.r + 3, 0, TAU);
   996	    ctx.fill();
   997	    ctx.stroke();
   998	    ctx.fillStyle = '#7df0c3';
   999	    ctx.beginPath();
  1000	    ctx.moveTo(0, -player.r - 7);
  1001	    ctx.lineTo(5, 2);
  1002	    ctx.lineTo(0, player.r - 3);
  1003	    ctx.lineTo(-5, 2);
  1004	    ctx.closePath();
  1005	    ctx.fill();
  1006	    ctx.restore();
  1007	
  1008	    if (player.orbit > 0) {
  1009	      const count = Math.min(7, 1 + player.orbit);
  1010	      for (let i = 0; i < count; i += 1) {
  1011	        const angle = player.orbitAngle + i * TAU / count;
  1012	        const bladeX = player.x + Math.cos(angle) * (38 + player.orbit * 3) - cameraX;
  1013	        const bladeY = player.y + Math.sin(angle) * (38 + player.orbit * 3) - cameraY;
  1014	        ctx.save();
  1015	        ctx.translate(bladeX, bladeY);
  1016	        ctx.rotate(angle + Math.PI / 2);
  1017	        ctx.shadowBlur = 11;
  1018	        ctx.shadowColor = '#8ffff0';
  1019	        ctx.fillStyle = '#a4ffe0';
  1020	        ctx.beginPath();
  1021	        ctx.moveTo(0, -10);
  1022	        ctx.lineTo(5, 4);
  1023	        ctx.lineTo(0, 8);
  1024	        ctx.lineTo(-5, 4);
  1025	        ctx.closePath();
  1026	        ctx.fill();
  1027	        ctx.restore();
  1028	      }
  1029	    }
  1030	  }
  1031	
  1032	  function drawParticles(cameraX, cameraY) {
  1033	    for (const particle of particles) {
  1034	      const point = worldPoint(particle.x, particle.y, cameraX, cameraY);
  1035	      ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(particle.life / particle.maxLife, 0, 1);
  1036	      ctx.fillStyle = particle.color;
  1037	      ctx.beginPath();
  1038	      ctx.arc(point.x, point.y, particle.size * (0.5 + particle.life / particle.maxLife), 0, TAU);
  1039	      ctx.fill();
  1040	    }
  1041	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  1042	  }
  1043	
  1044	  function drawStick() {
  1045	    if (!input.active) return;
  1046	    ctx.save();
  1047	    ctx.globalAlpha = .32;
  1048	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#b5ffe5';
  1049	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(125, 240, 195, .13)';
  1050	    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  1051	    ctx.beginPath();
  1052	    ctx.arc(input.baseX, input.baseY, 42, 0, TAU);
  1053	    ctx.fill();
  1054	    ctx.stroke();
  1055	    ctx.globalAlpha = .72;
  1056	    ctx.fillStyle = '#a5ffe0';
  1057	    ctx.beginPath();
  1058	    ctx.arc(input.baseX + input.dx * 42, input.baseY + input.dy * 42, 18, 0, TAU);
  1059	    ctx.fill();
  1060	    ctx.restore();
  1061	  }
  1062	
  1063	  function drawToast() {
  1064	    if (!run.toast) return;
  1065	    const alpha = clamp(run.toast.life * 1.4, 0, 1);
  1066	    ctx.save();
  1067	    ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
  1068	    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  1069	    ctx.font = '900 12px ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif';
  1070	    ctx.letterSpacing = '2px';
  1071	    ctx.fillStyle = run.toast.color;
  1072	    ctx.shadowBlur = 10;
  1073	    ctx.shadowColor = '#000';
  1074	    ctx.fillText(run.toast.text, width / 2, height * .22);
  1075	    ctx.restore();
  1076	  }
  1077	
  1078	  function drawBossBar() {
  1079	    const boss = enemies.find(function (enemy) { return enemy.boss; });
  1080	    if (!boss) return;
  1081	    const barWidth = Math.min(width - 40, 330);
  1082	    const x = (width - barWidth) / 2;
  1083	    const y = height - 56;
  1084	    ctx.save();
  1085	    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  1086	    ctx.font = '900 9px ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif';
  1087	    ctx.fillStyle = '#efcfff';
  1088	    ctx.fillText('MERIDIAN CORE', width / 2, y - 9);
  1089	    roundedRect(x, y, barWidth, 7, 4);
  1090	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.65)';
  1091	    ctx.fill();
  1092	    roundedRect(x, y, barWidth * clamp(boss.hp / boss.maxHp, 0, 1), 7, 4);
  1093	    ctx.fillStyle = '#d59cff';
  1094	    ctx.fill();
  1095	    ctx.restore();
  1096	  }
  1097	
  1098	  function render() {
  1099	    const shakeX = run.shake ? (Math.random() - .5) * run.shake : 0;
  1100	    const shakeY = run.shake ? (Math.random() - .5) * run.shake : 0;
  1101	    const cameraX = player.x - width / 2 + shakeX;
  1102	    const cameraY = player.y - height / 2 + shakeY;
  1103	    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
  1104	    drawBackground(cameraX, cameraY);
  1105	    for (const gem of gems) drawGem(gem, cameraX, cameraY);
  1106	    for (const pulse of pulses) drawPulse(pulse, cameraX, cameraY);
  1107	    for (const enemy of enemies) drawEnemy(enemy, cameraX, cameraY);
  1108	    for (const shot of shots) drawShot(shot, cameraX, cameraY);
  1109	    drawParticles(cameraX, cameraY);
  1110	    drawPlayer(cameraX, cameraY);
  1111	    drawBossBar();
  1112	    drawToast();
  1113	    drawStick();
  1114	  }
  1115	
  1116	  function updateHud() {
  1117	    hud.time.textContent = formatTime(run.time);
  1118	    hud.timeLabel.textContent = run.bossSpawned ? 'CORE' : 'SURVIVE';
  1119	    hud.hp.style.transform = 'scaleX(' + clamp(player.hp / player.maxHp, 0, 1) + ')';
  1120	    hud.xp.style.transform = 'scaleX(' + clamp(run.xp / run.xpNext, 0, 1) + ')';
  1121	    hud.level.textContent = 'LV ' + run.level;
  1122	    hud.kills.textContent = run.kills + ' KILLS';
  1123	    hud.score.textContent = 'SCORE ' + (Math.floor(run.time) + run.kills);
  1124	  }
  1125	
  1126	  function tick(now) {
  1127	    const dt = lastFrame ? Math.min(.05, (now - lastFrame) / 1000) : 0;
  1128	    lastFrame = now;
  1129	    if (run.mode === 'playing') {
  1130	      run.time = Math.min(600, run.time + dt);
  1131	      updateMilestones();
  1132	      updatePlayer(dt);
  1133	      updateSpawning(dt);
  1134	      updateWeapons(dt);
  1135	      updatePulses(dt);
  1136	      updateShots(dt);
  1137	      updateEnemies(dt);
  1138	      updateGems(dt);
  1139	    }
  1140	    updateEffects(dt);
  1141	    updateHud();
  1142	    render();
  1143	    requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  1144	  }
  1145	
  1146	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', pointerDown, { passive: false });
  1147	  canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', pointerMove, { passive: false });
  1148	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', pointerUp, { passive: false });
  1149	  canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', pointerUp, { passive: false });
  1150	  canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', function (event) { event.preventDefault(); }, { passive: false });
  1151	  choices.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
  1152	    const button = event.target.closest('button[data-choice]');
  1153	    if (button) chooseUpgrade(Number(button.dataset.choice));
  1154	  });
  1155	  restartButton.addEventListener('click', resetRun);
  1156	  window.addEventListener('keydown', keyDown, { passive: false });
  1157	  window.addEventListener('keyup', keyUp);
  1158	  window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
  1159	  window.addEventListener('orientationchange', updateOrientation);
  1160	
  1161	  seedField();
  1162	  resize();
  1163	  resetRun();
  1164	  requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  1165	}());

--- notes ---
     1	Controls: drag anywhere with one finger (or mouse) to move; WASD/arrows also work.
     2	Weapons auto-fire; collect blue gems to level up, then tap one of three upgrades.
     3	Survive escalating waves, elite spikes each minute, and defeat the Meridian Core at 10:00.
     4	Death or victory shows score, kills, time, and a persisted best; tap Run Again for an instant restart.

    39	  function readBest() {
    40	    try { return Number(localStorage.getItem(BEST_KEY)) || 0; } catch (_) { return 0; }
    41	  }
    42	
    43	  function saveBest() {
    44	    if (score <= best) return;
    45	    best = score;
    46	    try { localStorage.setItem(BEST_KEY, String(best)); } catch (_) { /* storage is optional */ }
    47	  }
    48	
    49	  function makeRng(seed) {
    50	    let value = seed >>> 0;
    51	    return () => {
    52	      value += 0x6D2B79F5;
    53	      let t = value;
    54	      t = Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 15, t | 1);
    55	      t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, t | 61);
    56	      return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    57	    };
    58	  }
    59	
    60	  function clamp(value, min, max) { return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value)); }
    61	  function lerp(a, b, t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }
    62	  function distance(ax, ay, bx, by) { return Math.hypot(ax - bx, ay - by); }
    63	  function aliveTowns() { return towns.reduce((n, town) => n + (town.alive ? 1 : 0), 0); }

    65	  function resize() {
    66	    W = Math.max(280, window.innerWidth || 390);
    67	    H = Math.max(420, window.innerHeight || 700);
    68	    const dpr = Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
    69	    scale = Math.min(dpr, 960 / Math.max(W, H));
    70	    canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(W * scale));
    71	    canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(H * scale));
    72	    canvas.style.width = W + 'px';
    73	    canvas.style.height = H + 'px';
    74	    ctx.setTransform(scale, 0, 0, scale, 0, 0);
    75	    crosshair.x = clamp(crosshair.x, 18, W - 18);
    76	    crosshair.y = clamp(crosshair.y, 28, H * 0.73);
    77	    makeStars();
    78	    layoutBase();
    79	  }
    80	
    81	  function makeStars() {
    82	    const starRng = makeRng(4201 + Math.round(W * 2.3) + Math.round(H));
    83	    stars = [];
    84	    const count = Math.round(clamp(W * H / 7000, 38, 100));
    85	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    86	      stars.push({
    87	        x: starRng() * W,
    88	        y: starRng() * H * 0.72,
    89	        r: lerp(0.45, 1.35, starRng()),
    90	        a: lerp(0.2, 0.8, starRng()),
    91	        twinkle: lerp(0.5, 2, starRng())
    92	      });
    93	    }
    94	  }

    96	  function layoutBase() {
    97	    const townXs = [0.08, 0.22, 0.35, 0.65, 0.78, 0.92];
    98	    const batteryXs = [0.29, 0.50, 0.71];
    99	    const baseY = H - clamp(H * 0.09, 48, 72);
   100	    towns.forEach((town, i) => { town.x = W * townXs[i]; town.y = baseY; });
   101	    batteries.forEach((battery, i) => { battery.x = W * batteryXs[i]; battery.y = baseY + 2; });
   102	  }
   103	
   104	  function resetGame() {
   105	    state = 'playing';
   106	    wave = 1;
   107	    score = 0;
   108	    ammoBonus = 0;
   109	    waveClock = 0;
   110	    spawnClock = 0;
   111	    waveSpawned = 0;
   112	    clearClock = 0;
   113	    shake = 0;
   114	    hitFlash = 0;
   115	    waveMessage = 2.2;
   116	    waveMessageText = 'WAVE 01';
   117	    incoming.length = 0;
   118	    interceptors.length = 0;
   119	    blasts.length = 0;
   120	    particles.length = 0;
   121	    towns = Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => ({ x: 0, y: 0, alive: true, phase: i * 0.7 }));
   122	    batteries = Array.from({ length: 3 }, (_, i) => ({ x: 0, y: 0, ammo: 6, maxAmmo: 6, pulse: 0 }));
   123	    layoutBase();
   124	    beginWave();
   125	  }
   126	
   127	  function beginWave() {
   128	    rng = makeRng(0x51F17 + wave * 7919);
   129	    waveTotal = 5 + Math.floor(wave * 1.65);
   130	    waveSpawned = 0;
   131	    spawnClock = 0.45;
   132	    waveClock = 0;
   133	    clearClock = 0;
   134	    const capacity = 6 + ammoBonus;
   135	    batteries.forEach((battery) => {
   136	      battery.maxAmmo = capacity;
   137	      battery.ammo = capacity;
   138	      battery.pulse = 0;
   139	    });
   140	    waveMessage = 1.5;
   141	    waveMessageText = `WAVE ${String(wave).padStart(2, '0')}`;
   142	  }
   143	
   144	  function endWave() {
   145	    const survivors = aliveTowns();
   146	    score += survivors * 50 * wave;
   147	    saveBest();
   148	    let bonusMessage = '';
   149	    if (wave % 5 === 0) {
   150	      ammoBonus += 2;
   151	      const rebuild = towns.find((town) => !town.alive);
   152	      if (rebuild) {
   153	        rebuild.alive = true;
   154	        bonusMessage = 'REINFORCEMENTS  +AMMO  +TOWN';
   155	      } else {
   156	        bonusMessage = 'REINFORCEMENTS  +AMMO';
   157	      }
   158	    }
   159	    wave += 1;
   160	    beginWave();
   161	    if (bonusMessage) {
   162	      waveMessageText = bonusMessage;
   163	      waveMessage = 2.6;
   164	    }
   165	  }
