   167	  function screenPoint(event) {
   168	    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
   169	    return {
   170	      x: clamp(event.clientX - rect.left, 0, W),
   171	      y: clamp(event.clientY - rect.top, 0, H)
   172	    };
   173	  }
   174	
   175	  function setCrosshair(point) {
   176	    crosshair.x = clamp(point.x, 18, W - 18);
   177	    crosshair.y = clamp(point.y, 28, H * 0.72);
   178	  }
   179	
   180	  function fire(point) {
   181	    if (state !== 'playing') {
   182	      resetGame();
   183	      return;
   184	    }
   185	    setCrosshair(point);
   186	    let battery = batteries[0];
   187	    let bestDistance = Infinity;
   188	    batteries.forEach((candidate) => {
   189	      const difference = Math.abs(candidate.x - crosshair.x);
   190	      if (candidate.ammo > 0 && difference < bestDistance) {
   191	        bestDistance = difference;
   192	        battery = candidate;
   193	      }
   194	    });
   195	    if (!battery || battery.ammo <= 0) {
   196	      hitFlash = Math.max(hitFlash, 0.12);
   197	      return;
   198	    }
   199	    battery.ammo -= 1;
   200	    battery.pulse = 0.25;
   201	    interceptors.push({
   202	      x: battery.x,
   203	      y: battery.y - 12,
   204	      sx: battery.x,
   205	      sy: battery.y - 12,
   206	      tx: crosshair.x,
   207	      ty: crosshair.y,
   208	      t: 0,
   209	      speed: 390,
   210	      battery
   211	    });
   212	    burst(battery.x, battery.y - 9, '#6ef6ff', 4, 18);
   213	  }
   214	
   215	  function chooseTown() {
   216	    const available = towns.filter((town) => town.alive);
   217	    return available[Math.floor(rng() * available.length)] || towns[Math.floor(rng() * towns.length)];
   218	  }
   219	
   220	  function spawnWarhead(options = {}) {
   221	    const town = options.town || chooseTown();
   222	    const margin = Math.min(34, W * 0.1);
   223	    const x = options.x == null ? lerp(margin, W - margin, rng()) : options.x;
   224	    const y = options.y == null ? -12 : options.y;
   225	    const targetX = town.x + lerp(-10, 10, rng());
   226	    const targetY = town.y - 9;
   227	    let type = options.type;
   228	    if (!type) {
   229	      const roll = rng();
   230	      if (wave >= 4 && roll < 0.18) type = 'smart';
   231	      else if (wave >= 3 && roll < 0.4) type = 'fast';
   232	      else if (wave >= 2 && roll < 0.64) type = 'split';
   233	      else type = 'normal';
   234	    }
   235	    const speed = options.speed || (type === 'fast' ? 122 + wave * 3 : type === 'smart' ? 74 + wave * 2 : 61 + wave * 2.4);
   236	    const dx = targetX - x;
   237	    const dy = targetY - y;
   238	    const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
   239	    incoming.push({
   240	      x, y, targetX, targetY, town, type, speed,
   241	      vx: dx / len * speed,
   242	      vy: dy / len * speed,
   243	      age: 0,
   244	      distanceTravelled: 0,
   245	      totalDistance: len,
   246	      splitAt: lerp(0.43, 0.57, rng()),
   247	      split: false,
   248	      dodge: 0,
   249	      hue: type === 'smart' ? '#ec9bff' : type === 'fast' ? '#ffbd66' : type === 'split' ? '#ff6f91' : '#ff5e72'
   250	    });
   251	  }
   252	
   253	  function updateWave(dt) {
   254	    waveClock += dt;
   255	    if (waveSpawned < waveTotal) {
   256	      spawnClock -= dt;
   257	      if (spawnClock <= 0) {
   258	        spawnWarhead();
   259	        waveSpawned += 1;
   260	        const spacing = clamp(1.16 - wave * 0.026, 0.44, 1.16);
   261	        spawnClock = spacing * lerp(0.78, 1.18, rng());
   262	      }
   263	    } else if (incoming.length === 0 && interceptors.length === 0 && blasts.length === 0) {
   264	      clearClock += dt;
   265	      if (clearClock > 1.25) endWave();
   266	    }
   267	  }
   268	
   269	  function updateInterceptors(dt) {
   270	    for (let i = interceptors.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   271	      const shot = interceptors[i];
   272	      const dx = shot.tx - shot.sx;
   273	      const dy = shot.ty - shot.sy;
   274	      const length = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
   275	      shot.t += shot.speed * dt / length;
   276	      shot.x = lerp(shot.sx, shot.tx, shot.t);
   277	      shot.y = lerp(shot.sy, shot.ty, shot.t);
   278	      if (shot.t >= 1) {
   279	        blasts.push({ x: shot.tx, y: shot.ty, r: 3, maxR: 46 + Math.min(19, wave * 1.6), life: 1, age: 0 });
   280	        burst(shot.tx, shot.ty, '#d9fdff', 10, 45);
   281	        shake = Math.max(shake, 1.5);
   282	        interceptors.splice(i, 1);
   283	      }
   284	    }
   285	  }
   286	
   287	  function updateIncoming(dt) {
   288	    for (let i = incoming.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   289	      const missile = incoming[i];
   290	      missile.age += dt;
   291	      let vx = missile.vx;
   292	      let vy = missile.vy;
   293	      if (missile.type === 'smart') {
   294	        let nearest = null;
   295	        let nearestDistance = Infinity;
   296	        blasts.forEach((blast) => {
   297	          const d = distance(missile.x, missile.y, blast.x, blast.y);
   298	          if (d < nearestDistance) { nearestDistance = d; nearest = blast; }
   299	        });
   300	        if (nearest && nearestDistance < nearest.r + 70) {
   301	          const awayX = missile.x - nearest.x;
   302	          const awayY = missile.y - nearest.y;
   303	          const awayLength = Math.hypot(awayX, awayY) || 1;
   304	          missile.dodge = clamp(missile.dodge + dt * 2.8, 0, 1);
   305	          vx += awayX / awayLength * 85 * missile.dodge;
   306	          vy += awayY / awayLength * 85 * missile.dodge;
   307	        } else {
   308	          missile.dodge = Math.max(0, missile.dodge - dt * 1.8);
   309	        }
   310	      }
   311	      const velocityLength = Math.hypot(vx, vy) || 1;
   312	      const stepX = vx / velocityLength * missile.speed * dt;
   313	      const stepY = vy / velocityLength * missile.speed * dt;
   314	      missile.x += stepX;
   315	      missile.y += stepY;
   316	      missile.distanceTravelled += Math.hypot(stepX, stepY);
   317	
   318	      const progress = missile.distanceTravelled / missile.totalDistance;
   319	      if (missile.type === 'split' && !missile.split && progress > missile.splitAt) {
   320	        missile.split = true;
   321	        splitWarhead(missile);
   322	        incoming.splice(i, 1);
   323	        continue;
   324	      }
   325	      if (missile.y >= missile.targetY) {
   326	        impactTown(missile);
   327	        incoming.splice(i, 1);
   328	      }
   329	    }
   330	  }
   331	
   332	  function splitWarhead(parent) {
   333	    const candidates = towns.filter((town) => town.alive);
   334	    for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
   335	      const town = candidates.length ? candidates[Math.floor(rng() * candidates.length)] : chooseTown();
   336	      const targetX = town.x + (i ? 9 : -9);
   337	      const targetY = town.y - 8;
   338	      const dx = targetX - parent.x;
   339	      const dy = targetY - parent.y;
   340	      const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
   341	      incoming.push({
   342	        x: parent.x, y: parent.y, targetX, targetY, town,
   343	        type: 'child', speed: 94 + wave * 2,
   344	        vx: dx / len * (94 + wave * 2), vy: dy / len * (94 + wave * 2),
   345	        age: 0, distanceTravelled: 0, totalDistance: len,
   346	        splitAt: 1, split: true, dodge: 0, hue: '#ffda76'
   347	      });
   348	    }
   349	    burst(parent.x, parent.y, '#ff8dac', 16, 52);
   350	    shake = Math.max(shake, 3);
   351	  }
   352	
   353	  function impactTown(missile) {
   354	    if (!missile.town.alive) {
   355	      const fallback = towns.find((town) => town.alive);
   356	      if (fallback) fallback.alive = false;
   357	    } else {
   358	      missile.town.alive = false;
   359	    }
   360	    score += missile.type === 'child' ? 80 : 100;
   361	    hitFlash = 0.24;
   362	    shake = Math.max(shake, 7);
   363	    burst(missile.targetX, missile.targetY, '#ff5e72', 22, 95);
   364	    if (aliveTowns() <= 0) gameOver();
   365	  }
   366	
   367	  function updateBlasts(dt) {
   368	    for (let i = blasts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   369	      const blast = blasts[i];
   370	      blast.age += dt;
   371	      blast.r = blast.age < 0.28 ? lerp(3, blast.maxR, blast.age / 0.28) : blast.maxR;
   372	      blast.life = Math.max(0, 1 - Math.max(0, blast.age - 0.28) / 0.72);
   373	      for (let j = incoming.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
   374	        const missile = incoming[j];
   375	        const d = distance(blast.x, blast.y, missile.x, missile.y);
   376	        const collisionRadius = missile.type === 'fast' ? 7 : missile.type === 'child' ? 6 : 9;
   377	        const smartMargin = missile.type === 'smart' ? 12 : 0;
   378	        if (d <= blast.r - smartMargin + collisionRadius) {
   379	          if (missile.type === 'smart' && d > blast.r - 9 && blast.age < 0.48) continue;
   380	          score += missile.type === 'fast' ? 145 : missile.type === 'smart' ? 175 : missile.type === 'split' ? 185 : 110;
   381	          burst(missile.x, missile.y, missile.hue, missile.type === 'split' ? 14 : 9, 48);
   382	          shake = Math.max(shake, missile.type === 'split' ? 3 : 1.2);
   383	          incoming.splice(j, 1);
   384	        }
   385	      }
   386	      if (blast.age > 1) blasts.splice(i, 1);
   387	    }
   388	  }
   389	
   390	  function burst(x, y, color, count, force) {
   391	    const available = Math.max(0, 150 - particles.length);
   392	    count = Math.min(count, available);
   393	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   394	      const angle = rng() * TAU;
   395	      const speed = force * lerp(0.35, 1, rng());
   396	      particles.push({
   397	        x, y, vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed, vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
   398	        life: lerp(0.25, 0.75, rng()), maxLife: 0.75, size: lerp(1, 3.2, rng()), color
   399	      });
   400	    }
   401	  }
   402	
   403	  function updateParticles(dt) {
   404	    for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   405	      const particle = particles[i];
   406	      particle.life -= dt;
   407	      particle.x += particle.vx * dt;
   408	      particle.y += particle.vy * dt;
   409	      particle.vx *= Math.pow(0.04, dt);
   410	      particle.vy = particle.vy * Math.pow(0.04, dt) + 38 * dt;
   411	      if (particle.life <= 0) particles.splice(i, 1);
   412	    }
   413	  }
   414	
   415	  function update(dt) {
   416	    if (state === 'playing') {
   417	      updateWave(dt);
   418	      updateInterceptors(dt);
   419	      updateIncoming(dt);
   420	      updateBlasts(dt);
   421	      updateParticles(dt);
   422	      batteries.forEach((battery) => { battery.pulse = Math.max(0, battery.pulse - dt); });
   423	      if (keyFire) { fire(crosshair); keyFire = false; }
   424	    } else {
   425	      updateParticles(dt);
   426	    }
   427	    waveMessage = Math.max(0, waveMessage - dt);
   428	    hitFlash = Math.max(0, hitFlash - dt);
   429	    shake = Math.max(0, shake - dt * 15);
   430	  }
   431	
   432	  function gameOver() {
   433	    state = 'gameover';
   434	    incoming.length = 0;
   435	    interceptors.length = 0;
   436	    blasts.length = 0;
   437	    saveBest();
   438	    waveMessage = 0;
   439	    burst(W / 2, H * 0.62, '#ff5e72', 42, 145);
   440	  }
   441	
   442	  function draw() {
   443	    ctx.setTransform(scale, 0, 0, scale, 0, 0);
   444	    const gradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H);
   445	    gradient.addColorStop(0, '#071224');
   446	    gradient.addColorStop(0.62, '#101c35');
   447	    gradient.addColorStop(1, '#172238');
   448	    ctx.fillStyle = gradient;
   449	    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   450	
   451	    ctx.save();
   452	    if (shake > 0) ctx.translate(lerp(-shake, shake, rng()), lerp(-shake, shake, rng()));
   453	    drawSky();
   454	    drawTrails();
   455	    drawBlasts();
   456	    drawInterceptors();
   457	    drawIncoming();
   458	    drawParticles();
   459	    drawBase();
   460	    ctx.restore();
   461	    drawHud();
   462	    if (hitFlash > 0) {
   463	      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255, 73, 98, ${hitFlash * 0.35})`;
   464	      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   465	    }
   466	    if (state === 'gameover') drawGameOver();
   467	  }
   468	
   469	  function drawSky() {
   470	    const now = performance.now() / 1000;
   471	    stars.forEach((star) => {
   472	      const alpha = star.a * (0.7 + Math.sin(now * star.twinkle + star.x) * 0.3);
   473	      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(185, 224, 255, ${alpha})`;
   474	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(star.x, star.y, star.r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   475	    });
   476	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(108, 170, 221, 0.08)';
   477	    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
   478	    for (let y = H * 0.14; y < H * 0.72; y += 54) {
   479	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, y); ctx.lineTo(W, y); ctx.stroke();
   480	    }
   481	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(91, 143, 185, 0.12)';
   482	    const horizon = H * 0.72;
   483	    for (let x = -10; x < W; x += 38) {
   484	      const height = 12 + ((x * 17) % 28 + 28) % 28;
   485	      ctx.fillRect(x, horizon - height, 25, height);
   486	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(123, 185, 218, 0.24)';
   487	      ctx.fillRect(x + 6, horizon - height + 8, 3, 3);
   488	      ctx.fillRect(x + 16, horizon - height + 15, 3, 3);
   489	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(91, 143, 185, 0.12)';
   490	    }
   491	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(102, 202, 224, 0.28)';
   492	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, horizon); ctx.lineTo(W, horizon); ctx.stroke();
   493	  }
   494	
   495	  function drawTrails() {
   496	    incoming.forEach((missile) => {
   497	      ctx.strokeStyle = missile.type === 'smart' ? 'rgba(236, 155, 255, 0.28)' : 'rgba(255, 105, 122, 0.26)';
   498	      ctx.lineWidth = missile.type === 'fast' ? 2 : 1;
   499	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(missile.x, missile.y); ctx.lineTo(missile.x - missile.vx * 0.15, missile.y - missile.vy * 0.15); ctx.stroke();
   500	    });
   501	  }
   502	
   503	  function drawIncoming() {
   504	    incoming.forEach((missile) => {
   505	      const angle = Math.atan2(missile.vy, missile.vx);
   506	      ctx.save();
   507	      ctx.translate(missile.x, missile.y);
   508	      ctx.rotate(angle + Math.PI / 2);
   509	      ctx.strokeStyle = missile.hue;
   510	      ctx.fillStyle = missile.hue;
   511	      ctx.lineWidth = missile.type === 'fast' ? 2.5 : 2;
   512	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, -11); ctx.lineTo(0, 8); ctx.stroke();
   513	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, -10, missile.type === 'fast' ? 3.4 : 3, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   514	      if (missile.type === 'split') {
   515	        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 218, 118, 0.8)';
   516	        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-4, 3); ctx.lineTo(-9, 8); ctx.moveTo(4, 3); ctx.lineTo(9, 8); ctx.stroke();
   517	      }
   518	      if (missile.type === 'smart') {
   519	        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.7)';
   520	        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, -10, 6, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
   521	      }
   522	      ctx.restore();
   523	    });
   524	  }
   525	
   526	  function drawInterceptors() {
   527	    interceptors.forEach((shot) => {
   528	      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(110, 246, 255, 0.38)';
   529	      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   530	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(shot.sx, shot.sy); ctx.lineTo(shot.x, shot.y); ctx.stroke();
   531	      ctx.fillStyle = '#eaffff';
   532	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(shot.x, shot.y, 3, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   533	    });
   534	  }
   535	
   536	  function drawBlasts() {
   537	    blasts.forEach((blast) => {
   538	      const glow = ctx.createRadialGradient(blast.x, blast.y, 0, blast.x, blast.y, blast.r * 1.35);
   539	      glow.addColorStop(0, `rgba(222, 255, 255, ${0.8 * blast.life})`);
   540	      glow.addColorStop(0.34, `rgba(101, 237, 255, ${0.38 * blast.life})`);
   541	      glow.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(58, 167, 255, 0)');
   542	      ctx.fillStyle = glow;
   543	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(blast.x, blast.y, blast.r * 1.35, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   544	      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(188, 255, 255, ${0.9 * blast.life})`;
   545	      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   546	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(blast.x, blast.y, blast.r, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
   547	    });
   548	  }
   549	
   550	  function drawParticles() {
   551	    particles.forEach((particle) => {
   552	      ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(particle.life / particle.maxLife, 0, 1);
   553	      ctx.fillStyle = particle.color;
   554	      ctx.fillRect(particle.x - particle.size / 2, particle.y - particle.size / 2, particle.size, particle.size);
   555	    });
   556	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   557	  }
   558	
   559	  function drawBase() {
   560	    const baseY = H - clamp(H * 0.09, 48, 72);
   561	    ctx.fillStyle = '#0b1427';
   562	    ctx.fillRect(0, baseY + 15, W, H - baseY - 15);
   563	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(112, 218, 232, 0.42)';
   564	    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   565	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, baseY + 15); ctx.lineTo(W, baseY + 15); ctx.stroke();
   566	    towns.forEach((town, i) => drawTown(town, i));
   567	    batteries.forEach((battery, i) => drawBattery(battery, i));
   568	  }
   569	
   570	  function drawTown(town, index) {
   571	    const y = town.y;
   572	    ctx.save();
   573	    ctx.translate(town.x, y);
   574	    if (!town.alive) {
   575	      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 94, 114, 0.65)';
   576	      ctx.lineWidth = 3;
   577	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-12, -4); ctx.lineTo(12, 10); ctx.moveTo(12, -4); ctx.lineTo(-12, 10); ctx.stroke();
   578	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 94, 114, 0.18)';
   579	      ctx.fillRect(-16, 10, 32, 3);
   580	    } else {
   581	      ctx.fillStyle = '#254660';
   582	      ctx.fillRect(-14, -7, 28, 17);
   583	      ctx.fillStyle = '#6ce4db';
   584	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-18, -7); ctx.lineTo(0, -22); ctx.lineTo(18, -7); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
   585	      ctx.fillStyle = '#0d2638';
   586	      ctx.fillRect(-3, 1, 6, 9);
   587	      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd978';
   588	      ctx.fillRect(-11, -3, 4, 4); ctx.fillRect(7, -3, 4, 4);
   589	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(189, 255, 245, 0.4)';
   590	      ctx.fillRect(-20, 11, 40, 3);
   591	    }
   592	    ctx.restore();
   593	    if (town.alive) {
   594	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(178, 227, 236, 0.38)';
   595	      ctx.font = '9px system-ui, sans-serif';
   596	      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   597	      ctx.fillText(String(index + 1), town.x, y + 25);
   598	    }
   599	  }
   600	
   601	  function drawBattery(battery, index) {
   602	    const y = battery.y;
   603	    ctx.save();
   604	    ctx.translate(battery.x, y);
   605	    ctx.fillStyle = '#263d56';
   606	    ctx.fillRect(-18, 2, 36, 12);
   607	    ctx.fillStyle = battery.pulse > 0 ? '#caffff' : '#72d5dd';
   608	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 2, 12, Math.PI, TAU); ctx.fill();
   609	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#b6ffff';
   610	    ctx.lineWidth = 4;
   611	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, 2); ctx.lineTo(0, -20); ctx.stroke();
   612	    ctx.fillStyle = '#0b1527';
   613	    ctx.fillRect(-13, 7, 26, 3);
   614	    ctx.restore();
   615	    ctx.fillStyle = battery.ammo > 0 ? '#b7ecff' : '#ff6478';
   616	    ctx.font = 'bold 10px system-ui, sans-serif';
   617	    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   618	    ctx.fillText(`${battery.ammo}`, battery.x, y + 28);
   619	    if (index === 1) {
   620	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(150, 219, 233, 0.35)';
   621	      ctx.font = '8px system-ui, sans-serif';
   622	      ctx.fillText('BATTERIES', battery.x, y + 40);
   623	    }
   624	  }
   625	
   626	  function drawHud() {
   627	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(4, 10, 22, 0.52)';
   628	    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, 76);
   629	    ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   630	    ctx.fillStyle = '#d8f8ff';
   631	    ctx.font = '700 14px system-ui, sans-serif';
   632	    ctx.fillText('SKYFALL COMMAND', 16, 23);

   636	    ctx.textAlign = 'right';
   637	    ctx.fillStyle = '#f3fbff';
   638	    ctx.font = '700 15px system-ui, sans-serif';
   639	    ctx.fillText(String(score).padStart(5, '0'), W - 16, 23);
   640	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(196, 229, 239, 0.62)';
   641	    ctx.font = '9px system-ui, sans-serif';
   642	    ctx.fillText(`BEST ${String(best).padStart(5, '0')}`, W - 16, 39);

   646	    ctx.fillText(`WAVE ${String(wave).padStart(2, '0')}`, 16, 61);

   650	    if (waveMessage > 0) {

   656	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;

   658	    drawCrosshair();
   659	  }
   660	
   661	  function drawCrosshair() {
   662	    if (state !== 'playing') return;
   663	    const x = crosshair.x;
   664	    const y = crosshair.y;
   665	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(221, 255, 255, 0.85)';
   666	    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
   667	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, 13, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
   668	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x - 21, y); ctx.lineTo(x - 8, y); ctx.moveTo(x + 8, y); ctx.lineTo(x + 21, y); ctx.moveTo(x, y - 21); ctx.lineTo(x, y - 8); ctx.moveTo(x, y + 8); ctx.lineTo(x, y + 21); ctx.stroke();
   669	    ctx.fillStyle = '#dfffff';
   670	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, 2, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   671	  }
   672	
   673	  function drawGameOver() {
   674	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(4, 8, 18, 0.72)';
   675	    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   676	    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   677	    ctx.fillStyle = '#ff7181';
   678	    ctx.font = '700 27px system-ui, sans-serif';
   679	    ctx.fillText('COMMAND LOST', W / 2, H * 0.37);
   680	    ctx.fillStyle = '#d7faff';
   681	    ctx.font = '14px system-ui, sans-serif';
   682	    ctx.fillText(`SCORE  ${score}`, W / 2, H * 0.43);
   683	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(207, 237, 244, 0.66)';
   684	    ctx.font = '11px system-ui, sans-serif';
   685	    ctx.fillText(`BEST  ${best}`, W / 2, H * 0.47);
   686	    ctx.fillStyle = '#a9f4ef';
   687	    ctx.font = '700 12px system-ui, sans-serif';
   688	    ctx.fillText('Tap or press Enter to redeploy', W / 2, H * 0.56);
   689	  }
   690	
   691	  function moveCrosshair(dx, dy) {
   692	    setCrosshair({ x: crosshair.x + dx, y: crosshair.y + dy });
   693	  }
   694	
   695	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', (event) => {
   696	    event.preventDefault();
   697	    if (canvas.setPointerCapture) canvas.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
   698	    fire(screenPoint(event));
   699	  }, { passive: false });
   700	  canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', (event) => {
   701	    event.preventDefault();
   702	    if (event.pointerType === 'mouse' || event.buttons) setCrosshair(screenPoint(event));
   703	  }, { passive: false });
   704	  canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', (event) => event.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   705	  canvas.addEventListener('contextmenu', (event) => event.preventDefault());
   706	
   707	  window.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
   708	    const key = event.key.toLowerCase();
   709	    if (['arrowleft', 'arrowright', 'arrowup', 'arrowdown', ' ', 'enter', 'w', 'a', 's', 'd'].includes(key)) event.preventDefault();
   710	    if (state === 'gameover' && (key === 'enter' || key === ' ')) { resetGame(); return; }
   711	    const step = 18;
   712	    if (key === 'arrowleft' || key === 'a') moveCrosshair(-step, 0);
   713	    if (key === 'arrowright' || key === 'd') moveCrosshair(step, 0);
   714	    if (key === 'arrowup' || key === 'w') moveCrosshair(0, -step);
   715	    if (key === 'arrowdown' || key === 's') moveCrosshair(0, step);
   716	    if (key === ' ' || key === 'enter') keyFire = true;
   717	  }, { passive: false });
   718	
   719	  function frame(time) {
   720	    const dt = Math.min(0.033, (time - last) / 1000 || 0);
   721	    last = time;
   722	    update(dt);
   723	    draw();
   724	    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   725	  }
   726	
   727	  window.addEventListener('resize', resize, { passive: true });
   728	  resize();
   729	  resetGame();
   730	  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   731	})();

Tap anywhere in the sky to launch an interceptor from the nearest battery.
Keyboard: arrows/WASD move the crosshair; Space or Enter fires.
Destroy incoming warheads before they reach the six towns.
MIRVs split, smart bombs dodge blasts, and fast movers need quick reactions.
Every fifth cleared wave restores one town and adds interceptor ammo.

    47	  function readBest() {
    48	    try { return Number(localStorage.getItem(BEST_KEY)) || 0; } catch (_) { return 0; }
    49	  }
    50	
    51	  function saveBest(score) {
    52	    if (score <= bestScore) return;
    53	    bestScore = score;
    54	    try { localStorage.setItem(BEST_KEY, String(bestScore)); } catch (_) {}
    55	  }
    56	
    57	  function rng(seed) {
    58	    let value = seed >>> 0;
    59	    return () => {
    60	      value += 0x6D2B79F5;
    61	      let t = value;
    62	      t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    63	      t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    64	      return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    65	    };
    66	  }
    67	
    68	  function seedFor(index) {
    69	    return (0x51A7E + Math.imul(index + 1, 0x9E3779B9)) >>> 0;
    70	  }
    71	
    72	  function seedLabel(seed) {
    73	    return seed.toString(36).toUpperCase().slice(-4).padStart(4, '0');
    74	  }
    75	
    76	  function makeStage(index) {
    77	    const random = rng(seedFor(index));
    78	    const length = 1510 + Math.min(index, 5) * 45;
    79	    const points = [];
    80	    let center = W / 2;
    81	    for (let y = 0; y <= length + 160; y += 80) {
    82	      const wave = Math.sin(y * 0.009 + index * 1.8) * 17;
    83	      center += (random() - 0.5) * 48 + wave * 0.08;
    84	      center = Math.max(118, Math.min(W - 118, center));
    85	      const narrowing = (y > length * 0.46 && y < length * 0.69) ? 35 : 0;
    86	      const half = Math.max(72, 132 - random() * 21 - narrowing);
    87	      points.push({ y, left: Math.max(14, center - half), right: Math.min(W - 14, center + half) });
    88	    }
    89	
    90	    const cavern = {
    91	      seed: seedFor(index),
    92	      label: seedLabel(seedFor(index)),
    93	      length,
    94	      floorY: length,
    95	      points,
    96	      crystals: [],
    97	      vents: [],
    98	      pads: [],
    99	      door: null,
   100	      stars: []
   101	    };
   102	
   103	    for (let i = 0; i < 34; i++) {
   104	      cavern.stars.push({
   105	        x: random() * W,
   106	        y: random() * (length + 300),
   107	        size: 0.5 + random() * 1.5,
   108	        alpha: 0.18 + random() * 0.48
   109	      });
   110	    }
   111	
   112	    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
   113	      const y = 235 + i * 205 + random() * 70;
   114	      const side = i % 2 === 0 ? 'left' : 'right';
   115	      const size = 19 + random() * 18;
   116	      const edge = corridorAtFrom(points, y, 'left') + size * 0.46;
   117	      const rightEdge = corridorAtFrom(points, y, 'right') - size * 0.46;
   118	      cavern.crystals.push({ y, side, size, x: side === 'left' ? edge : rightEdge, phase: random() * TAU });
   119	    }
   120	
   121	    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
   122	      const y = 300 + i * 285 + random() * 90;
   123	      const side = random() > 0.5 ? 'left' : 'right';
   124	      cavern.vents.push({ y, side, strength: (side === 'left' ? 1 : -1) * (16 + random() * 15), phase: random() * TAU });
   125	    }
   126	
   127	    const padYs = [360, 780, 1130].map((value, i) => Math.min(length - 150, value + (random() - 0.5) * 80 - i * index * 2));
   128	    padYs.forEach((y, i) => {
   129	      const bounds = corridorAtFrom(points, y);
   130	      const centerX = (bounds.left + bounds.right) / 2 + (random() - 0.5) * Math.min(55, bounds.right - bounds.left - 100);
   131	      cavern.pads.push({ x: centerX, y, width: 76, landed: false, tone: i % 2 });
   132	    });
   133	
   134	    cavern.door = { y: 935 + (random() - 0.5) * 80, gap: 104, phase: random() * TAU, speed: 0.7 + random() * 0.25 };
   135	    return cavern;
   136	  }
   137	
   138	  function corridorAtFrom(points, y, side) {
   139	    if (y <= points[0].y) return side === 'left' ? points[0].left : side === 'right' ? points[0].right : points[0];
   140	    for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i++) {
   141	      if (y <= points[i].y) {
   142	        const a = points[i - 1];
   143	        const b = points[i];
   144	        const t = (y - a.y) / (b.y - a.y);
   145	        if (side === 'left') return a.left + (b.left - a.left) * t;
   146	        if (side === 'right') return a.right + (b.right - a.right) * t;
   147	        return {
   148	          left: a.left + (b.left - a.left) * t,
   149	          right: a.right + (b.right - a.right) * t
   150	        };
   151	      }
   152	    }
   153	    const last = points[points.length - 1];
   154	    return side === 'left' ? last.left : side === 'right' ? last.right : { left: last.left, right: last.right };
   155	  }
   156	
   157	  function corridorAt(y) { return corridorAtFrom(stage.points, y); }
   158	
   159	  function resetRun() {
   160	    stageNumber = 0;
   161	    runScore = 0;
   162	    runPads = 0;
   163	    state = 'playing';
   164	    clearClock = 0;
   165	    messageClock = 0;
   166	    hintClock = 9;
   167	    particles = [];
   168	    floaters = [];
   169	    loadStage(0);
   170	    hud.screen.classList.remove('visible');
   171	  }
   172	
   173	  function loadStage(index) {
   174	    stageNumber = index;
   175	    stage = makeStage(index);
   176	    const bounds = corridorAt(90);
   177	    player = {
   178	      x: (bounds.left + bounds.right) / 2,
   179	      y: 90,
   180	      vx: 0,
   181	      vy: 8,
   182	      angle: 0,
   183	      fuel: 100,
   184	      maxFuel: 100,
   185	      thrusting: false,
   186	      flash: 0
   187	    };
   188	    cameraY = 0;
   189	    state = 'playing';
   190	    hintClock = 9;
   191	    hud.screen.classList.remove('visible');
   192	  }
   193	
   194	  function getScore() {
   195	    const depth = Math.floor((stageNumber * stage.length + player.y) / 3);
   196	    return Math.max(0, runScore + depth);
   197	  }
   198	
   199	  function setStateOverlay(kicker, title, copy, buttonText) {
   200	    hud.kicker.textContent = kicker;
   201	    hud.title.textContent = title;
   202	    hud.copy.innerHTML = copy;
   203	    document.getElementById('restart').textContent = buttonText;
   204	    hud.screen.classList.add('visible');
   205	  }
   206	
   207	  function crash(reason) {
   208	    if (state !== 'playing') return;
   209	    state = 'crashed';
   210	    shake = 11;
   211	    player.flash = 1;
   212	    burst(player.x, player.y, '#ff6d68', 30, 1.8);
   213	    const finalScore = getScore();
   214	    saveBest(finalScore);
   215	    setStateOverlay('RUN OVER', 'CRASHED', `${reason}<br><br>SCORE <b>${pad(finalScore, 5)}</b> · BEST <b>${pad(bestScore, 5)}</b>`, 'RESTART RUN');
   216	  }
   217	
   218	  function clearStage() {
   219	    if (state !== 'playing') return;
   220	    state = 'cleared';
   221	    const used = player.maxFuel - player.fuel;
   222	    const par = 70;
   223	    const fuelBonus = Math.max(0, Math.floor((par - used) * 3));
   224	    const clearBonus = 250 + fuelBonus;
   225	    runScore += clearBonus;
   226	    burst(player.x, player.y, '#7fffc1', 36, 1.4);
   227	    floaters.push({ x: player.x, y: player.y - 26, text: `CAVERN CLEAR  +${clearBonus}`, life: 2.3, max: 2.3, color: '#86f9c2' });
   228	    saveBest(getScore());
   229	    setStateOverlay('DEPTH REACHED', 'CAVERN CLEAR', `SEED ${stage.label} · FUEL BONUS +${fuelBonus}<br><br>SCORE <b>${pad(getScore(), 5)}</b>`, 'NEXT CAVERN');
   230	    clearClock = 1.45;
   231	  }
   232	
   233	  function pad(value, count) { return String(Math.max(0, Math.floor(value))).padStart(count, '0'); }
   234	
   235	  function update(dt) {
   236	    elapsed += dt;
   237	    shake = Math.max(0, shake - dt * 21);
   238	    if (hintClock > 0) hintClock -= dt;
   239	    hud.hint.classList.toggle('visible', hintClock > 0 && state === 'playing');
   240	
   241	    if (state === 'cleared') {
   242	      clearClock -= dt;
   243	      if (clearClock <= 0) loadStage(stageNumber + 1);
   244	      updateParticles(dt);
   245	      updateFloaters(dt);
   246	      updateHud();
   247	      return;
   248	    }
   249	    if (state !== 'playing') {
   250	      updateParticles(dt);
   251	      updateFloaters(dt);
   252	      updateHud();
   253	      return;
   254	    }
   255	
   256	    const left = input.left;
   257	    const right = input.right;
   258	    const both = left && right;
   259	    const straight = input.main || input.up || both;
   260	    const side = left === right ? 0 : left ? -1 : 1;
   261	    const wasThrusting = player.thrusting;
   262	    player.thrusting = player.fuel > 0 && (straight || side !== 0);
   263	
   264	    if (side !== 0) player.angle += side * 2.45 * dt;
   265	    player.angle *= Math.pow(0.82, dt);
   266	    player.angle = Math.max(-0.82, Math.min(0.82, player.angle));
   267	
   268	    if (player.thrusting) {
   269	      const power = straight ? 75 : 52;
   270	      player.vx += Math.sin(player.angle) * power * dt;
   271	      player.vy -= Math.cos(player.angle) * power * dt;
   272	      player.fuel = Math.max(0, player.fuel - dt * (straight ? 9.5 : 6.5));
   273	      if (!wasThrusting || Math.random() < dt * 13) {
   274	        const flame = straight ? 1.15 : 0.9;
   275	        burst(player.x - Math.sin(player.angle) * 12, player.y + Math.cos(player.angle) * 12, '#ffcf6e', 1, flame, true);
   276	      }
   277	    }
   278	    if (input.down) player.vy += 18 * dt;
   279	
   280	    for (const vent of stage.vents) {
   281	      const distance = Math.abs(player.y - vent.y);
   282	      if (distance < 105) {
   283	        const falloff = 1 - distance / 105;
   284	        player.vx += vent.strength * falloff * dt;
   285	        if (Math.random() < dt * 10 * falloff) {
   286	          const bounds = corridorAt(vent.y);
   287	          const x = vent.side === 'left' ? bounds.left + 8 : bounds.right - 8;
   288	          burst(x, vent.y + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 25, '#7cdbff', 1, 0.5, true);
   289	        }
   290	      }
   291	    }
   292	
   293	    player.vy += GRAVITY * dt;
   294	    player.vx *= Math.pow(0.988, dt * 60);
   295	    player.vy *= Math.pow(0.998, dt * 60);
   296	    player.x += player.vx * dt;
   297	    player.y += player.vy * dt;
   298	
   299	    const bounds = corridorAt(player.y);
   300	    if (player.x < bounds.left + 11 || player.x > bounds.right - 11) {
   301	      crash('WALL CONTACT');
   302	      return;
   303	    }
   304	
   305	    for (const crystal of stage.crystals) {
   306	      if (Math.abs(player.y - crystal.y) < crystal.size * 1.12 && Math.abs(player.x - crystal.x) < crystal.size * 0.95) {
   307	        crash('CRYSTAL IMPACT');
   308	        return;
   309	      }
   310	    }
   311	
   312	    const door = stage.door;
   313	    const doorCenter = (corridorAt(door.y).left + corridorAt(door.y).right) / 2 + Math.sin(elapsed * door.speed + door.phase) * 38;
   314	    if (Math.abs(player.y - door.y) < 12 && (player.x < doorCenter - door.gap / 2 + 10 || player.x > doorCenter + door.gap / 2 - 10)) {
   315	      crash('DOOR CONTACT');
   316	      return;
   317	    }
   318	
   319	    for (const padItem of stage.pads) {
   320	      if (!padItem.landed && player.vy > 0 && player.y >= padItem.y - 17 && player.y <= padItem.y + 14 && Math.abs(player.x - padItem.x) < padItem.width / 2 + 11) {
   321	        const gentle = player.vy < 115 && Math.abs(player.vx) < 72 && Math.abs(player.angle) < 0.45;
   322	        if (!gentle) {
   323	          crash('PAD HIT TOO FAST');
   324	          return;
   325	        }
   326	        padItem.landed = true;
   327	        player.y = padItem.y - 17;
   328	        player.vy = -24;
   329	        player.vx *= 0.25;
   330	        player.angle *= 0.25;
   331	        player.fuel = player.maxFuel;
   332	        runPads += 1;
   333	        runScore += 100;
   334	        burst(padItem.x, padItem.y, '#7fffc1', 18, 1.0);
   335	        floaters.push({ x: padItem.x, y: padItem.y - 20, text: 'PAD +100 · FUEL TOPPED', life: 1.7, max: 1.7, color: '#82ffc6' });
   336	      }
   337	    }
   338	
   339	    if (player.y > stage.floorY - 67) {
   340	      const floorBounds = corridorAt(stage.floorY - 40);
   341	      const beaconX = (floorBounds.left + floorBounds.right) / 2;
   342	      if (Math.abs(player.x - beaconX) < 108 && Math.hypot(player.vx, player.vy) < 160) clearStage();
   343	      else if (Math.hypot(player.vx, player.vy) >= 160 || player.y > stage.floorY + 6) crash('BEACON IMPACT');
   344	    }
   345	
   346	    cameraY += ((Math.max(0, Math.min(stage.floorY - H + 130, player.y - 250))) - cameraY) * Math.min(1, dt * 5);
   347	    updateParticles(dt);
   348	    updateFloaters(dt);
   349	    updateHud();
   350	  }
   351	
   352	  function updateHud() {
   353	    if (!player) return;
   354	    hud.stage.textContent = `CAVERN ${pad(stageNumber + 1, 2)} · ${stage.label}`;
   355	    hud.score.textContent = `SCORE ${pad(getScore(), 5)}`;
   356	    hud.depth.textContent = `DEPTH ${pad((stageNumber * stage.length + player.y) / 10, 3)}`;
   357	    hud.fuel.style.transform = `scaleX(${Math.max(0, player.fuel / player.maxFuel)})`;
   358	    hud.fuel.style.background = player.fuel < 25 ? '#ff7970' : player.fuel < 50 ? '#ffd16e' : '#70f4b0';
   359	  }
   360	
   361	  function burst(x, y, color, amount, force, engine = false) {
   362	    for (let i = 0; i < amount; i++) {
   363	      const angle = Math.random() * TAU;
   364	      const speed = (0.3 + Math.random() * 0.7) * force * (engine ? 24 : 48);
   365	      particles.push({ x, y, vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed, vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed, life: engine ? 0.35 : 0.75 + Math.random() * 0.6, max: engine ? 0.35 : 1.35, size: engine ? 2 + Math.random() * 2 : 2 + Math.random() * 4, color });
   366	    }
   367	  }
   368	
   369	  function updateParticles(dt) {
   370	    for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   371	      const item = particles[i];
   372	      item.life -= dt;
   373	      item.x += item.vx * dt;
   374	      item.y += item.vy * dt;
   375	      item.vy += 17 * dt;
   376	      if (item.life <= 0) particles.splice(i, 1);
   377	    }
   378	  }
   379	
   380	  function updateFloaters(dt) {
   381	    for (let i = floaters.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   382	      floaters[i].life -= dt;
   383	      floaters[i].y -= dt * 19;
   384	      if (floaters[i].life <= 0) floaters.splice(i, 1);
   385	    }
   386	  }
   387	
   388	  function resize() {
   389	    const rect = frame.getBoundingClientRect();
   390	    cssWidth = Math.max(1, rect.width);
   391	    cssHeight = Math.max(1, rect.height);
   392	    const dpr = Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
   393	    canvas.width = Math.min(960, Math.max(1, Math.floor(cssWidth * dpr)));
   394	    canvas.height = Math.min(960, Math.max(1, Math.floor(cssHeight * dpr)));
   395	    ctx.setTransform(canvas.width / W, 0, 0, canvas.height / H, 0, 0);
   396	  }
   397	
   398	  function draw() {
   399	    ctx.save();
   400	    ctx.setTransform(canvas.width / W, 0, 0, canvas.height / H, 0, 0);
   401	    ctx.fillStyle = '#07111e';
   402	    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   403	    const sx = shake > 0 ? (Math.random() - 0.5) * shake : 0;
   404	    const sy = shake > 0 ? (Math.random() - 0.5) * shake : 0;
   405	    ctx.translate(sx, sy);
   406	    drawCavern();
   407	    drawParticles();
   408	    if (player) drawShip();
   409	    drawFloaters();
   410	    ctx.restore();
   411	  }
   412	
   413	  function drawCavern() {
   414	    const bg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H);
   415	    bg.addColorStop(0, '#091a2a');
   416	    bg.addColorStop(0.5, '#07121e');
   417	    bg.addColorStop(1, '#101b26');
   418	    ctx.fillStyle = bg;
   419	    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   420	
   421	    for (const star of stage.stars) {
   422	      const y = star.y - cameraY;
   423	      if (y < -4 || y > H + 4) continue;
   424	      ctx.globalAlpha = star.alpha;
   425	      ctx.fillStyle = '#c7efff';
   426	      ctx.fillRect(star.x, y, star.size, star.size);
   427	    }
   428	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   429	
   430	    const visible = stage.points.filter((point) => point.y >= cameraY - 100 && point.y <= cameraY + H + 100);
   431	    ctx.fillStyle = '#0b1622';
   432	    ctx.beginPath();
   433	    ctx.moveTo(0, 0);
   434	    visible.forEach((point) => ctx.lineTo(point.left, point.y - cameraY));
   435	    ctx.lineTo(0, H);
   436	    ctx.closePath();
   437	    ctx.fill();
   438	    ctx.beginPath();
   439	    ctx.moveTo(W, 0);
   440	    visible.forEach((point) => ctx.lineTo(point.right, point.y - cameraY));
   441	    ctx.lineTo(W, H);
   442	    ctx.closePath();
   443	    ctx.fill();
   444	
   445	    drawWall(visible, 'left');
   446	    drawWall(visible, 'right');
   447	    drawPads();
   448	    drawCrystals();
   449	    drawVents();
   450	    drawDoor();
   451	    drawBeacon();
   452	  }
   453	
   454	  function drawWall(points, side) {
   455	    ctx.save();
   456	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#294454';
   457	    ctx.lineWidth = 8;
   458	    ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
   459	    ctx.beginPath();
   460	    points.forEach((point, index) => {
   461	      const x = side === 'left' ? point.left : point.right;
   462	      if (index === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, point.y - cameraY);
   463	      else ctx.lineTo(x, point.y - cameraY);
   464	    });
   465	    ctx.stroke();
   466	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#76bfbd';
   467	    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
   468	    ctx.globalAlpha = .8;
   469	    ctx.stroke();
   470	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   471	    for (let i = 0; i < points.length - 1; i++) {
   472	      const a = points[i];
   473	      const b = points[i + 1];
   474	      const x1 = side === 'left' ? a.left : a.right;
   475	      const x2 = side === 'left' ? b.left : b.right;
   476	      ctx.strokeStyle = i % 2 ? '#152d3b' : '#1c3945';
   477	      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   478	      ctx.beginPath();
   479	      ctx.moveTo(x1, a.y - cameraY);
   480	      ctx.lineTo(x2, b.y - cameraY);
   481	      ctx.stroke();
   482	    }
   483	    ctx.restore();
   484	  }
   485	
   486	  function drawPads() {
   487	    for (const padItem of stage.pads) {
   488	      const y = padItem.y - cameraY;
   489	      if (y < -30 || y > H + 30) continue;
   490	      const glow = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, y - 14, 0, y + 8);
   491	      glow.addColorStop(0, '#8cffe044');
   492	      glow.addColorStop(1, '#8cffe400');
   493	      ctx.fillStyle = glow;
   494	      ctx.fillRect(padItem.x - 55, y - 15, 110, 30);
   495	      ctx.fillStyle = padItem.landed ? '#56d696' : '#98eee1';
   496	      ctx.fillRect(padItem.x - padItem.width / 2, y - 4, padItem.width, 8);
   497	      ctx.fillStyle = '#102831';
   498	      ctx.fillRect(padItem.x - padItem.width / 2 + 7, y - 1, padItem.width - 14, 2);
   499	      ctx.fillStyle = '#b5fff1';
   500	      for (let i = -2; i <= 2; i++) ctx.fillRect(padItem.x + i * 13 - 2, y - 9, 4, 2);
   501	      ctx.fillStyle = '#8cfbdd';
   502	      ctx.font = '8px ui-monospace, monospace';
   503	      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   504	      ctx.fillText(padItem.landed ? 'TOPPED' : 'REFUEL', padItem.x, y + 17);
   505	    }
   506	  }
   507	
   508	  function drawCrystals() {
   509	    for (const crystal of stage.crystals) {
   510	      const y = crystal.y - cameraY;
   511	      if (y < -55 || y > H + 55) continue;
   512	      const size = crystal.size;
   513	      const direction = crystal.side === 'left' ? 1 : -1;
   514	      ctx.save();
   515	      ctx.translate(crystal.x, y);
   516	      ctx.rotate(Math.sin(elapsed * 0.7 + crystal.phase) * 0.04);
   517	      ctx.shadowBlur = 12;
   518	      ctx.shadowColor = '#ff6688aa';
   519	      ctx.fillStyle = '#be5a82';
   520	      ctx.beginPath();
   521	      ctx.moveTo(-direction * size * .62, size * .7);
   522	      ctx.lineTo(direction * size * .95, 0);
   523	      ctx.lineTo(-direction * size * .25, -size * .9);
   524	      ctx.lineTo(-direction * size * .9, -size * .2);
   525	      ctx.closePath();
   526	      ctx.fill();
   527	      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   528	      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff9aaf';
   529	      ctx.globalAlpha = .8;
   530	      ctx.beginPath();
   531	      ctx.moveTo(-direction * size * .2, size * .44);
   532	      ctx.lineTo(direction * size * .63, 0);
   533	      ctx.lineTo(-direction * size * .22, -size * .63);
   534	      ctx.closePath();
   535	      ctx.fill();
   536	      ctx.restore();
   537	    }
   538	  }
   539	
   540	  function drawVents() {
   541	    for (const vent of stage.vents) {
   542	      const y = vent.y - cameraY;
   543	      if (y < -40 || y > H + 40) continue;
   544	      const bounds = corridorAt(vent.y);
   545	      const x = vent.side === 'left' ? bounds.left : bounds.right;
   546	      const direction = vent.side === 'left' ? 1 : -1;
   547	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#70dfff';
   548	      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   549	      ctx.beginPath();
   550	      ctx.moveTo(x, y - 14);
   551	      ctx.lineTo(x + direction * 13, y);
   552	      ctx.lineTo(x, y + 14);
   553	      ctx.stroke();
   554	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#70dfff66';
   555	      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
   556	      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
   557	        const offset = (i - 1) * 11;
   558	        ctx.beginPath();
   559	        ctx.moveTo(x + direction * 7, y + offset);
   560	        ctx.lineTo(x + direction * 25, y + offset - direction * 5);
   561	        ctx.stroke();
   562	      }
   563	      ctx.fillStyle = '#8ceaff';
   564	      ctx.font = '8px ui-monospace, monospace';
   565	      ctx.textAlign = vent.side === 'left' ? 'left' : 'right';
   566	      ctx.fillText('WIND', x + direction * 10, y - 19);
   567	    }
   568	  }
   569	
   570	  function drawDoor() {
   571	    const door = stage.door;
   572	    const y = door.y - cameraY;
   573	    if (y < -30 || y > H + 30) return;
   574	    const bounds = corridorAt(door.y);
   575	    const center = (bounds.left + bounds.right) / 2 + Math.sin(elapsed * door.speed + door.phase) * 38;
   576	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#e2b66b';
   577	    ctx.lineWidth = 6;
   578	    ctx.beginPath();
   579	    ctx.moveTo(bounds.left + 3, y);
   580	    ctx.lineTo(center - door.gap / 2, y);
   581	    ctx.moveTo(center + door.gap / 2, y);
   582	    ctx.lineTo(bounds.right - 3, y);
   583	    ctx.stroke();
   584	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff0ad';
   585	    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
   586	    ctx.beginPath();
   587	    ctx.moveTo(bounds.left, y - 7);
   588	    ctx.lineTo(center - door.gap / 2, y - 7);
   589	    ctx.moveTo(center + door.gap / 2, y - 7);
   590	    ctx.lineTo(bounds.right, y - 7);
   591	    ctx.stroke();
   592	    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd988';
   593	    ctx.font = '8px ui-monospace, monospace';
   594	    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   595	    ctx.fillText('MOVING DOOR', center, y - 15);
   596	  }
   597	
   598	  function drawBeacon() {
   599	    const y = stage.floorY - cameraY - 40;
   600	    if (y < -80 || y > H + 80) return;
   601	    const bounds = corridorAt(stage.floorY - 40);
   602	    const x = (bounds.left + bounds.right) / 2;
   603	    const pulse = 1 + Math.sin(elapsed * 4) * .08;
   604	    ctx.save();
   605	    ctx.translate(x, y);
   606	    ctx.scale(pulse, pulse);
   607	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#72f4bd55';
   608	    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   609	    ctx.beginPath();
   610	    ctx.arc(0, 0, 31, 0, TAU);
   611	    ctx.stroke();
   612	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#a7ffe0';
   613	    ctx.beginPath();
   614	    ctx.moveTo(-21, 0); ctx.lineTo(21, 0);
   615	    ctx.moveTo(0, -21); ctx.lineTo(0, 21);
   616	    ctx.stroke();
   617	    ctx.fillStyle = '#8fffc9';
   618	    ctx.shadowBlur = 18;
   619	    ctx.shadowColor = '#7cffc1';
   620	    ctx.beginPath();
   621	    ctx.arc(0, 0, 8, 0, TAU);
   622	    ctx.fill();
   623	    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   624	    ctx.fillStyle = '#aaffdc';
   625	    ctx.font = '9px ui-monospace, monospace';
   626	    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   627	    ctx.fillText('BEACON', 0, 45);
   628	    ctx.restore();
   629	  }
   630	
   631	  function drawParticles() {
   632	    for (const item of particles) {
   633	      const y = item.y - cameraY;
   634	      if (y < -10 || y > H + 10) continue;
   635	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, item.life / item.max);
   636	      ctx.fillStyle = item.color;
   637	      ctx.beginPath();
   638	      ctx.arc(item.x, y, item.size * (0.5 + item.life / item.max), 0, TAU);
   639	      ctx.fill();
   640	    }
   641	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   642	  }
   643	
   644	  function drawShip() {
   645	    const y = player.y - cameraY;
   646	    ctx.save();
   647	    ctx.translate(player.x, y);
   648	    ctx.rotate(player.angle);
   649	    if (player.thrusting && player.fuel > 0 && state === 'playing') {
   650	      const flame = 12 + Math.sin(elapsed * 40) * 4;
   651	      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffb65e';
   652	      ctx.shadowBlur = 14;
   653	      ctx.shadowColor = '#ffad55';
   654	      ctx.beginPath();
   655	      ctx.moveTo(-5, 12);
   656	      ctx.lineTo(0, 12 + flame);
   657	      ctx.lineTo(5, 12);
   658	      ctx.closePath();
   659	      ctx.fill();
   660	      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   661	    }
   662	    ctx.shadowBlur = 13;
   663	    ctx.shadowColor = player.flash > 0 ? '#ff6868' : '#7fe9ff';
   664	    ctx.fillStyle = player.flash > 0 ? '#ff7f7f' : '#c7f7ff';
   665	    ctx.beginPath();
   666	    ctx.moveTo(0, -19);
   667	    ctx.lineTo(10, 10);
   668	    ctx.lineTo(0, 15);
   669	    ctx.lineTo(-10, 10);
   670	    ctx.closePath();
   671	    ctx.fill();
   672	    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
   673	    ctx.fillStyle = '#246a82';
   674	    ctx.beginPath();
   675	    ctx.moveTo(0, -12);
   676	    ctx.lineTo(5, 1);
   677	    ctx.lineTo(-5, 1);
   678	    ctx.closePath();
   679	    ctx.fill();
   680	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#eaffff';
   681	    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
   682	    ctx.beginPath();
   683	    ctx.moveTo(-12, 8); ctx.lineTo(-6, 6);
   684	    ctx.moveTo(12, 8); ctx.lineTo(6, 6);
   685	    ctx.stroke();
   686	    ctx.restore();
   687	    player.flash = Math.max(0, player.flash - 0.05);
   688	  }
   689	
   690	  function drawFloaters() {
   691	    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   692	    ctx.font = '800 10px ui-monospace, monospace';
   693	    for (const item of floaters) {
   694	      const y = item.y - cameraY;
   695	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.min(1, item.life / (item.max * .35));
   696	      ctx.fillStyle = item.color;
   697	      ctx.fillText(item.text, item.x, y);
   698	    }
   699	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   700	  }
   701	
   702	  function setAction(action, active) {
   703	    input[action] = active;
   704	    const element = document.getElementById(`${action}-control`);
   705	    if (element) element.classList.toggle('active', active);
   706	  }
   707	
   708	  function syncPointerActions() {
   709	    const active = new Set(pointers.values());
   710	    setAction('left', active.has('left'));
   711	    setAction('main', active.has('main'));
   712	    setAction('right', active.has('right'));
   713	  }
   714	
   715	  function controlAction(event) {
   716	    const button = event.currentTarget;
   717	    const action = button.id.replace('-control', '');
   718	    event.preventDefault();
   719	    if (state !== 'playing') {
   720	      resetRun();
   721	      return;
   722	    }
   723	    if (event.type === 'pointerdown') {
   724	      pointers.set(event.pointerId, action);
   725	      try { button.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId); } catch (_) {}
   726	      syncPointerActions();
   727	    } else if (event.type === 'pointerup' || event.type === 'pointercancel' || event.type === 'lostpointercapture') {
   728	      pointers.delete(event.pointerId);
   729	      syncPointerActions();
   730	    }
   731	  }
   732	
   733	  for (const button of document.querySelectorAll('.control')) {
   734	    button.addEventListener('pointerdown', controlAction, { passive: false });
   735	    button.addEventListener('pointerup', controlAction, { passive: false });
   736	    button.addEventListener('pointercancel', controlAction, { passive: false });
   737	    button.addEventListener('lostpointercapture', controlAction, { passive: false });
   738	  }
   739	
   740	  document.getElementById('restart').addEventListener('click', (event) => {
   741	    event.preventDefault();
   742	    if (state === 'cleared') loadStage(stageNumber + 1);
   743	    else resetRun();
   744	  });
   745	
   746	  window.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
   747	    const key = event.key.toLowerCase();
   748	    if (['arrowleft', 'arrowright', 'arrowup', 'arrowdown', ' ', 'enter', 'a', 'd', 'w', 's'].includes(key)) event.preventDefault();
   749	    if (state !== 'playing' && [' ', 'enter', 'r'].includes(key)) {
   750	      if (state === 'cleared') loadStage(stageNumber + 1);
   751	      else resetRun();
   752	      return;
   753	    }
   754	    keys.add(key);
   755	    setAction('left', keys.has('arrowleft') || keys.has('a'));
   756	    setAction('right', keys.has('arrowright') || keys.has('d'));
   757	    input.main = keys.has(' ') || keys.has('enter');
   758	    input.up = keys.has('arrowup') || keys.has('w');
   759	    input.down = keys.has('arrowdown') || keys.has('s');
   760	    document.getElementById('main-control').classList.toggle('active', input.main || input.up);
   761	  }, { passive: false });
   762	
   763	  window.addEventListener('keyup', (event) => {
   764	    const key = event.key.toLowerCase();
   765	    keys.delete(key);
   766	    setAction('left', keys.has('arrowleft') || keys.has('a'));
   767	    setAction('right', keys.has('arrowright') || keys.has('d'));
   768	    input.main = keys.has(' ') || keys.has('enter');
   769	    input.up = keys.has('arrowup') || keys.has('w');
   770	    input.down = keys.has('arrowdown') || keys.has('s');
   771	    document.getElementById('main-control').classList.toggle('active', input.main || input.up);
   772	  }, { passive: false });
   773	
   774	  frame.addEventListener('touchmove', (event) => event.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   775	  frame.addEventListener('wheel', (event) => event.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   776	  window.addEventListener('resize', resize, { passive: true });
   777	
   778	  function tick(now) {
   779	    const dt = Math.min(0.035, Math.max(0.001, (now - lastTime) / 1000));
   780	    lastTime = now;
   781	    update(dt);
   782	    draw();
   783	    requestAnimationFrame(tick);
   784	  }
   785	
   786	  resize();
   787	  resetRun();
   788	  requestAnimationFrame(tick);
   789	})();

Controls: hold LEFT or RIGHT to rotate and thrust; hold both for a straight main burn.
MAIN or keyboard Space/Enter burns straight; arrows and WASD also work.
Land gently on green REFUEL pads to top up fuel and earn 100 points.
Reach the beacon at the cavern floor; hard contact with walls, crystals, or doors crashes.
Best score is saved locally in the browser.

   776	  function pointFromEvent(event) {

   786	  function handleDown(event) {
   787	    event.preventDefault();
   788	    ensureAudio();
   789	    const point = pointFromEvent(event);
   790	    if (buttonHit(point)) { placeBomb(); return; }
   791	    input.active = true;
   792	    input.held = true;
   793	    input.dir = null;
   794	    input.x = point.x;
   795	    input.y = point.y;
   796	  }

   798	  function handleMove(event) {
   799	    if (!input.active) return;
   800	    event.preventDefault();
   801	    const point = pointFromEvent(event);
   802	    const dx = point.x - input.x;
   803	    const dy = point.y - input.y;
   804	    if (Math.abs(dx) + Math.abs(dy) < 12) return;
   805	    input.dir = Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy) ? { dx: dx > 0 ? 1 : -1, dy: 0 } : { dx: 0, dy: dy > 0 ? 1 : -1 };
   806	    input.lastRepeat = performance.now();
   807	    input.x = point.x;
   808	    input.y = point.y;
   809	    requestMove(input.dir.dx, input.dir.dy);
   810	  }
   811	
   812	  function handleUp(event) {
   813	    if (event) event.preventDefault();
   814	    input.active = false;
   815	    input.held = false;
   816	    input.dir = null;
   817	  }

   477	  function pointerPos(e) { const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect(); return { x: e.clientX - r.left, y: e.clientY - r.top }; }
   478	  function hitCircle(p, c) { return dist(p.x, p.y, c.x, c.y) <= c.r + 10; }
   479	  function hitRect(p, r) { return p.x >= r.x - 8 && p.x <= r.x + r.w + 8 && p.y >= r.y - 8 && p.y <= r.y + r.h + 8; }
   480	  function handlePointerDown(e) {
   481	    e.preventDefault(); const p = pointerPos(e); canvas.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);
   482	    if (gameState === 'dead') { startRun(); return; }
   483	    if (hitRect(p, touch.swap)) { cycleWeapon(); return; }
   484	    if (hitCircle(p, touch.fire)) { pointer.fireId = e.pointerId; pointer.fire = true; return; }
   485	    if (hitCircle(p, touch.stick)) { pointer.moveId = e.pointerId; updateStick(p); }
   486	  }
   487	  function updateStick(p) {
   488	    let dx = p.x - touch.stick.x, dy = p.y - touch.stick.y, l = Math.hypot(dx, dy); if (l > 42) { dx *= 42 / l; dy *= 42 / l; }
   489	    pointer.moveX = dx / 42; pointer.moveY = dy / 42;
   490	  }
   491	  function handlePointerMove(e) { if (pointer.moveId === e.pointerId) { e.preventDefault(); updateStick(pointerPos(e)); } }
   492	  function handlePointerUp(e) { e.preventDefault(); if (pointer.moveId === e.pointerId) { pointer.moveId = null; pointer.moveX = 0; pointer.moveY = 0; } if (pointer.fireId === e.pointerId) { pointer.fireId = null; pointer.fire = false; } }

   467	  function onPointerDown(e) {
   468	    e.preventDefault();
   469	    canvas.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);
   470	    pointers.set(e.pointerId, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });
   471	    if (pointers.size >= 2) { aim = null; pan = { last: midpoint() }; return; }
   472	    if (phase !== 'play' || ball.moving) return;
   473	    const p = getWorld(e.clientX, e.clientY);
   474	    if (distance(p, ball) <= 72) { aim = { id: e.pointerId, point: p }; updateAim(p); }
   475	  }
   476	  function onPointerMove(e) {
   477	    e.preventDefault();
   478	    if (!pointers.has(e.pointerId)) return;
   479	    pointers.set(e.pointerId, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });
   480	    if (pointers.size >= 2) {
   481	      if (!pan) pan = { last: midpoint() };
   482	      const m = midpoint();
   483	      camera.x += (m.x - pan.last.x) / view.scale;
   484	      camera.y += (m.y - pan.last.y) / view.scale;
   485	      camera.x = clamp(camera.x, -90, 90); camera.y = clamp(camera.y, -100, 100);

   702	  function setAction(action, active) {
   703	    input[action] = active;
   704	    const element = document.getElementById(`${action}-control`);
   705	    if (element) element.classList.toggle('active', active);
   706	  }
   707	
   708	  function syncPointerActions() {
   709	    const active = new Set(pointers.values());
   710	    setAction('left', active.has('left'));
   711	    setAction('main', active.has('main'));
   712	    setAction('right', active.has('right'));
   713	  }
   714	
   715	  function controlAction(event) {
   716	    const button = event.currentTarget;
   717	    const action = button.id.replace('-control', '');
   718	    event.preventDefault();
   719	    if (state !== 'playing') {
   720	      resetRun();
   721	      return;
   722	    }
   723	    if (event.type === 'pointerdown') {
   724	      pointers.set(event.pointerId, action);
   725	      try { button.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId); } catch (_) {}
   726	      syncPointerActions();
   727	    } else if (event.type === 'pointerup' || event.type === 'pointercancel' || event.type === 'lostpointercapture') {
   728	      pointers.delete(event.pointerId);
   729	      syncPointerActions();
   730	    }
   731	  }
   732	
   733	  for (const button of document.querySelectorAll('.control')) {
   734	    button.addEventListener('pointerdown', controlAction, { passive: false });
   735	    button.addEventListener('pointerup', controlAction, { passive: false });
   736	    button.addEventListener('pointercancel', controlAction, { passive: false });
   737	    button.addEventListener('lostpointercapture', controlAction, { passive: false });
   738	  }
